```diff
+
+    const CLAUDE_AUTHORIZED_MESSAGE: &str =
+        "Responde solo OK, sin usar herramientas ni modificar archivos";
+
+    fn own_assistant_ok(bytes: &[u8]) -> bool {
+        let mut plain = Vec::new();
+        let mut index = 0;
+        while index < bytes.len() {
+            if bytes[index..].starts_with(b"\x1b[") {
+                index += 2;
+                while index < bytes.len() && !(0x40..=0x7e).contains(&bytes[index]) {
+                    index += 1;
+                }
+                if index < bytes.len() && matches!(bytes[index], b'H' | b'f' | b'E' | b'F') {
+                    plain.push(b'\n');
+                }
+            } else if bytes[index..].starts_with(b"\x1b]") {
+                index += 2;
+                while index < bytes.len()
+                    && bytes[index] != 7
+                    && !bytes[index..].starts_with(b"\x1b\\")
+                {
+                    index += 1;
+                }
+                if bytes.get(index) == Some(&27) {
+                    index += 1;
+                }
+            } else {
+                plain.push(if bytes[index] == b'\r' {
+                    b'\n'
+                } else {
+                    bytes[index]
+                });
+            }
+            index += 1;
+        }
+        let mut marker = false;
+        for line in String::from_utf8_lossy(&plain).lines() {
+            let line = line.trim();
+            if let Some(body) = line.strip_prefix(['⏺', '●', '•']) {
+                if body.trim() == "OK" {
+                    return true;
+                }
+                marker = body.trim().is_empty();
+            } else if marker && line == "OK" {
+                return true;
+            } else if !line.is_empty() {
+                marker = false;
+            }
+        }
+        false
+    }
+
+    #[test]
+    fn single_claude_reply_classifier_requires_an_assistant_marker_not_the_prompt_echo() {
+        assert!(!own_assistant_ok(
+            format!("❯ {CLAUDE_AUTHORIZED_MESSAGE}\r\n").as_bytes()
+        ));
+        assert!(own_assistant_ok("\u{1b}[1m⏺\u{1b}[0m OK\r\n".as_bytes()));
+        assert!(own_assistant_ok("●\u{1b}[3;1HOK\r\n".as_bytes()));
+        assert!(!own_assistant_ok("❯ OK\r\n".as_bytes()));
+    }
+
+    #[cfg(windows)]
+    mod codex_probe {
+        macro_rules! windows_unix_omission {
+            ($name:ident, $reason:literal) => {
+                #[test]
+                #[ignore = $reason]
+                fn $name() {}
+            };
+        }
+
+        windows_unix_omission!(
+            installed_codex_hook_stdout_preflight,
+            "unix-only: requires Linux Unix-domain client sockets and process identity"
+        );
+        windows_unix_omission!(
+            installed_codex_hook_trust_preflight,
+            "unix-only: requires Linux Unix-domain client sockets and process identity"
+        );
+        windows_unix_omission!(
+            installed_codex_resume_only_status_diagnostic,
+            "unix-only: requires Linux Unix-domain client sockets and process identity"
+        );
+        windows_unix_omission!(
+            installed_codex_one_message_capture_and_exact_resume,
+            "unix-only: requires Linux Unix-domain client sockets and process identity"
+        );
+
+        fn codex_accepts_hook_stdout(bytes: &[u8]) -> bool {
+            if bytes.iter().all(u8::is_ascii_whitespace) {
+                return true;
+            }
+            let Ok(serde_json::Value::Object(fields)) = serde_json::from_slice(bytes) else {
+                return false;
+            };
+            fields.iter().all(|(key, value)| match key.as_str() {
+                "continue" | "suppressOutput" => value.is_boolean(),
+                "stopReason" | "systemMessage" => value.is_string() || value.is_null(),
+                "hookSpecificOutput" => value.as_object().is_some_and(|output| {
+                    output
+                        .get("hookEventName")
+                        .is_some_and(|event| event == "SessionStart")
+                        && output.iter().all(|(key, value)| {
+                            key == "hookEventName"
+                                || key == "additionalContext"
+                                    && (value.is_string() || value.is_null())
+                        })
+                }),
+                _ => false,
+            })
+        }
+        fn own_ok(plain: &str) -> bool {
+            plain.lines().any(|line| {
+                line.trim()
+                    .strip_prefix('•')
+                    .is_some_and(|body| body.trim() == "OK")
+            })
+        }
+        #[test]
+        fn codex_probe_filters_reply_schema_and_does_not_count_prompt_echo_as_ok() {
+            assert!(!codex_accepts_hook_stdout(br#"{"ok":true}"#));
+            assert!(codex_accepts_hook_stdout(b""));
+            assert!(codex_accepts_hook_stdout(br#"{"continue":true}"#));
+            assert!(!own_ok(
+                "Responde solo OK, sin usar herramientas ni modificar archivos"
+            ));
+            assert!(!own_ok("› OK"));
+            assert!(own_ok("• OK"));
+        }
+        #[test]
+        fn codex_probe_readiness_requires_the_live_composer_cursor_not_history() {
+            let mut screen = term_core::Screen::default();
+            screen.push("\x1b[1;1H› old prompt\x1b[40;1H\x1b[?25h".as_bytes());
+            assert!(!screen.composer_ready());
+            screen.push("› ".as_bytes());
+            assert!(screen.composer_ready());
+            screen.push(b"\x1b[?25l");
+            assert!(!screen.composer_ready());
+        }
+    }
+}

+    ($name:ident, $reason:literal) => {
+        #[cfg(windows)]
+        #[test]
+        #[ignore = $reason]
+        fn $name() {}
+    };
+}

+                    "-NoProfile",
+                    "-NonInteractive",

+    #[test]
+    fn inherits_auth_and_mcp_environment_without_changing_configuration_or_adding_api_keys() {
+        let client = Client::new();
+        let config = client.dir.join("config.toml");
+        let auth = client.dir.join("auth.json");
+        let config_bytes =
+            b"# existing user settings\n[mcp_servers.ivy]\nenv_vars = ['PANEL_ID', 'PANEL_DIR']\n";
+        let auth_bytes = b"{\"session\":\"inherited subscription sentinel\"}\n";
+        fs::write(&config, config_bytes).unwrap();
+        fs::write(&auth, auth_bytes).unwrap();
+        let args = [
+            "--no-daemon",
+            "-c",
+            "mcp_servers.other.command='existing command'",
+            "resume",
+            "session one",
+        ];
+        let output = client.command(&args, REMOTE_HELP).output().unwrap();
+        assert_eq!(output.status.code(), Some(37), "{}", diagnostic(&output));
+        let record = client.fields("record");
+        client.assert_environment(&record);
+        assert_eq!(&record[10..], args);
+        assert_eq!(fs::read(&config).unwrap(), config_bytes);
+        assert_eq!(fs::read(&auth).unwrap(), auth_bytes);
+        assert_eq!(
+            fs::read(client.dir.join("read-config")).unwrap(),
+            [config_bytes.as_slice(), auth_bytes.as_slice()].concat()
+        );
+        assert!(client.calls().is_empty());
+    }
 }

+    /// Collects a real PTY's output through the asserted text.
+    ///
+    /// On Windows the ConPTY keeps the output pipe's write end while its master lives, so the
+    /// reader cannot observe EOF before `Pty` is dropped. These behavior tests need the live PTY for
+    /// their assertion; wait for the output they actually assert instead of waiting for EOF.
+    fn output_through(pty_output: &Receiver<Vec<u8>>, expected: &str) -> Vec<u8> {
+        #[cfg(windows)]
+        {
+            let deadline = Instant::now() + DEADLINE;
+            let mut output = Vec::new();
+            while !contains(&output, expected) {
+                let remaining = deadline.saturating_duration_since(Instant::now());
+                match pty_output.recv_timeout(remaining) {
+                    Ok(bytes) => output.extend_from_slice(&bytes),
+                    Err(error) => panic!(
+                        "the PTY did not produce {expected:?} before {DEADLINE:?}: {error}; output={:?}",
+                        String::from_utf8_lossy(&output)
+                    ),
+                }
+            }
+            output
+        }
+        #[cfg(not(windows))]
+        {
+            let _ = expected;
+            drain_until_closed(pty_output)
+        }
+    }
+
     fn contains(output: &[u8], text: &str) -> bool {
         String::from_utf8_lossy(output).contains(text)
     }

+#[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
 use std::sync::mpsc::Receiver;
+#[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
 use std::time::{Duration, Instant};
 
+#[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
 use term_core::{Pty, Size};
 
 /// How long a console is left waiting while its cost is measured.
+#[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
 const IDLE: Duration = Duration::from_millis(500);
 
 /// What that wait may cost at most. A spinning thread spends the whole of [`IDLE`], ten times this;
 /// a waiting one spends a small fraction of it.
+#[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
 const AT_MOST: Duration = Duration::from_millis(50);
 
 /// The processor time this whole process has used, every thread included.
+#[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
 fn processor_time() -> Duration {
     let mut now = libc::timespec {
         tv_sec: 0,
@@ -36,6 +40,7 @@ fn processor_time() -> Duration {
     Duration::new(now.tv_sec as u64, now.tv_nsec as u32)
 }
 
+#[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
 fn launch(script: &str, marker: &str) -> (Pty, Receiver<Vec<u8>>) {
     let (pty, output) = Pty::spawn("sh", &["-c", script], Size::default()).expect("launch sh");
     let mut seen = Vec::new();
@@ -49,6 +54,7 @@ fn launch(script: &str, marker: &str) -> (Pty, Receiver<Vec<u8>>) {
     (pty, output)
 }
 
+#[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
 fn spent_over_idle() -> Duration {
     let before = processor_time();
     std::thread::sleep(IDLE);
@@ -56,6 +62,7 @@ fn spent_over_idle() -> Duration {
 }
 
 #[test]
+#[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
 fn a_waiting_console_costs_no_processor_time() {
     // The reader, waiting for a program that says nothing more.
     let (pty, output) = launch(
@@ -92,3 +99,8 @@ fn a_waiting_console_costs_no_processor_time() {
     drop(pty);
     drop(output);
 }
+
+#[cfg(windows)]
+#[test]
+#[ignore = "unix-only: requires Linux process CPU accounting and nonblocking PTY behavior"]
+fn a_waiting_console_costs_no_processor_time() {}
diff --git a/packaging/windows/test-install.ps1 b/packaging/windows/test-install.ps1
index 14a5490..bf01c81 100644
--- a/packaging/windows/test-install.ps1
+++ b/packaging/windows/test-install.ps1
@@ -37,6 +37,23 @@ function Install-Candidate {
     if ($Shell.CreateShortcut($Shortcut).TargetPath -ne (Join-Path $Destination 'Ivy.exe')) { throw 'Invalid Start menu shortcut' }
 }
 Install-Candidate
+$Launch = Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'test-launch.ps1'
+$Installed = Join-Path $Destination 'Ivy.exe'
+$Evidence = Join-Path (Join-Path $env:GITHUB_WORKSPACE 'windows-launch-evidence') ([guid]::NewGuid().ToString('N'))
+New-Item -ItemType Directory $Evidence -Force | Out-Null
+foreach ($Control in 'crash', 'shell', 'layout', 'deadline') {
+    $Rejected = $false
+    try {
+        & $Launch -Executable $Installed -EvidenceDir (Join-Path $Evidence $Control) -Control $Control -TimeoutSeconds 20
+    } catch {
+        $ExpectedStage = @{ crash = 'startup'; shell = 'shell'; layout = 'layout'; deadline = 'deadline' }[$Control]
+        if ($_.Exception.Message -notmatch "^${ExpectedStage}:") { throw }
+        Write-Output "Launch control '$Control' rejected as expected: $($_.Exception.Message)"
+        $Rejected = $true
+    }
+    if (-not $Rejected) { throw "Launch control '$Control' incorrectly passed" }
+}
+& $Launch -Executable $Installed -EvidenceDir (Join-Path $Evidence 'normal')
 # Repeat installation to exercise replacement without claiming an older-version upgrade trial.
 Install-Candidate
 $Process = Start-Process (Join-Path $Destination 'unins000.exe') -ArgumentList @('/VERYSILENT', '/SUPPRESSMSGBOXES', '/NORESTART') -Wait -PassThru
diff --git a/packaging/windows/test-launch-contract.ps1 b/packaging/windows/test-launch-contract.ps1
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..1547a4f
--- /dev/null
+++ b/packaging/windows/test-launch-contract.ps1
@@ -0,0 +1,19 @@
+# These portable checks exercise the launch oracle without substituting for native execution.
+$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
+$errors = $null
+$ast = [System.Management.Automation.Language.Parser]::ParseFile((Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'test-launch.ps1'), [ref]$null, [ref]$errors)
+if ($errors.Count) { $errors | Format-List; exit 1 }
+$type = $ast.FindAll({ param($n) $n -is [System.Management.Automation.Language.StringConstantExpressionAst] -and $n.Value.StartsWith('using System;') }, $true)
+Add-Type -TypeDefinition $type[0].Value
+$function = $ast.FindAll({ param($n) $n -is [System.Management.Automation.Language.FunctionDefinitionAst] -and $n.Name -eq 'Saved-Leaves' }, $true)
+Invoke-Expression $function[0].Extent.Text
+$leaf = '{"leaf":{"panel_width":36,"folded":false}}' | ConvertFrom-Json
+if ((Saved-Leaves $leaf) -ne 1) { throw 'valid leaf rejected' }
+$branch = @{branch=@{children=@(@{node=$leaf}, @{node=$leaf})}}
+if ((Saved-Leaves $branch) -ne 2) { throw 'valid branch rejected' }
+foreach ($invalid in @(@{leaf=@{panel_width=0;folded=$false}}, @{leaf=@{panel_width=36}}, @{branch=@{children=@()}})) {
+    $failed = $false
+    try { Saved-Leaves $invalid } catch { $failed = $true }
+    if (-not $failed) { throw 'invalid layout accepted' }
+}
+'PowerShell syntax, C# native interop compilation, saved leaf and invalid tree fixtures passed.'
diff --git a/packaging/windows/test-launch.ps1 b/packaging/windows/test-launch.ps1
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..94e919a
--- /dev/null
+++ b/packaging/windows/test-launch.ps1
@@ -0,0 +1,230 @@
+param([Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$Executable,
+      [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$EvidenceDir,
+      [ValidateSet('none', 'crash', 'shell', 'layout', 'deadline')][string]$Control = 'none',
+      [ValidateRange(1, 120)][int]$TimeoutSeconds = 30)
+$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
+if ($env:GITHUB_ACTIONS -ne 'true' -or $env:IVY_DISPOSABLE_RUNNER -ne 'yes') {
+    throw 'Launch checks require a disposable GitHub Windows runner.'
+}
+$Executable = (Resolve-Path $Executable).Path
+$EvidenceDir = [IO.Path]::GetFullPath($EvidenceDir)
+if (Test-Path $EvidenceDir) { throw 'Launch evidence directory must be fresh.' }
+New-Item -ItemType Directory $EvidenceDir | Out-Null
+$Profile = Join-Path $EvidenceDir 'roaming'
+New-Item -ItemType Directory $Profile | Out-Null
+$Layout = Join-Path $Profile 'ivy/layout.json'
+$Witness = Join-Path $EvidenceDir 'shell-witness.txt'
+$Token = [guid]::NewGuid().ToString('N')
+if (-not ('IvyLaunchNative' -as [type])) {
+    Add-Type -TypeDefinition @'
+using System;
+using System.Collections.Generic;
+using System.Runtime.InteropServices;
+using System.Text;
+public static class IvyLaunchNative {
+    public delegate bool EnumProc(IntPtr h, IntPtr p);
+    [DllImport("user32.dll")] static extern bool EnumWindows(EnumProc f, IntPtr p);
+    [DllImport("user32.dll")] static extern uint GetWindowThreadProcessId(IntPtr h, out uint p);
+    [DllImport("user32.dll")] static extern bool IsWindowVisible(IntPtr h);
+    [DllImport("user32.dll", CharSet=CharSet.Unicode)] static extern int GetWindowText(IntPtr h, StringBuilder s, int n);
+    [DllImport("user32.dll", CharSet=CharSet.Unicode)] static extern int GetClassName(IntPtr h, StringBuilder s, int n);
+    [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern bool SetForegroundWindow(IntPtr h);
+    [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern IntPtr GetForegroundWindow();
+    [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern bool PostMessage(IntPtr h, uint m, IntPtr w, IntPtr l);
+    [DllImport("user32.dll", SetLastError=true)] static extern uint SendInput(uint n, INPUT[] i, int size);
+    [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential)] struct INPUT { public uint type; public UNION u; }
+    [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Explicit)] struct UNION {
+        [FieldOffset(0)] public KEYBOARD keyboard;
+        [FieldOffset(0)] public MOUSE mouse;
+    }
+    [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential)] struct KEYBOARD {
+        public ushort key, scan; public uint flags, time; public UIntPtr extra;
+    }
+    [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential)] struct MOUSE {
+        public int x, y; public uint data, flags, time; public UIntPtr extra;
+    }
+    public sealed class Window {
+        public IntPtr Handle; public string Title; public string Class;
+    }
+    public static Window[] Windows(int pid) {
+        var result = new List<Window>();
+        EnumWindows((h, unused) => {
+            uint owner; GetWindowThreadProcessId(h, out owner);
+            if (owner == pid && IsWindowVisible(h)) {
+                var title = new StringBuilder(512); var name = new StringBuilder(256);
+                GetWindowText(h, title, title.Capacity); GetClassName(h, name, name.Capacity);
+                result.Add(new Window { Handle=h, Title=title.ToString(), Class=name.ToString() });
+            }
+            return true;
+        }, IntPtr.Zero);
+        return result.ToArray();
+    }
+    static INPUT Key(ushort key, ushort scan, uint flags) {
+        return new INPUT { type=1, u=new UNION { keyboard=new KEYBOARD { key=key, scan=scan, flags=flags } } };
+    }
+    public static void Type(string text) {
+        var events = new List<INPUT>();
+        foreach (char c in text) {
+            events.Add(Key(0, c, 4)); events.Add(Key(0, c, 6));
+        }
+        events.Add(Key(13, 0, 0)); events.Add(Key(13, 0, 2));
+        INPUT[] input = events.ToArray();
+        if (SendInput((uint)input.Length, input, Marshal.SizeOf<INPUT>()) != input.Length)
+            throw new Exception("SendInput failed: " + Marshal.GetLastWin32Error());
+    }
+}
+'@
+}
+function Expected-Shell {
+    foreach ($Name in 'pwsh.exe', 'powershell.exe') {
+        foreach ($Directory in ($env:PATH -split ';')) {
+            if ([IO.Path]::IsPathFullyQualified($Directory)) {
+                $Candidate = Join-Path $Directory $Name
+                if (Test-Path $Candidate -PathType Leaf) { return [IO.Path]::GetFullPath($Candidate) }
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    if ($env:ComSpec -and [IO.Path]::IsPathFullyQualified($env:ComSpec)) { return $env:ComSpec }
+    return Join-Path $env:SystemRoot 'System32/cmd.exe'
+}
+function Descendants([int]$RootPid, [int[]]$Known = @()) {
+    $All = @(Get-CimInstance Win32_Process -OperationTimeoutSec 5)
+    $Ids = [Collections.Generic.HashSet[int]]::new()
+    [void]$Ids.Add($RootPid)
+    foreach ($KnownPid in $Known) { [void]$Ids.Add($KnownPid) }
+    do {
+        $Added = $false
+        foreach ($Child in $All) {
+            if ($Ids.Contains([int]$Child.ParentProcessId) -and $Ids.Add([int]$Child.ProcessId)) { $Added = $true }
+        }
+    } while ($Added)
+    return @($All | Where-Object { $_.ProcessId -ne $RootPid -and $Ids.Contains([int]$_.ProcessId) })
+}
+function Saved-Leaves($Node) {
+    if ($null -ne $Node.leaf) {
+        if ($Node.leaf.panel_width -le 0 -or $null -eq $Node.leaf.folded) { throw 'layout: invalid saved console' }
+        return 1
+    }
+    if ($null -eq $Node.branch -or @($Node.branch.children).Count -lt 2) { throw 'layout: invalid saved tree' }
+    $Count = 0
+    foreach ($Child in $Node.branch.children) { $Count += Saved-Leaves $Child.node }
+    return $Count
+}
+$Start = [Diagnostics.ProcessStartInfo]::new($Executable)
+$Start.UseShellExecute = $false
+$Start.WorkingDirectory = $EvidenceDir
+$Start.RedirectStandardOutput = $true
+$Start.RedirectStandardError = $true
+foreach ($Name in @($Start.Environment.Keys)) {
+    if ($Name -match '^(XDG_|MEASURE_|IVY_)' -or $Name -in @('SHELL', 'PANEL_ID', 'PANEL_DIR')) {
+        [void]$Start.Environment.Remove($Name)
+    }
+}
+$Start.Environment['APPDATA'] = $Profile
+$Expected = Expected-Shell
+$Process = $null
+$Children = @()
+$Stage = 'startup'
+$Clock = [Diagnostics.Stopwatch]::StartNew()
+function Check-Alive {
+    if ($Process.HasExited) { throw "${Stage}: Ivy exited before normal close ($($Process.ExitCode))" }
+    if ([IvyLaunchNative]::Windows($Process.Id) | Where-Object { $_.Class -eq '#32770' }) { throw "${Stage}: unexpected modal dialog" }
+    if ($Clock.Elapsed.TotalSeconds -ge $TimeoutSeconds) { throw "deadline: $Stage exceeded ${TimeoutSeconds}s" }
+}
+try {
+    if ($Control -eq 'layout') { New-Item -ItemType Directory $Layout -Force | Out-Null }
+    $Process = [Diagnostics.Process]::Start($Start)
+    $Stdout = $Process.StandardOutput.ReadToEndAsync()
+    $Stderr = $Process.StandardError.ReadToEndAsync()
+    if ($Control -eq 'crash') {
+        $Process.Kill($true)
+        if (-not $Process.WaitForExit(5000)) { throw 'control: startup termination did not complete' }
+    }
+    $Window = $null
+    while ($null -eq $Window) {
+        Check-Alive
+        $Windows = @([IvyLaunchNative]::Windows($Process.Id))
+        if ($Windows | Where-Object { $_.Class -eq '#32770' }) { throw 'startup: unexpected modal dialog' }
+        $Window = $Windows | Where-Object { $_.Title -match 'Ivy' -and $_.Class -ne '#32770' } | Select-Object -First 1
+        if ($null -eq $Window) { Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 50 }
+    }
+    $Stage = 'shell'
+    $Shell = $null
+    while ($null -eq $Shell) {
+        Check-Alive
+        $Children = @(Descendants $Process.Id)
+        $Shell = $Children | Where-Object { $_.ExecutablePath -ieq $Expected } | Select-Object -First 1
+        if ($null -eq $Shell) { Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 50 }
+    }
+    $Shell | Select-Object ProcessId, ParentProcessId, ExecutablePath, CommandLine | ConvertTo-Json | Set-Content (Join-Path $EvidenceDir 'shell.json')
+    $ShellProcess = [Diagnostics.Process]::GetProcessById($Shell.ProcessId)
+    if ($Control -eq 'shell') { Stop-Process -Id $Shell.ProcessId -Force }
+    $Activated = [IvyLaunchNative]::SetForegroundWindow($Window.Handle)
+    if (-not $Activated -and [IvyLaunchNative]::GetForegroundWindow() -ne $Window.Handle) { throw 'shell: could not focus the application window' }
+    while ([IvyLaunchNative]::GetForegroundWindow() -ne $Window.Handle) {
+        Check-Alive
+        Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 50
+    }
+    if ([IO.Path]::GetFileName($Expected) -ieq 'cmd.exe') {
+        $Command = "echo $Token>`"$Witness`""
+    } else {
+        $Escaped = $Witness.Replace("'", "''")
+        $Command = "[IO.File]::WriteAllText('$Escaped', '$Token|' + `$PID)"
+    }
+    [IvyLaunchNative]::Type($Command)
+    $Required = if ([IO.Path]::GetFileName($Expected) -ieq 'cmd.exe') { $Token } else { "$Token|$($Shell.ProcessId)" }
+    while ($true) {
+        if ($ShellProcess.HasExited) { throw 'shell: console program exited without executing the input witness' }
+        Check-Alive
+        if (Test-Path $Witness -PathType Leaf) {
+            try { $Text = [IO.File]::ReadAllText($Witness).Trim() } catch [IO.IOException] { $Text = '' }
+            if ($Text -eq $Required) { break }
+        }
+        Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 50
+    }
+    $Stage = 'close'
+    if ($Control -ne 'deadline') {
+        if (-not [IvyLaunchNative]::PostMessage($Window.Handle, 0x0010, [IntPtr]::Zero, [IntPtr]::Zero)) { throw 'close: WM_CLOSE failed' }
+    }
+    while (-not $Process.HasExited) {
+        if ($Clock.Elapsed.TotalSeconds -ge $TimeoutSeconds) { throw 'deadline: normal shutdown did not finish' }
+        Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 50
+    }
+    if ($Process.ExitCode -ne 0) { throw "close: Ivy exited $($Process.ExitCode)" }
+    $Known = @($Children | ForEach-Object { [int]$_.ProcessId })
+    while (@(Descendants $Process.Id $Known).Count -ne 0) {
+        if ($Clock.Elapsed.TotalSeconds -ge $TimeoutSeconds) { throw 'close: descendant processes survived normal shutdown' }
+        Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 50
+    }
+    $Stage = 'layout'
+    if (-not (Test-Path $Layout -PathType Leaf)) { throw 'layout: no saved layout' }
+    $Document = Get-Content $Layout -Raw | ConvertFrom-Json
+    if ($Document.v -ne 2 -or @($Document.tabs).Count -eq 0) { throw 'layout: empty or unsupported document' }
+    $Leaves = 0
+    foreach ($Tab in $Document.tabs) { $Leaves += Saved-Leaves $Tab.root }
+    if ($Leaves -eq 0) { throw 'layout: no saved console' }
+    Write-Output "Installed launch passed: native window, $Expected, real console input, $Leaves saved console(s), normal exit 0."
+} catch {
+    $_ | Out-String | Set-Content (Join-Path $EvidenceDir 'failure.txt')
+    if ($null -ne $Process) {
+        [IvyLaunchNative]::Windows($Process.Id) | Select-Object Title, Class, Handle | ConvertTo-Json | Set-Content (Join-Path $EvidenceDir 'windows.json')
+        @(Descendants $Process.Id) | Select-Object ProcessId, ParentProcessId, ExecutablePath, CommandLine | ConvertTo-Json | Set-Content (Join-Path $EvidenceDir 'processes.json')
+    }
+    throw
+} finally {
+    if ($null -ne $Process) {
+        $Remaining = @(Descendants $Process.Id @($Children | ForEach-Object { [int]$_.ProcessId }))
+        if (-not $Process.HasExited) {
+            try { $Process.Kill($true) } catch { if (-not $Process.HasExited) { throw } }
+            if (-not $Process.WaitForExit(5000)) { throw 'cleanup: Ivy did not terminate' }
+        }
+        foreach ($Child in $Remaining) { Stop-Process -Id $Child.ProcessId -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue }
+        foreach ($Log in @(@($Stdout, 'stdout.txt'), @($Stderr, 'stderr.txt'))) {
+            if ($null -ne $Log[0]) {
+                $Text = if ($Log[0].Wait(5000)) { $Log[0].GetAwaiter().GetResult() } else { 'Output pipe did not close after cleanup.' }
+                $Text | Set-Content (Join-Path $EvidenceDir $Log[1])
+            }
+        }
+        $Process.Dispose()
+    }
+}
diff --git a/scripts/test_windows_test_report.py b/scripts/test_windows_test_report.py
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..656dbb3
--- /dev/null
+++ b/scripts/test_windows_test_report.py
@@ -0,0 +1,148 @@
+"""Unit tests for the compiler-backed Unix-only omission checker."""
+
+import importlib.util
+import json
+import tempfile
+import unittest
+from pathlib import Path
+
+MODULE_PATH = Path(__file__).with_name("windows-test-report.py")
+SPEC = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("windows_test_report", MODULE_PATH)
+assert SPEC and SPEC.loader
+reporter = importlib.util.module_from_spec(SPEC)
+SPEC.loader.exec_module(reporter)
+
+
+def entry(name, binary="ivy/app:lib", reason="unix-only: fork"):
+    return {"binary": binary, "name": name, "reason": reason}
+
+
+def baseline(name, platform="windows", binary="ivy/app:lib", reason="manual diagnostic"):
+    return {"platform": platform, "binary": binary, "name": name, "reason": reason}
+
+
+class WindowsTestReportTests(unittest.TestCase):
+    def test_inventory_requires_array_and_exact_fields(self):
+        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as directory:
+            path = Path(directory) / "inventory.json"
+            path.write_text('{"binary":"ivy/app:lib"}', encoding="utf-8")
+            with self.assertRaisesRegex(reporter.ReportError, "JSON array"):
+                reporter.parse_inventory(path)
+            path.write_text('[{"binary":"ivy/app:lib","name":"x","reason":"fork"}]', encoding="utf-8")
+            with self.assertRaisesRegex(reporter.ReportError, "unix-only"):
+                reporter.parse_inventory(path)
+            path.write_text(
+                '[{"binary":"ivy/app:lib","name":"x","reason":"unix-only: scope",'
+                '"linux_ignore_reason":"requires a real systemd manager"}]',
+                encoding="utf-8",
+            )
+            self.assertEqual("requires a real systemd manager", reporter.parse_inventory(path)[0]["linux_ignore_reason"])
+
+    def test_baseline_is_platform_qualified_and_rejects_duplicates(self):
+        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as directory:
+            path = Path(directory) / "baseline.json"
+            good = baseline("tests::manual")
+            path.write_text(json.dumps([good]), encoding="utf-8")
+            self.assertEqual([good], reporter.parse_ignore_baseline(path))
+            path.write_text(json.dumps([good, good]), encoding="utf-8")
+            with self.assertRaisesRegex(reporter.ReportError, "duplicate ignored-test baseline"):
+                reporter.parse_ignore_baseline(path)
+
+    def test_duplicate_entries_are_rejected(self):
+        compiled = {"ivy/app:lib": ({"tests::fork"}, {"tests::fork"})}
+        with self.assertRaisesRegex(reporter.ReportError, "duplicate inventory"):
+            reporter.validate([entry("tests::fork"), entry("tests::fork")], compiled, "windows")
+
+    def test_missing_inventory_entry_is_rejected_against_compiled_ignored_set(self):
+        compiled = {"ivy/app:lib": ({"tests::fork", "tests::socket"}, {"tests::fork", "tests::socket"})}
+        errors = reporter.validate([entry("tests::fork")], compiled, "windows")
+        self.assertTrue(any("tests::socket" in error for error in errors))
+
+    def test_inventory_entry_must_be_a_compiled_ignored_test_on_windows(self):
+        compiled = {"ivy/app:lib": ({"tests::fork", "tests::portable"}, {"tests::fork"})}
+        errors = reporter.validate([entry("tests::portable")], compiled, "windows")
+        self.assertTrue(any("tests::portable" in error for error in errors))
+
+    def test_unrelated_ignore_baseline_is_distinct_and_checked(self):
+        compiled = {"ivy/app:lib": ({"tests::fork", "tests::manual"}, {"tests::fork", "tests::manual"})}
+        inventory = [entry("tests::fork")]
+        known = [baseline("tests::manual")]
+        self.assertEqual([], reporter.validate(inventory, compiled, "windows", known))
+        errors = reporter.validate(inventory, compiled, "windows", [])
+        self.assertTrue(any("tests::manual" in error for error in errors))
+        errors = reporter.validate(inventory, compiled, "windows", known + [baseline("tests::removed")])
+        self.assertTrue(any("tests::removed" in error for error in errors))
+
+    def test_linux_baseline_requires_exact_existing_ignore_set(self):
+        compiled = {"ivy/app:lib": ({"tests::fork", "tests::manual"}, {"tests::manual"})}
+        inventory = [entry("tests::fork")]
+        known = [baseline("tests::manual", platform="linux")]
+        self.assertEqual([], reporter.validate(inventory, compiled, "linux", known))
+        errors = reporter.validate(inventory, compiled, "linux", [])
+        self.assertTrue(any("missing from inventory or baseline on Linux" in error for error in errors))
+        compiled_without_ignore = {"ivy/app:lib": ({"tests::fork", "tests::manual"}, set())}
+        errors = reporter.validate(inventory, compiled_without_ignore, "linux", known)
+        self.assertTrue(any("baseline tests are not ignored on Linux" in error for error in errors))
+
+    def test_preignored_linux_unix_omission_stays_in_inventory(self):
+        item = entry("tests::systemd", reason="unix-only: systemd user scopes")
+        item["linux_ignore_reason"] = "requires an owned systemd test scope"
+        compiled = {"ivy/app:lib": ({"tests::systemd"}, {"tests::systemd"})}
+        self.assertEqual([], reporter.validate([item], compiled, "linux"))
+        errors = reporter.validate([item], {"ivy/app:lib": ({"tests::systemd"}, set())}, "linux")
+        self.assertTrue(any("expected ignored on Linux" in error for error in errors))
+
+    def test_linux_requires_each_omission_to_be_a_compiled_real_test(self):
+        inventory = [entry("tests::fork")]
+        self.assertEqual([], reporter.validate(inventory, {"ivy/app:lib": ({"tests::fork"}, set())}, "linux"))
+        errors = reporter.validate(inventory, {"ivy/app:lib": ({"tests::other"}, set())}, "linux")
+        self.assertTrue(any("tests::fork" in error for error in errors))
+        errors = reporter.validate(inventory, {"ivy/app:lib": ({"tests::fork"}, {"tests::fork"})}, "linux")
+        self.assertTrue(any("ignored on Linux" in error for error in errors))
+
+    def test_truncated_or_changed_list_format_is_rejected(self):
+        with self.assertRaisesRegex(reporter.ReportError, "count does not match"):
+            reporter.parse_list_output("tests::fork: test\n2 tests, 0 benchmarks\n")
+
+    def test_libtest_list_parser_uses_names_only(self):
+        output = "tests::fork: test\ntests::ordinary: test\n\n2 tests, 0 benchmarks\n"
+        self.assertEqual({"tests::fork", "tests::ordinary"}, reporter.parse_list_output(output))
+
+    def test_libtest_list_rejects_duplicate_or_unrecognized_output(self):
+        with self.assertRaisesRegex(reporter.ReportError, "duplicate test names"):
+            reporter.parse_list_output("tests::fork: test\ntests::fork: test\n2 tests, 0 benchmarks\n")
+        with self.assertRaisesRegex(reporter.ReportError, "unrecognized"):
+            reporter.parse_list_output("some unexpected output\n")
+
+    def test_exact_ignored_query_checks_reason_and_does_not_run_the_body(self):
+        name = "tests::fork"
+        reason = "unix-only: fork"
+        output = (
+            "running 1 test\n"
+            f"test {name} ... ignored, {reason}\n\n"
+            "test result: ok. 0 passed; 0 failed; 1 ignored; 0 measured; 1 filtered out; finished in 0.00s\n"
+        )
+        reporter.validate_ignored_reason_output(name, reason, output)
+
+    def test_exact_ignored_query_rejects_reason_mismatch_or_missing_reason(self):
+        name = "tests::fork"
+        good_summary = "test result: ok. 0 passed; 0 failed; 1 ignored; 0 measured; 1 filtered out\n"
+        with self.assertRaisesRegex(reporter.ReportError, "expected ignored reason"):
+            reporter.validate_ignored_reason_output(
+                name, "unix-only: fork", f"test {name} ... ignored, unix-only: sockets\n{good_summary}"
+            )
+        with self.assertRaisesRegex(reporter.ReportError, "expected ignored reason"):
+            reporter.validate_ignored_reason_output(name, "unix-only: fork", f"test {name} ... ignored\n{good_summary}")
+
+    def test_exact_ignored_query_rejects_accidentally_executed_test(self):
+        name = "tests::fork"
+        output = (
+            f"test {name} ... ok\n"
+            "test result: ok. 1 passed; 0 failed; 0 ignored; 0 measured; 1 filtered out\n"
+        )
+        with self.assertRaisesRegex(reporter.ReportError, "exactly 0 passed, 1 ignored, 0 failed"):
+            reporter.validate_ignored_reason_output(name, "unix-only: fork", output)
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    unittest.main()

diff --git a/scripts/windows-test-report.py b/scripts/windows-test-report.py
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..e78275a
--- /dev/null
+++ b/scripts/windows-test-report.py
@@ -0,0 +1,309 @@
+#!/usr/bin/env python3
+"""Check the Windows Unix-only test inventory against compiled libtest harnesses."""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import argparse
+import json
+import os
+import re
+import subprocess
+import sys
+from pathlib import Path
+from typing import Iterable
+
+ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
+DEFAULT_INVENTORY = ROOT / "scripts" / "windows-test-omissions.json"
+DEFAULT_IGNORE_BASELINE = ROOT / "scripts" / "windows-test-ignore-baseline.json"
+
+
+class ReportError(Exception):
+    pass
+
+
+def parse_inventory(path: Path) -> list[dict[str, str]]:
+    try:
+        data = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
+    except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
+        raise ReportError(f"cannot read omission inventory {path}: {exc}") from exc
+    if not isinstance(data, list):
+        raise ReportError("omission inventory must be a JSON array")
+    required = {"binary", "name", "reason"}
+    result = []
+    for index, entry in enumerate(data):
+        optional = {"linux_ignore_reason"}
+        if not isinstance(entry, dict) or not (set(entry) == required or set(entry) == required | optional):
+            raise ReportError(f"inventory entry {index} must contain {sorted(required)} and optional linux_ignore_reason")
+        if not all(isinstance(entry[key], str) and entry[key] for key in required):
+            raise ReportError(f"inventory entry {index} fields must be nonempty strings")
+        if not re.fullmatch(r"unix-only: .+", entry["reason"]):
+            raise ReportError(f"inventory entry {index} reason must start with 'unix-only: '")
+        if "linux_ignore_reason" in entry and (
+            not isinstance(entry["linux_ignore_reason"], str) or not entry["linux_ignore_reason"]
+        ):
+            raise ReportError(f"inventory entry {index} linux_ignore_reason must be a nonempty string")
+        result.append(entry)
+    return result
+
+
+def parse_ignore_baseline(path: Path) -> list[dict[str, str]]:
+    try:
+        data = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
+    except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
+        raise ReportError(f"cannot read ignored-test baseline {path}: {exc}") from exc
+    if not isinstance(data, list):
+        raise ReportError("ignored-test baseline must be a JSON array")
+    required = {"platform", "binary", "name", "reason"}
+    seen: set[tuple[str, str, str]] = set()
+    result = []
+    for index, entry in enumerate(data):
+        if not isinstance(entry, dict) or set(entry) != required:
+            raise ReportError(f"baseline entry {index} must contain exactly {sorted(required)}")
+        if not all(isinstance(entry[key], str) and entry[key] for key in required):
+            raise ReportError(f"baseline entry {index} fields must be nonempty strings")
+        if entry["platform"] not in {"linux", "windows"}:
+            raise ReportError(f"baseline entry {index} platform must be linux or windows")
+        key = entry["platform"], entry["binary"], entry["name"]
+        if key in seen:
+            raise ReportError(f"duplicate ignored-test baseline entry: {key[0]} {key[1]}::{key[2]}")
+        seen.add(key)
+        result.append(entry)
+    return result
+
+
+def parse_list_output(output: str) -> set[str]:
+    """Extract test names from stable libtest --list lines (including ignored entries)."""
+    names: list[str] = []
+    for line in output.splitlines():
+        match = re.fullmatch(r"(.+): test", line.strip())
+        if match:
+            names.append(match.group(1))
+    summary = re.search(r"(?m)^(\d+) tests?, \d+ benchmarks?\s*$", output)
+    if summary is None:
+        raise ReportError("unrecognized libtest --list output (missing test summary)")
+    if len(names) != int(summary.group(1)):
+        raise ReportError("libtest listing count does not match its test summary")
+    found: set[str] = set()
+    duplicates: set[str] = set()
+    for name in names:
+        if name in found:
+            duplicates.add(name)
+        found.add(name)
+    if duplicates:
+        raise ReportError(f"duplicate test names in libtest listing: {', '.join(sorted(duplicates))}")
+    return found
+
+
+def validate_ignored_reason_output(name: str, reason: str, output: str) -> None:
+    """Check default libtest selection reports this exact ignored case and reason."""
+    summary = re.search(
+        r"^test result: ok\. (\d+) passed; (\d+) failed; (\d+) ignored;",
+        output,
+        re.MULTILINE,
+    )
+    if not summary or summary.groups() != ("0", "0", "1"):
+        raise ReportError(f"{name}: filtered run did not report exactly 0 passed, 1 ignored, 0 failed")
+    expected_line = f"test {name} ... ignored, {reason}"
+    if expected_line not in output.splitlines():
+        raise ReportError(f"{name}: libtest did not report expected ignored reason {reason!r}")
+
+
+def validate(
+    inventory: list[dict[str, str]],
+    compiled: dict[str, tuple[set[str], set[str]]],
+    platform: str,
+    unrelated_baseline: list[dict[str, str]] | None = None,
+) -> list[str]:
+    """Validate inventory against binary -> (all test names, ignored test names)."""
+    if platform not in {"linux", "windows"}:
+        raise ReportError(f"unsupported platform {platform!r}")
+    seen: set[tuple[str, str]] = set()
+    for entry in inventory:
+        key = entry["binary"], entry["name"]
+        if key in seen:
+            raise ReportError(f"duplicate inventory entry: {key[0]}::{key[1]}")
+        seen.add(key)
+        if key[0] not in compiled:
+            raise ReportError(f"inventory binary did not compile: {key[0]}")
+
+    unrelated_baseline = [entry for entry in (unrelated_baseline or []) if entry["platform"] == platform]
+    baseline_keys = {(entry["binary"], entry["name"]) for entry in unrelated_baseline}
+    for binary, name in baseline_keys:
+        if binary not in compiled:
+            raise ReportError(f"unrelated-ignore baseline binary did not compile on {platform}: {binary}")
+        if (binary, name) in seen:
+            raise ReportError(f"inventory and unrelated-ignore baseline overlap: {binary}::{name}")
+
+    mismatches: list[str] = []
+    for binary, (all_tests, ignored) in compiled.items():
+        declared = {name for name_bin, name in seen if name_bin == binary}
+        baseline_names = {name for bin_name, name in baseline_keys if bin_name == binary}
+        if platform == "windows":
+            expected_ignored = declared | baseline_names
+            missing = expected_ignored - ignored
+            unexpected = ignored - expected_ignored
+            if missing:
+                mismatches.append(f"{binary}: expected ignored tests not compiled as ignored: {', '.join(sorted(missing))}")
+            if unexpected:
+                mismatches.append(f"{binary}: ignored tests missing from omission inventory or unrelated baseline: {', '.join(sorted(unexpected))}")
+        else:
+            baseline_missing = baseline_names - ignored
+            linux_ignored = {item["name"] for item in inventory if item["binary"] == binary and "linux_ignore_reason" in item}
+            declared_real = declared - linux_ignored
+            absent = declared - all_tests
+            wrongly_ignored = declared_real & ignored
+            missing_linux_ignored = linux_ignored - ignored
+            if absent:
+                mismatches.append(f"{binary}: inventory tests not compiled on Linux: {', '.join(sorted(absent))}")
+            if wrongly_ignored:
+                mismatches.append(f"{binary}: inventory tests are ignored on Linux: {', '.join(sorted(wrongly_ignored))}")
+            if baseline_missing:
+                mismatches.append(f"{binary}: baseline tests are not ignored on Linux: {', '.join(sorted(baseline_missing))}")
+            if missing_linux_ignored:
+                mismatches.append(f"{binary}: inventory tests expected ignored on Linux are not ignored: {', '.join(sorted(missing_linux_ignored))}")
+            expected_ignored = linux_ignored | baseline_names
+            unexpected = ignored - expected_ignored
+            if unexpected:
+                mismatches.append(f"{binary}: ignored tests missing from inventory or baseline on Linux: {', '.join(sorted(unexpected))}")
+    return mismatches
+
+
+def cargo_test_executables() -> list[tuple[str, Path]]:
+    try:
+        metadata = subprocess.run(
+            ["cargo", "metadata", "--locked", "--no-deps", "--format-version", "1"],
+            cwd=ROOT,
+            text=True,
+            stdout=subprocess.PIPE,
+            stderr=None,
+            check=False,
+            timeout=120,
+        )
+    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired as exc:
+        raise ReportError("cargo metadata exceeded 120 seconds") from exc
+    if metadata.returncode:
+        raise ReportError(f"cargo metadata failed with exit code {metadata.returncode}")
+    package_names = {package["id"]: package["name"] for package in json.loads(metadata.stdout)["packages"]}
+    try:
+        proc = subprocess.run(
+            ["cargo", "test", "--locked", "--workspace", "--no-run", "--message-format=json"],
+            cwd=ROOT,
+            text=True,
+            stdout=subprocess.PIPE,
+            stderr=None,
+            check=False,
+            timeout=30 * 60,
+        )
+    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired as exc:
+        raise ReportError("cargo test --no-run exceeded 30 minutes") from exc
+    if proc.returncode:
+        raise ReportError(f"cargo test --no-run failed with exit code {proc.returncode}")
+    found: dict[tuple[str, str, str], Path] = {}
+    for line in proc.stdout.splitlines():
+        try:
+            message = json.loads(line)
+        except json.JSONDecodeError:
+            continue
+        if message.get("reason") != "compiler-artifact" or not message.get("executable"):
+            continue
+        target = message.get("target", {})
+        profile = message.get("profile", {})
+        if not profile.get("test"):
+            continue
+        package = package_names.get(message.get("package_id", ""))
+        target_name = target.get("name")
+        kinds = target.get("kind", [])
+        if package and target_name and kinds:
+            found[(package, target_name, ",".join(sorted(kinds)))] = Path(message["executable"])
+    if not found:
+        raise ReportError("cargo emitted no compiled test executables")
+    return [(f"{package}/{target}:{kind}", path) for (package, target, kind), path in sorted(found.items())]
+
+
+def load_compiled(executables: Iterable[tuple[str, Path]]) -> dict[str, tuple[set[str], set[str]]]:
+    compiled = {}
+    for binary, executable in executables:
+        try:
+            all_proc = subprocess.run([str(executable), "--list"], text=True, capture_output=True, check=False, timeout=30)
+        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired as exc:
+            raise ReportError(f"{binary}: --list exceeded 30 seconds") from exc
+        if all_proc.returncode:
+            raise ReportError(f"{binary}: --list failed: {all_proc.stderr.strip()}")
+        try:
+            ignored_proc = subprocess.run(
+                [str(executable), "--list", "--ignored"], text=True, capture_output=True, check=False, timeout=30
+            )
+        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired as exc:
+            raise ReportError(f"{binary}: --list --ignored exceeded 30 seconds") from exc
+        if ignored_proc.returncode:
+            raise ReportError(f"{binary}: --list --ignored failed: {ignored_proc.stderr.strip()}")
+        compiled[binary] = (parse_list_output(all_proc.stdout), parse_list_output(ignored_proc.stdout))
+    return compiled
+
+
+def verify_ignored_reason(executable: Path, name: str, reason: str) -> None:
+    """Ask libtest to select one test normally; ignored bodies remain unexecuted."""
+    try:
+        proc = subprocess.run(
+            [str(executable), "--exact", name, "--color", "never"],
+            cwd=ROOT,
+            text=True,
+            stdout=subprocess.PIPE,
+            stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
+            check=False,
+            timeout=15,
+        )
+    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired as exc:
+        raise ReportError(f"{name}: exact ignored-reason query exceeded 15 seconds") from exc
+    if proc.returncode:
+        raise ReportError(f"{name}: exact ignored-reason query failed with exit code {proc.returncode}: {proc.stdout}")
+    validate_ignored_reason_output(name, reason, proc.stdout)
+
+
+def main() -> int:
+    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
+    parser.add_argument("--platform", choices=("linux", "windows"), default=None)
+    parser.add_argument("--inventory", type=Path, default=DEFAULT_INVENTORY)
+    parser.add_argument("--baseline", type=Path, default=DEFAULT_IGNORE_BASELINE)
+    args = parser.parse_args()
+    platform = args.platform or ("windows" if os.name == "nt" else "linux")
+    try:
+        inventory = parse_inventory(args.inventory)
+        baseline = parse_ignore_baseline(args.baseline)
+        executables = cargo_test_executables()
+        compiled = load_compiled(executables)
+        mismatches = validate(inventory, compiled, platform, baseline)
+        if mismatches:
+            raise ReportError("\n".join(mismatches))
+        executable_by_binary = dict(executables)
+        reason_checks = []
+        if platform == "windows":
+            reason_checks.extend((entry["binary"], entry["name"], entry["reason"]) for entry in inventory)
+        else:
+            reason_checks.extend(
+                (entry["binary"], entry["name"], entry["linux_ignore_reason"])
+                for entry in inventory if "linux_ignore_reason" in entry
+            )
+        reason_checks.extend(
+            (entry["binary"], entry["name"], entry["reason"])
+            for entry in baseline if entry["platform"] == platform
+        )
+        for binary, name, reason in reason_checks:
+            verify_ignored_reason(executable_by_binary[binary], name, reason)
+        for entry in sorted(inventory, key=lambda item: (item["binary"], item["name"])):
+            print(f"omitted {entry['binary']}::{entry['name']} — {entry['reason']}")
+            if platform == "linux" and "linux_ignore_reason" in entry:
+                print(f"  existing Linux ignore: {entry['linux_ignore_reason']}")
+        print(f"Windows Unix-only omissions: {len(inventory)}")
+        for entry in sorted((item for item in baseline if item["platform"] == platform), key=lambda item: (item["binary"], item["name"])):
+            print(f"unrelated ignored test [{platform}]: {entry['binary']}::{entry['name']} — {entry['reason']}")
+        print(f"Unrelated ignored tests [{platform}]: {sum(item['platform'] == platform for item in baseline)}")
+        print(f"Compiled test executables checked: {len(compiled)} ({platform})")
+        return 0
+    except ReportError as exc:
+        print(f"windows-test-report: {exc}", file=sys.stderr)
+        return 1
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    raise SystemExit(main())
```

## Changed files, in full

### .github/workflows/ivy-prerelease.yml

```
name: Ivy packages

on:
  pull_request:
  workflow_dispatch:
    inputs:
      publish:
        description: Publish the selected tag as an unsigned Linux, macOS and Windows prerelease
        type: boolean
        default: false

permissions:
  contents: read

concurrency:
  group: ivy-package-${{ github.ref }}-${{ github.event_name }}
  cancel-in-progress: ${{ github.event_name == 'pull_request' }}

jobs:
  checks:
    if: ${{ github.event_name == 'pull_request' }}
    runs-on: ubuntu-24.04
    timeout-minutes: 30
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@fbc6f3992d24b796d5a048ff273f7fcc4a7b6c09 # v5.1.0
      - name: Install Linux build dependencies
        run: sudo apt-get update && sudo apt-get install -y build-essential libxkbcommon-dev libssl-dev pkg-config
      - name: Check formatting
        run: cargo fmt --all --check
      - name: Lint workspace
        run: cargo clippy --locked --workspace --all-targets -- -D warnings
      - name: Test workspace
        run: cargo test --locked --workspace
      - name: Check documentation links
        run: cargo doc-check --locked
      - name: Test portable packaging logic
        run: python3 -m unittest scripts/test_release.py scripts/test_macos.py scripts/test_windows.py -v
      - name: Check compiled Windows omission inventory on Linux
        run: python3 scripts/windows-test-report.py --platform linux
      - name: Test omission reporter fixtures
        run: python3 -m unittest scripts/test_windows_test_report.py -v

  macos-panel-checks:
    if: ${{ github.event_name == 'pull_request' }}
    runs-on: ${{ matrix.runner }}
    timeout-minutes: 15
    strategy:
      fail-fast: false
      matrix:
        runner: [macos-15, macos-15-intel]
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@fbc6f3992d24b796d5a048ff273f7fcc4a7b6c09 # v5.1.0
      - name: Test native panel routing and Codex launcher
        run: cargo test --locked -p panel-control

  # Keep this job name for branch protection. The full workspace suite now exercises Windows cfgs.
  windows-startup-checks:
    if: ${{ github.event_name == 'pull_request' || github.event_name == 'workflow_dispatch' }}
    runs-on: windows-2025
    timeout-minutes: 45
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@fbc6f3992d24b796d5a048ff273f7fcc4a7b6c09 # v5.1.0
      - name: Check Windows launch helper contracts
        shell: pwsh
        run: ./packaging/windows/test-launch-contract.ps1
      - name: Lint all Windows workspace targets
        shell: pwsh
        run: |
          cargo clippy --locked --workspace --all-targets -- -D warnings
          if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { exit $LASTEXITCODE }
      - name: Test the Windows workspace
        shell: pwsh
        run: |
          cargo test --locked --workspace
          if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { exit $LASTEXITCODE }
      - name: Check compiler-listed Unix-only omissions
        shell: pwsh
        run: |
          python scripts/windows-test-report.py --platform windows
          if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { exit $LASTEXITCODE }
      - name: Keep named Windows startup witnesses
        shell: pwsh
        run: |
          cargo test --locked -p app --lib -- windows_startup 2>&1 | Tee-Object -Variable printed
          if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { exit $LASTEXITCODE }
          foreach ($name in 'windows_startup_keeps_the_configuration_in_appdata',
                            'windows_startup_finds_a_configuration_directory',
                            'windows_startup_default_and_fallback_run_a_command') {
            if (-not ($printed -match "$name \.\.\. ok$")) { throw "$name did not run and pass" }
          }
      - name: Test the Windows path arms
        shell: pwsh
        # A filter exits 0 when it matches nothing, and a cfg typo compiles a test out in silence, so
        # the Windows-only tests have to be named in the output.
        run: |
          cargo test --locked -p app --lib -- windows_paths 2>&1 | Tee-Object -Variable printed
          if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { exit $LASTEXITCODE }
          foreach ($name in 'windows_paths_project_identity_has_no_verbatim_prefix',
                            'windows_paths_roadmap_aliases_have_no_verbatim_prefix',
                            'windows_paths_untracked_file_symlink_is_not_followed',
                            'windows_paths_untracked_listing_runs_git_on_the_verbatim_root',
                            'windows_paths_tilde_follows_home_dir',
                            'windows_paths_a_link_planted_at_the_temporary_is_not_written_through') {
            if (-not ($printed -match "$name \.\.\. ok$")) { throw "$name did not run and pass" }
          }

  build:
    if: ${{ github.event_name == 'workflow_dispatch' }}
    runs-on: ubuntu-24.04
    timeout-minutes: 30
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@fbc6f3992d24b796d5a048ff273f7fcc4a7b6c09 # v5.1.0
      - name: Validate selected tag for publication
        if: ${{ github.event_name == 'workflow_dispatch' && inputs.publish }}
        env:
          REF_TYPE: ${{ github.ref_type }}
          REF_NAME: ${{ github.ref_name }}
          EXPECTED_COMMIT: ${{ github.sha }}
        run: |
          test "$REF_TYPE" = tag
          test "$(git rev-parse HEAD)" = "$EXPECTED_COMMIT"
          python3 scripts/release.py version --tag "$REF_NAME"
          scripts/verify-release-tag.sh "$REF_NAME" "$EXPECTED_COMMIT"
      - name: Install Linux build dependencies
        run: sudo apt-get update && sudo apt-get install -y build-essential libxkbcommon-dev libssl-dev pkg-config dpkg-dev binutils desktop-file-utils
      - name: Build pinned workspace binaries
        run: cargo build --locked --release -p app -p panel-control --bins
      - name: Package and record checksums
        env:
          COMMIT: ${{ github.sha }}
        run: |
          cargo metadata --locked --filter-platform x86_64-unknown-linux-gnu --format-version 1 > target/release-metadata.json
          python3 scripts/release.py notices --metadata target/release-metadata.json --directory target/release/notices
          version=$(python3 scripts/release.py version)
          scripts/package-linux.sh --binary-dir target/release --output-dir dist --version "$version"
          scripts/test-linux-package.sh dist/*.deb "$version" target/release/notices
          python3 -m unittest scripts/test_release.py scripts/test_macos.py scripts/test_windows.py -v
          python3 scripts/release.py generate --directory dist --commit "$COMMIT" --built-on ubuntu-24.04
          (cd dist && sha256sum --check SHA256SUMS)
      - name: Upload package and metadata
        uses: actions/upload-artifact@b7c566a772e6b6bfb58ed0dc250532a479d7789f # v6.0.0
        with:
          name: ivy-linux-x86_64
          path: dist/
          if-no-files-found: error

  install:
    needs: build
    runs-on: ubuntu-24.04
    timeout-minutes: 15
    strategy:
      matrix:
        ubuntu: ['24.04', '26.04']
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@fbc6f3992d24b796d5a048ff273f7fcc4a7b6c09 # v5.1.0
      - uses: actions/download-artifact@37930b1c2abaa49bbe596cd826c3c89aef350131 # v7.0.0
        with:
          name: ivy-linux-x86_64
          path: dist
      - name: Install, upgrade and remove in a disposable Ubuntu container
        env:
          UBUNTU_VERSION: ${{ matrix.ubuntu }}
        run: |
          docker run --rm --env IVY_DISPOSABLE_CONTAINER=yes \
            --mount "type=bind,src=$PWD,dst=/src,readonly" \
            --mount "type=bind,src=$PWD/dist,dst=/dist,readonly" \
            "ubuntu:$UBUNTU_VERSION" bash -euc '
              set -- /dist/*.deb
              test "$#" -eq 1
              package=$1
              # Ubuntu container images skip /usr/share/doc, which a desktop install keeps; the
              # license notices live there and dpkg --verify would report them missing.
              rm -f /etc/dpkg/dpkg.cfg.d/excludes
              apt-get update -qq
              DEBIAN_FRONTEND=noninteractive apt-get install -y "$package" python3 xvfb xauth xdotool
              dpkg -r ivy-terminal
              /src/packaging/linux/test-install.sh "$package"
            '

  arch:
    needs: build
    runs-on: ubuntu-24.04
    timeout-minutes: 15
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@fbc6f3992d24b796d5a048ff273f7fcc4a7b6c09 # v5.1.0
      - uses: actions/download-artifact@37930b1c2abaa49bbe596cd826c3c89aef350131 # v7.0.0
        with:
          name: ivy-linux-x86_64
          path: baseline
      - name: Prepare the verified shared Linux payload
        run: |
          (cd baseline && sha256sum --check SHA256SUMS)
          dpkg-deb -x baseline/*.deb payload
          tar -cf payload.tar -C payload usr
          mkdir arch-dist
      - name: Build and test native Arch package
        run: |
          version=$(python3 scripts/release.py version)
          docker run --rm --env IVY_DISPOSABLE_CONTAINER=yes --env VERSION="$version" \
            --mount "type=bind,src=$PWD,dst=/src,readonly" \
            --mount "type=bind,src=$PWD/payload.tar,dst=/payload.tar,readonly" \
            --mount "type=bind,src=$PWD/arch-dist,dst=/out" \
            archlinux:base-devel bash -euc '
              pacman -Syu --noconfirm --needed python fontconfig libx11 libxcb libxcursor libxi libxrandr libxkbcommon libxkbcommon-x11 wayland libglvnd mesa xorg-server-xvfb xorg-xauth xdotool desktop-file-utils diffutils
              useradd -m -u 1000 builder
              chown builder /out
              runuser -u builder -- /src/scripts/package-arch.sh /payload.tar /out "$VERSION"
              arch_version=${VERSION/-/}
              arch_version=${arch_version/alpha./alpha}
              arch_version=${arch_version/beta./beta}
              arch_version=${arch_version/rc./rc}
              /src/packaging/arch/test-package.sh /out/*.pkg.tar.zst /payload.tar "$arch_version"
              /src/packaging/arch/test-install.sh /out/*.pkg.tar.zst "$VERSION"
              /src/packaging/arch/test-package.sh --versions
            '
      - uses: actions/upload-artifact@b7c566a772e6b6bfb58ed0dc250532a479d7789f # v6.0.0
        with:
          name: ivy-arch-x86_64
          path: arch-dist/*.pkg.tar.zst
          if-no-files-found: error

  macos:
    if: ${{ github.event_name == 'workflow_dispatch' }}
    runs-on: ${{ matrix.runner }}
    timeout-minutes: 35
    strategy:
      fail-fast: false
      matrix:
        include:
          - runner: macos-15
            target: aarch64-apple-darwin
            arch: arm64
          - runner: macos-15-intel
            target: x86_64-apple-darwin
            arch: x86_64
    env:
      MACOSX_DEPLOYMENT_TARGET: '15.0'
      TARGET: ${{ matrix.target }}
      ARCH: ${{ matrix.arch }}
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@fbc6f3992d24b796d5a048ff273f7fcc4a7b6c09 # v5.1.0
      - name: Build pinned native Mac executables
        run: |
          rustup target add "$TARGET"
          cargo build --locked --release --target "$TARGET" -p app -p panel-control --bins
      - name: Bundle original notices and create DMG
        run: |
          cargo metadata --locked --filter-platform "$TARGET" --format-version 1 > target/release-metadata.json
          python3 scripts/release.py notices --metadata target/release-metadata.json --directory "target/$TARGET/release/notices"
          version=$(python3 scripts/release.py version)
          python3 scripts/package-macos.py build --binary-dir "target/$TARGET/release" --directory dist --version "$version" --arch "$ARCH"
          python3 -m unittest scripts/test_macos.py -v
          python3 packaging/macos/test-install.py dist/*.dmg "$version" "$ARCH" "target/$TARGET/release/notices" "dist/Ivy-$version-macos-$ARCH.executables.json"
      - uses: actions/upload-artifact@b7c566a772e6b6bfb58ed0dc250532a479d7789f # v6.0.0
        with:
          name: ivy-macos-${{ matrix.arch }}
          path: dist/*.dmg
          if-no-files-found: error

  windows:
    needs: windows-startup-checks
    if: ${{ always() && (github.event_name == 'pull_request' || github.event_name == 'workflow_dispatch') && needs.windows-startup-checks.result == 'success' }}
    runs-on: windows-2025
    timeout-minutes: 40
    env:
      CARGO_TARGET_X86_64_PC_WINDOWS_MSVC_RUSTFLAGS: -C target-feature=+crt-static
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@fbc6f3992d24b796d5a048ff273f7fcc4a7b6c09 # v5.1.0
      - name: Build pinned native Windows executables
        shell: pwsh
        run: |
          cargo build --locked --release --target x86_64-pc-windows-msvc -p app -p panel-control --bins
          if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { exit $LASTEXITCODE }
          cargo metadata --locked --filter-platform x86_64-pc-windows-msvc --format-version 1 > target/release-metadata.json
          if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { exit $LASTEXITCODE }
      - name: Build per-user installer with original notices
        shell: pwsh
        run: |
          python scripts/release.py notices --metadata target/release-metadata.json --directory target/x86_64-pc-windows-msvc/release/notices
          if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { exit $LASTEXITCODE }
          $Version = python scripts/release.py version
          python scripts/package-windows.py --binary-dir target/x86_64-pc-windows-msvc/release --output-dir dist --version $Version
          if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { exit $LASTEXITCODE }
      - name: Check installed payload, replacement and removal
        shell: pwsh
        env:
          IVY_DISPOSABLE_RUNNER: 'yes'
        run: |
          $Installer = (Get-ChildItem dist/*-setup.exe).FullName
          ./packaging/windows/test-install.ps1 -Installer $Installer -BinaryDir target/x86_64-pc-windows-msvc/release
      - name: Upload Windows launch evidence
        if: always()
        uses: actions/upload-artifact@b7c566a772e6b6bfb58ed0dc250532a479d7789f # v6.0.0
        with:
          name: windows-launch-evidence
          path: windows-launch-evidence/
          if-no-files-found: ignore
      - uses: actions/upload-artifact@b7c566a772e6b6bfb58ed0dc250532a479d7789f # v6.0.0
        with:
          name: ivy-windows-x86_64
          path: dist/*-setup.exe
          if-no-files-found: error

  publish:
    if: ${{ github.event_name == 'workflow_dispatch' && inputs.publish }}
    needs: [build, install, arch, macos, windows, windows-startup-checks]
    runs-on: ubuntu-24.04
    timeout-minutes: 10
    permissions:
      contents: write
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@fbc6f3992d24b796d5a048ff273f7fcc4a7b6c09 # v5.1.0
      - uses: actions/download-artifact@37930b1c2abaa49bbe596cd826c3c89aef350131 # v7.0.0
        with:
          pattern: ivy-*
          path: dist
          merge-multiple: true
      - name: Validate and publish complete draft
        env:
          GH_TOKEN: ${{ github.token }}
          TAG: ${{ github.ref_name }}
          REF_TYPE: ${{ github.ref_type }}
          COMMIT: ${{ github.sha }}
        run: |
          set -eu
          test "$REF_TYPE" = tag
          test "$(git rev-parse HEAD)" = "$COMMIT"
          (cd dist && sha256sum --check SHA256SUMS)
          python3 scripts/release.py generate --directory dist --commit "$COMMIT" --built-on ubuntu-24.04 --tag "$TAG" --include-arch --include-macos --include-windows
          (cd dist && sha256sum --check SHA256SUMS)
          scripts/verify-release-tag.sh "$TAG" "$COMMIT"
          if gh release view "$TAG" --json id >/dev/null 2>&1; then
            echo 'A release already exists for this tag; inspect it instead of overwriting it.' >&2
            exit 1
          fi
          gh release create "$TAG" dist/*.deb dist/*.pkg.tar.zst dist/*.dmg dist/*-setup.exe dist/release-manifest.json dist/SHA256SUMS --verify-tag --draft --prerelease --title "$TAG" --notes-file resources/release-notes.md
          scripts/verify-release-tag.sh "$TAG" "$COMMIT"
          gh release edit "$TAG" --draft=false --prerelease
```

### crates/app/src/agent_setup.rs

```
//! Explicit installation of Ivy's own OpenCode integration asset.
use std::{
    fs::{self, OpenOptions},
    io::{self, Write},
    path::{Path, PathBuf},
    sync::atomic::{AtomicU64, Ordering},
};

const ASSET: &str = include_str!("../../../resources/agent-integrations/opencode/ivy-opencode.tsx");
static SERIAL: AtomicU64 = AtomicU64::new(0);

pub(crate) fn plugin_path(config_dir: &Path) -> PathBuf {
    config_dir.join("agent-integrations/opencode/ivy-opencode.tsx")
}

/// A JSON fragment for adding to, not replacing, OpenCode's existing plugin array.
pub(crate) fn snippet(config_dir: &Path) -> String {
    serde_json::to_string(&plugin_path(config_dir).to_string_lossy().as_ref())
        .expect("path serializes")
}

/// Install or repair the bundled asset; never edit the OpenCode configuration.
pub(crate) fn install(config_dir: &Path) -> io::Result<PathBuf> {
    let path = plugin_path(config_dir);
    let parent = path
        .parent()
        .ok_or_else(|| io::Error::other("plugin has no parent directory"))?;
    fs::create_dir_all(parent)?;
    match fs::symlink_metadata(&path) {
        Ok(m) if !m.is_file() || m.file_type().is_symlink() => {
            return Err(io::Error::other("plugin destination is not a regular file"));
        }
        Ok(_) => {}
        Err(e) if e.kind() == io::ErrorKind::NotFound => {}
        Err(e) => return Err(e),
    }
    let temporary = parent.join(format!(
        ".ivy-opencode-{}-{}.tmp",
        std::process::id(),
        SERIAL.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed)
    ));
    let result = (|| {
        let mut file = OpenOptions::new()
            .write(true)
            .create_new(true)
            .open(&temporary)?;
        file.write_all(ASSET.as_bytes())?;
        file.sync_all()?;
        fs::rename(&temporary, &path)?;
        Ok(path.clone())
    })();
    if result.is_err() {
        let _ = fs::remove_file(&temporary);
    }
    result
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    #[test]
    fn install_and_repair_touch_only_owned_asset() {
        let dir = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!(
            "ivy-plugin-test-{}-{}",
            std::process::id(),
            SERIAL.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed)
        ));
        fs::create_dir_all(&dir).unwrap();
        fs::write(dir.join("tui.json"), "existing").unwrap();
        let path = install(&dir).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(fs::read_to_string(&path).unwrap(), ASSET);
        fs::write(&path, "broken").unwrap();
        install(&dir).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(fs::read_to_string(&path).unwrap(), ASSET);
        assert_eq!(
            fs::read_to_string(dir.join("tui.json")).unwrap(),
            "existing"
        );
        assert_eq!(
            serde_json::from_str::<String>(&snippet(&dir)).unwrap(),
            path.to_string_lossy()
        );
        fs::remove_dir_all(dir).unwrap();
    }
    crate::windows_unix_omission!(
        refuses_symlink_destination,
        "unix-only: requires Unix symlinks to plant a competing destination"
    );
    #[cfg(unix)]
    #[test]
    fn refuses_symlink_destination() {
        use std::os::unix::fs::symlink;
        let dir = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!(
            "ivy-plugin-link-{}-{}",
            std::process::id(),
            SERIAL.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed)
        ));
        let path = plugin_path(&dir);
        fs::create_dir_all(path.parent().unwrap()).unwrap();
        let target = dir.join("target");
        fs::write(&target, "untouched").unwrap();
        symlink(&target, &path).unwrap();
        assert!(install(&dir).is_err());
        assert_eq!(fs::read_to_string(target).unwrap(), "untouched");
        fs::remove_dir_all(dir).unwrap();
    }
}
```
