### crates/app/src/ai_sessions/mod.rs

```
//! Explicit client declarations bound to one native foreground invocation.

#[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
mod programs;
#[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
mod protocol;
#[cfg(all(test, target_os = "linux"))]
mod tests;
#[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
mod verify;
#[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
mod worker;

use panel_control::session::{Identity, SessionHandler};
use std::path::PathBuf;
use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicBool, AtomicU64, Ordering};
use std::sync::{Arc, Condvar, Mutex};
use std::time::{Duration, Instant};
use term_core::ForegroundScope;

#[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
pub(crate) use programs::{ResolvedLaunch, resolve_launch};

#[cfg(not(target_os = "linux"))]
pub(crate) struct ResolvedLaunch {
    pub(crate) program: PathBuf,
    pub(crate) prefix_args: Vec<String>,
    pub(crate) path: std::ffi::OsString,
}

#[cfg(not(target_os = "linux"))]
pub(crate) fn resolve_launch(_: panel_control::session::Client) -> std::io::Result<ResolvedLaunch> {
    Err(std::io::ErrorKind::Unsupported.into())
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub(crate) enum Status {
    Disabled,
    Waiting,
    Registered,
    Unsupported,
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub(crate) struct Snapshot {
    pub(crate) identity: Option<Identity>,
    pub(crate) cwd: Option<PathBuf>,
    pub(crate) revision: u64,
    pub(crate) status: Status,
    pub(crate) sampled: bool,
}

pub(crate) struct Handle {
    shared: Arc<Shared>,
}

struct Shared {
    scope: ForegroundScope,
    enabled: AtomicBool,
    frozen: AtomicBool,
    close_acknowledged: Arc<AtomicBool>,
    epoch: AtomicU64,
    published: Mutex<Snapshot>,
    changed: Condvar,
    wake: Arc<dyn Fn() + Send + Sync>,
    #[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
    supplied: Option<Vec<(panel_control::session::Client, PathBuf)>>,
}

impl Handle {
    pub(crate) fn new(scope: ForegroundScope, wake: Arc<dyn Fn() + Send + Sync>) -> Self {
        Self::build(scope, wake, None)
    }

    #[cfg(all(test, target_os = "linux"))]
    pub(crate) fn with_programs(
        scope: ForegroundScope,
        wake: Arc<dyn Fn() + Send + Sync>,
        programs: Vec<(panel_control::session::Client, PathBuf)>,
    ) -> Self {
        Self::build(scope, wake, Some(programs))
    }

    fn build(
        scope: ForegroundScope,
        wake: Arc<dyn Fn() + Send + Sync>,
        supplied: Option<Vec<(panel_control::session::Client, PathBuf)>>,
    ) -> Self {
        #[cfg(not(target_os = "linux"))]
        let _ = supplied;
        Self {
            shared: Arc::new(Shared {
                scope,
                enabled: AtomicBool::new(false),
                frozen: AtomicBool::new(false),
                close_acknowledged: Arc::new(AtomicBool::new(false)),
                epoch: AtomicU64::new(0),
                published: Mutex::new(Snapshot {
                    identity: None,
                    cwd: None,
                    revision: 0,
                    status: Status::Disabled,
                    sampled: false,
                }),
                changed: Condvar::new(),
                wake,
                #[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
                supplied,
            }),
        }
    }

    pub(crate) fn callback(&self) -> SessionHandler {
        let weak = Arc::downgrade(&self.shared);
        Arc::new(move |peer, request| {
            let Some(shared) = weak.upgrade() else {
                return error("disabled");
            };
            if !shared.enabled.load(Ordering::Acquire) || !shared.scope.is_open() {
                return error("disabled");
            }
            #[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
            return worker::request(shared, peer, request);
            #[cfg(not(target_os = "linux"))]
            {
                let _ = (peer, request);
                error("unsupported")
            }
        })
    }

    pub(crate) fn set_enabled(&self, enabled: bool) {
        if self.shared.enabled.swap(enabled, Ordering::AcqRel) == enabled {
            return;
        }
        self.shared.epoch.fetch_add(1, Ordering::AcqRel);
        self.shared.publish(
            None,
            None,
            if enabled {
                Status::Waiting
            } else {
                Status::Disabled
            },
        );
        #[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
        worker::refresh(&self.shared);
    }

    pub(crate) fn snapshot(&self) -> Snapshot {
        self.shared
            .published
            .lock()
            .unwrap_or_else(|error| error.into_inner())
            .clone()
    }

    pub(crate) fn snapshot_if_changed(&self, revision: u64) -> Option<Snapshot> {
        let snapshot = self
            .shared
            .published
            .lock()
            .unwrap_or_else(|error| error.into_inner());
        (snapshot.revision != revision).then(|| snapshot.clone())
    }
}

impl Drop for Handle {
    fn drop(&mut self) {
        self.set_enabled(false);
        #[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
        worker::notify_existing();
    }
}

pub(crate) fn freeze_all(handles: &[&Handle], timeout: Duration) -> Vec<Snapshot> {
    let deadline = Instant::now() + timeout;
    for handle in handles {
        handle
            .shared
            .close_acknowledged
            .store(false, Ordering::Release);
    }
    #[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
    let tickets = worker::prepare_close(handles, deadline);
    #[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
    for (shared, token) in &tickets {
        let mut snapshot = shared
            .published
            .lock()
            .unwrap_or_else(|error| error.into_inner());
        while !token.load(Ordering::Acquire) && Instant::now() < deadline {
            let remaining = deadline.saturating_duration_since(Instant::now());
            snapshot = shared
                .changed
                .wait_timeout(snapshot, remaining)
                .unwrap_or_else(|error| error.into_inner())
                .0;
        }
    }
    #[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
    for handle in handles {
        handle.shared.frozen.store(true, Ordering::Release);
        let mut snapshot = handle
            .shared
            .published
            .lock()
            .unwrap_or_else(|error| error.into_inner());
        if !handle.shared.close_acknowledged.load(Ordering::Acquire)
            && (snapshot.identity.is_some() || snapshot.sampled)
        {
            snapshot.identity = None;
            snapshot.sampled = false;
            snapshot.revision = snapshot.revision.wrapping_add(1);
            snapshot.status = Status::Waiting;
        }
    }
    handles.iter().map(|handle| handle.snapshot()).collect()
}

impl Shared {
    fn publish(&self, identity: Option<Identity>, cwd: Option<PathBuf>, status: Status) {
        let mut snapshot = self
            .published
            .lock()
            .unwrap_or_else(|error| error.into_inner());
        if snapshot.identity == identity
            && snapshot.cwd == cwd
            && snapshot.status == status
            && !snapshot.sampled
        {
            return;
        }
        snapshot.identity = identity;
        snapshot.cwd = cwd;
        snapshot.status = status;
        snapshot.sampled = false;
        snapshot.revision = snapshot.revision.wrapping_add(1);
        drop(snapshot);
        self.changed.notify_all();
        (self.wake)();
    }
}

fn error(kind: &str) -> serde_json::Value {
    serde_json::json!({"ok":false,"error":kind})
}

#[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
pub(crate) fn reap_later() {
    worker::reap_later();
}

#[cfg(all(test, windows))]
mod tests {
    macro_rules! windows_unix_omission {
        ($name:ident, $reason:literal) => {
            #[test]
            #[ignore = $reason]
            fn $name() {}
        };
    }

    windows_unix_omission!(
        claude_null_directory_uses_the_verified_client_not_its_shell_or_helper,
        "unix-only: requires Linux process ancestry and Unix-domain client session sockets"
    );
    windows_unix_omission!(
        unavailable_claude_root_directory_revokes_the_verified_identity,
        "unix-only: requires Linux process ancestry and Unix-domain client session sockets"
    );
    windows_unix_omission!(
        native_session_generations_sequences_helpers_and_idle_probes,
        "unix-only: requires Linux process ancestry and Unix-domain client session sockets"
    );
    windows_unix_omission!(
        native_root_exit_invalidates_once_and_codex_keeps_last_accepted_report,
        "unix-only: requires Linux process exit observation and Unix-domain client session sockets"
    );
    windows_unix_omission!(
        native_close_uses_one_deadline_and_strips_unacknowledged_live_clients,
        "unix-only: requires Linux process lifetime observation and Unix-domain client session sockets"
    );
    windows_unix_omission!(
        native_close_accepts_only_a_post_probe_ack_and_retains_the_frozen_sample,
        "unix-only: requires Linux process lifetime observation and Unix-domain client session sockets"
    );
    windows_unix_omission!(
        native_session_endpoint_rejects_a_reporter_from_another_real_pane,
        "unix-only: requires Linux foreground-process identity and Unix-domain client session sockets"
    );
    windows_unix_omission!(
        detached_reporters_require_their_own_current_foreground_client_root,
        "unix-only: requires Linux process ancestry and Unix-domain client session sockets"
    );
    windows_unix_omission!(
        native_close_rejects_an_old_challenge_even_when_the_sequence_increases,
        "unix-only: requires Linux process lifetime observation and Unix-domain client session sockets"
    );
    windows_unix_omission!(
        installed_session_client_probe,
        "unix-only: requires Linux process ancestry and Unix-domain client session sockets"
    );

    const CLAUDE_AUTHORIZED_MESSAGE: &str =
        "Responde solo OK, sin usar herramientas ni modificar archivos";

    fn own_assistant_ok(bytes: &[u8]) -> bool {
        let mut plain = Vec::new();
        let mut index = 0;
        while index < bytes.len() {
            if bytes[index..].starts_with(b"\x1b[") {
                index += 2;
                while index < bytes.len() && !(0x40..=0x7e).contains(&bytes[index]) {
                    index += 1;
                }
                if index < bytes.len() && matches!(bytes[index], b'H' | b'f' | b'E' | b'F') {
                    plain.push(b'\n');
                }
            } else if bytes[index..].starts_with(b"\x1b]") {
                index += 2;
                while index < bytes.len()
                    && bytes[index] != 7
                    && !bytes[index..].starts_with(b"\x1b\\")
                {
                    index += 1;
                }
                if bytes.get(index) == Some(&27) {
                    index += 1;
                }
            } else {
                plain.push(if bytes[index] == b'\r' {
                    b'\n'
                } else {
                    bytes[index]
                });
            }
            index += 1;
        }
        let mut marker = false;
        for line in String::from_utf8_lossy(&plain).lines() {
            let line = line.trim();
            if let Some(body) = line.strip_prefix(['⏺', '●', '•']) {
                if body.trim() == "OK" {
                    return true;
                }
                marker = body.trim().is_empty();
            } else if marker && line == "OK" {
                return true;
            } else if !line.is_empty() {
                marker = false;
            }
        }
        false
    }

    #[test]
    fn single_claude_reply_classifier_requires_an_assistant_marker_not_the_prompt_echo() {
        assert!(!own_assistant_ok(
            format!("❯ {CLAUDE_AUTHORIZED_MESSAGE}\r\n").as_bytes()
        ));
        assert!(own_assistant_ok("\u{1b}[1m⏺\u{1b}[0m OK\r\n".as_bytes()));
        assert!(own_assistant_ok("●\u{1b}[3;1HOK\r\n".as_bytes()));
        assert!(!own_assistant_ok("❯ OK\r\n".as_bytes()));
    }

    #[cfg(windows)]
    mod codex_probe {
        macro_rules! windows_unix_omission {
            ($name:ident, $reason:literal) => {
                #[test]
                #[ignore = $reason]
                fn $name() {}
            };
        }

        windows_unix_omission!(
            installed_codex_hook_stdout_preflight,
            "unix-only: requires Linux Unix-domain client sockets and process identity"
        );
        windows_unix_omission!(
            installed_codex_hook_trust_preflight,
            "unix-only: requires Linux Unix-domain client sockets and process identity"
        );
        windows_unix_omission!(
            installed_codex_resume_only_status_diagnostic,
            "unix-only: requires Linux Unix-domain client sockets and process identity"
        );
        windows_unix_omission!(
            installed_codex_one_message_capture_and_exact_resume,
            "unix-only: requires Linux Unix-domain client sockets and process identity"
        );

        fn codex_accepts_hook_stdout(bytes: &[u8]) -> bool {
            if bytes.iter().all(u8::is_ascii_whitespace) {
                return true;
            }
            let Ok(serde_json::Value::Object(fields)) = serde_json::from_slice(bytes) else {
                return false;
            };
            fields.iter().all(|(key, value)| match key.as_str() {
                "continue" | "suppressOutput" => value.is_boolean(),
                "stopReason" | "systemMessage" => value.is_string() || value.is_null(),
                "hookSpecificOutput" => value.as_object().is_some_and(|output| {
                    output
                        .get("hookEventName")
                        .is_some_and(|event| event == "SessionStart")
                        && output.iter().all(|(key, value)| {
                            key == "hookEventName"
                                || key == "additionalContext"
                                    && (value.is_string() || value.is_null())
                        })
                }),
                _ => false,
            })
        }
        fn own_ok(plain: &str) -> bool {
            plain.lines().any(|line| {
                line.trim()
                    .strip_prefix('•')
                    .is_some_and(|body| body.trim() == "OK")
            })
        }
        #[test]
        fn codex_probe_filters_reply_schema_and_does_not_count_prompt_echo_as_ok() {
            assert!(!codex_accepts_hook_stdout(br#"{"ok":true}"#));
            assert!(codex_accepts_hook_stdout(b""));
            assert!(codex_accepts_hook_stdout(br#"{"continue":true}"#));
            assert!(!own_ok(
                "Responde solo OK, sin usar herramientas ni modificar archivos"
            ));
            assert!(!own_ok("› OK"));
            assert!(own_ok("• OK"));
        }
        #[test]
        fn codex_probe_readiness_requires_the_live_composer_cursor_not_history() {
            let mut screen = term_core::Screen::default();
            screen.push("\x1b[1;1H› old prompt\x1b[40;1H\x1b[?25h".as_bytes());
            assert!(!screen.composer_ready());
            screen.push("› ".as_bytes());
            assert!(screen.composer_ready());
            screen.push(b"\x1b[?25l");
            assert!(!screen.composer_ready());
        }
    }
}
```

### crates/app/src/client_attestation_windows_tests.rs

```
mod programs {
    mod tests {
        macro_rules! windows_unix_omission {
            ($name:ident) => {
                #[test]
                #[ignore = "unix-only: exercises Unix native client discovery and process identity"]
                fn $name() {}
            };
        }
        windows_unix_omission!(desktop_path_discovers_native_claude_in_its_user_installation);
        windows_unix_omission!(native_claude_version_symlink_is_discovered_with_colon_in_home);
        windows_unix_omission!(explicit_path_keeps_precedence_over_native_claude_installation);
        windows_unix_omission!(unsupported_path_client_does_not_fall_back_to_another_installation);
        windows_unix_omission!(native_claude_fallback_refuses_scripts_missing_or_relative_home);
        windows_unix_omission!(native_fallback_does_not_discover_other_clients_or_nonexecutables);
        windows_unix_omission!(trusted_path_discards_empty_and_relative_entries);
    }
    mod child {
        mod tests {
            macro_rules! windows_unix_omission {
                    ($name:ident) => {
                        #[test]
                        #[ignore = "unix-only: exercises Unix resolver-child lifetime, process groups and pipe EOF"]
                        fn $name() {}
                    };
                }
            windows_unix_omission!(volta_stdout_and_success_are_collected_under_one_deadline);
            windows_unix_omission!(volta_timeout_does_not_wait_after_kill_and_the_child_is_reaped);
            windows_unix_omission!(
                volta_descendant_holding_stdout_cannot_extend_the_exchange_deadline
            );
            windows_unix_omission!(
                resolver_cleanup_exhaustion_rejects_before_spawning_another_child
            );
        }
    }
}
```

### crates/app/src/clipboard.rs

```
//! Desktop reads retain their destination while a clipboard owner answers off the UI thread.
use eframe::egui;
use std::sync::{Arc, Mutex, mpsc};
use std::time::{Duration, Instant};

const DEADLINE: Duration = Duration::from_secs(2);

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub(crate) enum Source {
    Clipboard,
    Primary,
}

struct Read {
    pane: usize,
    source: Source,
}

struct Reply {
    pane: usize,
    text: Result<String, String>,
}

#[derive(Default)]
struct Mailbox {
    read: Option<Read>,
    primary: Option<(String, Instant)>,
}

struct Worker {
    requests: mpsc::SyncSender<()>,
    replies: mpsc::Receiver<Reply>,
    mailbox: Arc<Mutex<Mailbox>>,
    context: egui::Context,
}

struct Pending {
    deadline: Instant,
    expired: bool,
}

/// One outstanding read keeps late clipboard replies from becoming a different pane's paste.
#[derive(Default)]
pub(crate) struct Service {
    worker: Option<Worker>,
    pending: Option<Pending>,
}

impl Service {
    pub(crate) fn new(context: egui::Context) -> Self {
        let (requests, receiver) = mpsc::sync_channel(1);
        let (sender, replies) = mpsc::channel();
        let mailbox = Arc::new(Mutex::new(Mailbox::default()));
        let selection = mailbox.clone();
        let repaint = context.clone();
        match std::thread::Builder::new()
            .name("clipboard".into())
            .spawn(move || run(receiver, sender, selection, repaint))
        {
            Ok(thread) => {
                // Abandoned on purpose: an unresponsive clipboard owner must not freeze closing.
                // The backend owns its connections; it borrows nothing from the application.
                drop(thread);
                Self {
                    worker: Some(Worker {
                        requests,
                        replies,
                        mailbox,
                        context,
                    }),
                    pending: None,
                }
            }
            Err(error) => {
                eprintln!("Clipboard is unavailable: {error}");
                Self::default()
            }
        }
    }

    pub(crate) fn request(&mut self, pane: usize, source: Source, now: Instant) -> bool {
        if self.pending.is_some() {
            eprintln!("Clipboard is busy; this paste was not requested");
            return false;
        }
        let Some(worker) = &self.worker else {
            eprintln!("Clipboard is unavailable");
            return false;
        };
        let Ok(mut mailbox) = worker.mailbox.lock() else {
            eprintln!("Clipboard mailbox is unavailable");
            return false;
        };
        mailbox.read = Some(Read { pane, source });
        if matches!(
            worker.requests.try_send(()),
            Err(mpsc::TrySendError::Disconnected(()))
        ) {
            mailbox.read = None;
            eprintln!("Clipboard request could not be queued");
            return false;
        }
        drop(mailbox);
        self.pending = Some(Pending {
            deadline: now + DEADLINE,
            expired: false,
        });
        worker.context.request_repaint_after(DEADLINE);
        true
    }

    pub(crate) fn publish_primary(&mut self, text: String) {
        if !cfg!(target_os = "linux") || text.is_empty() {
            return;
        }
        if let Some(worker) = &self.worker {
            // The worker only takes this value under the lock; backend calls never hold it.
            if let Ok(mut mailbox) = worker.mailbox.lock() {
                mailbox.primary = Some((text, Instant::now() + DEADLINE));
                if matches!(
                    worker.requests.try_send(()),
                    Err(mpsc::TrySendError::Disconnected(()))
                ) {
                    eprintln!("Primary selection could not be queued");
                }
            }
        }
    }

    pub(crate) fn poll(&mut self, now: Instant) -> Option<(usize, String)> {
        let worker = self.worker.as_ref()?;
        match worker.replies.try_recv() {
            Ok(reply) => {
                let pending = self.pending.take()?;
                if pending.expired || now >= pending.deadline {
                    if !pending.expired {
                        eprintln!("Clipboard read timed out; its late reply was discarded");
                    }
                    return None;
                }
                match reply.text {
                    Ok(text) if !text.is_empty() => return Some((reply.pane, text)),
                    Ok(_) => {}
                    Err(error) => eprintln!("Clipboard read failed: {error}"),
                }
            }
            Err(mpsc::TryRecvError::Disconnected) => {
                eprintln!("Clipboard worker stopped");
                self.pending = None;
                self.worker = None;
            }
            Err(mpsc::TryRecvError::Empty) => {
                if let Some(pending) = &mut self.pending
                    && !pending.expired
                    && now >= pending.deadline
                {
                    pending.expired = true;
                    eprintln!(
                        "Clipboard read timed out; waiting for its owner before another read"
                    );
                }
            }
        }
        None
    }
}

pub(crate) fn deliver(window: &mut crate::Window, context: &egui::Context) {
    if window.clipboard.pending.is_none() {
        return;
    }
    let started = Instant::now();
    if let Some((name, text)) = window.clipboard.poll(Instant::now())
        && let Some(pane) = window.all_panes_mut().find(|pane| pane.name == name)
    {
        crate::keyboard::paste_into(pane, &text);
        context.request_repaint();
    }
    window.chrome.record_work(started.elapsed());
    window.timed_separately += window.chrome.take_cost();
}

fn run(
    requests: mpsc::Receiver<()>,
    replies: mpsc::Sender<Reply>,
    mailbox: Arc<Mutex<Mailbox>>,
    context: egui::Context,
) {
    let mut backend = None;
    let native_context = context.clone();
    run_with(requests, replies, mailbox, context, |read, selection| {
        // Capability/seat setup finishes before operations, without borrowing the app's window.
        let backend = backend.get_or_insert_with(Backend::new);
        if let Some(selection) = selection {
            match backend.as_mut() {
                Ok(backend) => {
                    let focused = native_context.input(|input| input.focused);
                    if let Err(error) =
                        publish_current_selection(selection, Instant::now(), focused, |text| {
                            backend.store_primary(text)
                        })
                    {
                        eprintln!("Primary selection could not be published: {error}");
                    }
                }
                Err(error) => eprintln!("Primary selection is unavailable: {error}"),
            }
        }
        read.map(|read| Reply {
            pane: read.pane,
            text: match backend.as_mut() {
                Ok(backend) => backend.load(read.source),
                Err(error) => Err(error.clone()),
            },
        })
    });
}

fn run_with(
    requests: mpsc::Receiver<()>,
    replies: mpsc::Sender<Reply>,
    mailbox: Arc<Mutex<Mailbox>>,
    context: egui::Context,
    mut operate: impl FnMut(Option<Read>, Option<(String, Instant)>) -> Option<Reply>,
) {
    loop {
        // Bounded: dropping Service closes the only wake sender and ends this idle receive.
        if requests.recv().is_err() {
            break;
        }
        // Backend calls never hold this lock, so the UI never waits for a clipboard owner.
        let (read, selection) = match mailbox.lock() {
            Ok(mut mailbox) => (mailbox.read.take(), mailbox.primary.take()),
            Err(_) => break,
        };
        if read.is_none() && selection.is_none() {
            continue;
        }
        if let Some(reply) = operate(read, selection) {
            if replies.send(reply).is_err() {
                break;
            }
            context.request_repaint();
        }
    }
}

// A blocked clipboard owner must not publish an old selection after the person left the window.
fn publish_current_selection(
    (text, deadline): (String, Instant),
    now: Instant,
    focused: bool,
    publish: impl FnOnce(String) -> Result<(), String>,
) -> Result<(), String> {
    if now >= deadline || !focused {
        return Ok(());
    }
    publish(text)
}

struct Backend(arboard::Clipboard);

impl Backend {
    fn new() -> Result<Self, String> {
        // On Linux arboard tries Wayland data-control, then X11/XWayland. Pure Wayland
        // without data-control or a reachable X server is explicitly unavailable.
        arboard::Clipboard::new()
            .map(Self)
            .map_err(|error| error.to_string())
    }

    fn load(&mut self, source: Source) -> Result<String, String> {
        #[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
        {
            use arboard::GetExtLinux;
            self.0
                .get()
                .clipboard(source.selection())
                .text()
                .map_err(|error| error.to_string())
        }
        #[cfg(not(target_os = "linux"))]
        match source {
            Source::Clipboard => self.0.get_text().map_err(|error| error.to_string()),
            Source::Primary => Err("primary selection is unsupported on this platform".into()),
        }
    }

    fn store_primary(&mut self, text: String) -> Result<(), String> {
        #[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
        {
            use arboard::SetExtLinux;
            self.0
                .set()
                .clipboard(Source::Primary.selection())
                .text(text)
                .map_err(|error| error.to_string())
        }
        #[cfg(not(target_os = "linux"))]
        {
            let _ = text;
            Err("primary selection is unsupported".into())
        }
    }
}

#[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
impl Source {
    fn selection(self) -> arboard::LinuxClipboardKind {
        match self {
            Self::Clipboard => arboard::LinuxClipboardKind::Clipboard,
            Self::Primary => arboard::LinuxClipboardKind::Primary,
        }
    }
}

/// Desktop-free peer for UI integration tests; it uses the real request and reply paths.
#[cfg(test)]
pub(crate) struct Harness {
    wake: mpsc::Receiver<()>,
    mailbox: Arc<Mutex<Mailbox>>,
    replies: mpsc::Sender<Reply>,
}

#[cfg(test)]
impl Harness {
    pub(crate) fn requested(&self) -> Option<(usize, Source)> {
        let _ = self.wake.try_recv();
        self.mailbox
            .lock()
            .unwrap()
            .read
            .take()
            .map(|read| (read.pane, read.source))
    }

    pub(crate) fn reply(&self, pane: usize, text: Result<String, String>) {
        self.replies.send(Reply { pane, text }).unwrap();
    }

    pub(crate) fn primary(&self) -> Option<String> {
        self.mailbox
            .lock()
            .unwrap()
            .primary
            .take()
            .map(|(text, _)| text)
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
impl Service {
    pub(crate) fn testing_blocked(
        context: egui::Context,
    ) -> (
        Self,
        mpsc::Receiver<()>,
        mpsc::Sender<()>,
        mpsc::Receiver<()>,
    ) {
        let (mut service, harness) = Self::testing();
        service.worker.as_mut().unwrap().context = context.clone();
        let (entered, entering) = mpsc::channel();
        let (release, released) = mpsc::channel();
        let (finished, finishing) = mpsc::channel();
        std::thread::spawn(move || {
            run_with(
                harness.wake,
                harness.replies,
                harness.mailbox,
                context,
                |read, _| {
                    read.map(|read| {
                        entered.send(()).unwrap();
                        let text = released
                            .recv_timeout(DEADLINE + DEADLINE)
                            .map(|()| "clipboard completion".into())
                            .map_err(|_| "test clipboard owner did not release".into());
                        finished.send(()).unwrap();
                        Reply {
                            pane: read.pane,
                            text,
                        }
                    })
                },
            );
        });
        (service, entering, release, finishing)
    }

    pub(crate) fn testing() -> (Self, Harness) {
        let (requests, wake) = mpsc::sync_channel(1);
        let (replies, delivered) = mpsc::channel();
        let mailbox = Arc::new(Mutex::new(Mailbox::default()));
        (
            Self {
                worker: Some(Worker {
                    requests,
                    replies: delivered,
                    mailbox: mailbox.clone(),
                    context: egui::Context::default(),
                }),
                pending: None,
            },
            Harness {
                wake,
                mailbox,
                replies,
            },
        )
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    struct Requests {
        wake: mpsc::Receiver<()>,
        mailbox: Arc<Mutex<Mailbox>>,
    }

    impl Requests {
        fn try_recv(&self) -> Result<Read, ()> {
            let _ = self.wake.try_recv();
            self.mailbox.lock().unwrap().read.take().ok_or(())
        }
    }

    fn connected() -> (Service, Requests, mpsc::Sender<Reply>) {
        let (requests, received) = mpsc::sync_channel(1);
        let (replies, delivered) = mpsc::channel();
        let mailbox = Arc::new(Mutex::new(Mailbox::default()));
        let service = Service {
            worker: Some(Worker {
                requests,
                replies: delivered,
                mailbox: mailbox.clone(),
                context: egui::Context::default(),
            }),
            pending: None,
        };
        (
            service,
            Requests {
                wake: received,
                mailbox,
            },
            replies,
        )
    }

    crate::windows_unix_omission!(
        first_primary_operation_selects_primary_without_a_clipboard_fallback,
        "unix-only: exercises Linux primary-selection clipboard API semantics"
    );

    #[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
    #[test]
    fn first_primary_operation_selects_primary_without_a_clipboard_fallback() {
        assert!(matches!(
            Source::Primary.selection(),
            arboard::LinuxClipboardKind::Primary
        ));
        assert!(matches!(
            Source::Clipboard.selection(),
            arboard::LinuxClipboardKind::Clipboard
        ));
        let (mut service, requests, _replies) = connected();
        assert!(service.request(21, Source::Primary, Instant::now()));
        let first = requests.try_recv().unwrap();
        assert_eq!(first.pane, 21);
        assert!(matches!(
            first.source.selection(),
            arboard::LinuxClipboardKind::Primary
        ));
    }

    #[test]
    fn delayed_or_unfocused_primary_never_reaches_the_desktop_writer() {
        let now = Instant::now();
        for (deadline, focused) in [(now, true), (now + DEADLINE, false)] {
            publish_current_selection(("old selection".into(), deadline), now, focused, |_| {
                panic!("stale selection reached the desktop writer")
            })
            .unwrap();
        }
        let mut written = None;
        publish_current_selection(("current".into(), now + DEADLINE), now, true, |text| {
            written = Some(text);
            Ok(())
        })
        .unwrap();
        assert_eq!(written.as_deref(), Some("current"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn default_is_unavailable_without_opening_a_display() {
        let mut service = Service::default();
        assert!(!service.request(2, Source::Clipboard, Instant::now()));
        assert!(service.poll(Instant::now()).is_none());
    }

    #[test]
    fn delayed_reply_keeps_the_original_pane_and_clipboard_kind() {
        let (mut service, requests, replies) = connected();
        let now = Instant::now();
        assert!(service.request(17, Source::Primary, now));
        let read = requests.try_recv().unwrap();
        assert_eq!(read.pane, 17);
        assert_eq!(read.source, Source::Primary);
        assert!(service.poll(now).is_none());
        replies
            .send(Reply {
                pane: read.pane,
                text: Ok("selected".into()),
            })
            .unwrap();
        assert_eq!(
            service.poll(now + Duration::from_millis(10)),
            Some((17, "selected".into()))
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn timeout_does_not_release_a_busy_backend_or_deliver_its_late_text() {
        let (mut service, requests, replies) = connected();
        let now = Instant::now();
        assert!(service.request(3, Source::Clipboard, now));
        let read = requests.try_recv().unwrap();
        assert!(service.poll(now + DEADLINE).is_none());
        assert!(!service.request(8, Source::Primary, now + DEADLINE));
        assert!(requests.try_recv().is_err());
        replies
            .send(Reply {
                pane: read.pane,
                text: Ok("stale".into()),
            })
            .unwrap();
        assert!(service.poll(now + DEADLINE).is_none());
        assert!(service.request(8, Source::Primary, now + DEADLINE));
        assert_eq!(requests.try_recv().unwrap().pane, 8);
    }

    #[test]
    fn deadline_applies_even_if_no_frame_polled_while_the_owner_was_busy() {
        let (mut service, requests, replies) = connected();
        let now = Instant::now();
        assert!(service.request(1, Source::Clipboard, now));
        let read = requests.try_recv().unwrap();
        replies
            .send(Reply {
                pane: read.pane,
                text: Ok("late".into()),
            })
            .unwrap();
        assert!(service.poll(now + DEADLINE).is_none());
    }

    #[test]
    fn only_one_read_is_queued_and_errors_release_it() {
        let (mut service, requests, replies) = connected();
        let now = Instant::now();
        assert!(service.request(4, Source::Clipboard, now));
        assert!(!service.request(5, Source::Primary, now));
        let read = requests.try_recv().unwrap();
        assert_eq!(read.source, Source::Clipboard);
        assert!(requests.try_recv().is_err());
        replies
            .send(Reply {
                pane: read.pane,
                text: Err("empty owner".into()),
            })
            .unwrap();
        assert!(service.poll(now).is_none());
        assert!(service.request(5, Source::Primary, now));
    }

    crate::windows_unix_omission!(
        primary_publication_coalesces_without_issuing_an_ordinary_clipboard_command,
        "unix-only: exercises Linux primary-selection clipboard API semantics"
    );

    #[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
    #[test]
    fn primary_publication_coalesces_without_issuing_an_ordinary_clipboard_command() {
        let (mut service, requests, _replies) = connected();
        service.publish_primary("first".into());
        service.publish_primary("latest".into());
        service.publish_primary(String::new());
        assert_eq!(
            service
                .worker
                .as_ref()
                .unwrap()
                .mailbox
                .lock()
                .unwrap()
                .primary
                .as_ref()
                .map(|(text, _)| text.as_str()),
            Some("latest")
        );
        assert!(requests.try_recv().is_err());
    }

    #[test]
    fn unavailable_primary_does_not_fall_back_to_the_ordinary_clipboard() {
        let (mut service, requests, replies) = connected();
        let now = Instant::now();
        assert!(service.request(9, Source::Primary, now));
        let read = requests.try_recv().unwrap();
        assert_eq!(read.source, Source::Primary);
        replies
            .send(Reply {
                pane: read.pane,
                text: Err("primary selection is unsupported".into()),
            })
            .unwrap();
        assert!(service.poll(now).is_none());
        assert!(requests.try_recv().is_err());
        assert!(service.pending.is_none());
    }

    #[test]
    fn worker_disconnection_disables_future_requests() {
        let (mut service, requests, replies) = connected();
        let now = Instant::now();
        drop(replies);
        assert!(service.poll(now).is_none());
        assert!(!service.request(1, Source::Clipboard, now));
        assert!(requests.try_recv().is_err());
    }
}
```

### crates/app/src/header/splitting.rs

```
//! Pane creation shared by shortcuts and the header controls.

use crate::painter::CellMetrics;
use crate::tiling::{Orientation, Side, can_split};
use crate::{Pane, Window};
use eframe::egui;
use std::time::Instant;

impl Window {
    pub(crate) fn split_the_focused(
        &mut self,
        cut_along: Orientation,
        ctx: &egui::Context,
        whole_window: egui::Rect,
        metrics: &CellMetrics,
    ) {
        let before = Instant::now();
        #[cfg(test)]
        let before = crate::tests::OperationClock::start(before);
        split(
            self,
            self.active().focus,
            cut_along,
            ctx,
            whole_window,
            metrics,
        );
        self.timed_separately += before.elapsed();
    }

    #[cfg(test)]
    pub(crate) fn graft_the_new_one(
        &mut self,
        opened: term_core::Result<Pane>,
        cut_along: Orientation,
        ctx: &egui::Context,
    ) {
        graft(self, self.active().focus, opened, cut_along, ctx);
    }
}

/// Splits the named pane and gives focus to the newborn only after a successful graft.
///
/// **Focus passes to the new one because splitting is opening a console**, and whoever opens a
/// console wants to write in it; it is the same thing creating a tab does by putting it in
/// front. Staying on the old one would force a click to use what has just been asked for.
///
/// **If it does not fit, nothing is split and nothing is spent**: not a name, not a `fork`. A
/// child below the minimum cannot be used, so it is refused before launching a program.
///
/// **What it costs is declared in full**, and they are two things no pane is going to declare:
/// the tree walk to find the target, and the new program's `fork`/`exec`.
/// Without this, the frame's cost is shared out by subtraction and both would end up published
/// as interface framework cost — the figure D3 judges it with. The shortcut times this call;
/// a header press is already inside the mouse stopwatch and must not subtract it twice.
pub(crate) fn split(
    window: &mut Window,
    target: usize,
    cut_along: Orientation,
    ctx: &egui::Context,
    whole_window: egui::Rect,
    metrics: &CellMetrics,
) {
    // The bar is set aside with the tabs there are **now**: if the same batch of events has
    // just created one, the height left for the consoles is already a different one.
    let area = crate::tab_bar::geometry(window, whole_window, metrics).consoles;
    let target_area = window
        .active()
        .panes()
        .iter()
        .position(|pane| pane.name == target)
        .map(|at| {
            // The leaves and their rectangles are matched by position: the two walks go in the same
            // order, which is the invariant the whole painting already rests on.
            window.active().root.place(area, metrics.width()).parts[at]
        });

    // A target removed before its queued action is applied cannot open a replacement console.
    let Some(its_area) = target_area else {
        return;
    };
    if !can_split(its_area, cut_along, metrics, window.headers) {
        // **It is said out loud, because the chord has swallowed the key and nothing has
        // happened.** Without this line, in a narrow window the shortcut reads as broken: it
        // neither splits, nor reaches the program inside, nor leaves a trace of why. It is the
        // same as the sibling path already does —the console that could not be opened—, and for
        // now it is all that can be done: saying it on screen would need a way of warning that
        // this phase does not have.
        eprintln!(
            "it is not split: the pane cannot give both children their minimum. Make the window bigger \
                 or move the divider"
        );
        return;
    }

    let name = window.next_pane_name;
    let directory = window
        .active_mut()
        .panes_mut()
        .into_iter()
        .find(|pane| pane.name == target)
        .and_then(|pane| {
            // A remote/invalid report must not turn into the local ssh process's directory.
            if pane.screen.working_directory_reported() && pane.screen.working_directory().is_none()
            {
                return None;
            }
            // Native discovery sees a cd even before a shell publishes its next OSC 7 report.
            pane.pty.working_directory().or_else(|| {
                pane.screen
                    .working_directory()
                    .and_then(published_directory)
            })
        });
    if directory.is_none() {
        eprintln!(
            "the console's working directory is unavailable; splitting in the default directory"
        );
    }
    let opened = crate::opening::new_in_directory(
        ctx,
        name,
        window.control.read_activity(),
        directory.as_deref(),
    );
    // **The name is spent even if the program never gets to start**, just as when opening a
    // tab: whatever is opened afterwards must not be called the same as what never got to
    // exist, because the report may already have named the failed attempt on the error output.
    window.next_pane_name += 1;

    // **The stopwatch is not closed before the graft, and that line was drawn in the wrong
    // place.** Putting the leaf into the tree and rearming the watcher are the two
    // **expensive** parts of splitting —the second rebuilds the watcher over the panes of every
    // tab and makes them reread their `panel.json`—, and they happen inside the interface pass
    // without any pane declaring them. Closing it here, that work fell into «the rest of the
    // frame», which is what this project calls the interface framework cost: the figure D3
    // decides with.
    graft(window, target, opened, cut_along, ctx);
}

/// Puts the console that has just been opened next to its target, or leaves the window as
/// it was if it could not be opened.
///
/// **It receives the attempt already made instead of making it**, just like
/// [`Window::bring_to_front`] with the tabs and for the same reason: opening a console only
/// fails when the system gives no more pseudoterminals or descriptors, and that cannot be
/// provoked from a test without touching the whole process. Splitting it here is what makes it
/// possible to assert what the case promises — that a failure leaves no half pane, does not
/// move the focus and rearms nothing.
fn graft(
    window: &mut Window,
    target: usize,
    opened: term_core::Result<Pane>,
    cut_along: Orientation,
    ctx: &egui::Context,
) {
    let new = match opened {
        Ok(new) => new,
        Err(failure) => {
            eprintln!("the pane could not be split ({failure})");
            return;
        }
    };

    // **The name focus will go to is taken from the console being grafted**, and not from a
    // parameter saying which one it is. The two would be worth the same as long as the caller
    // took them from the same place, but the day they come apart the focus would point at a
    // pane that does not exist: the keys would reach no console at all —they would be lost in
    // silence—, the highlight would not be painted, and the next splitting chord would find
    // nothing to split.
    let name = new.name;
    let tab = window.active_mut();
    if let Some(leftover) = tab.root.split(target, Side::behind(cut_along), new) {
        // **It cannot happen**: the caller has just found the target among the leaves of this
        // very tab, and nothing has touched the tree in between. It is said out loud instead of
        // kept quiet because the console really did get opened, and on dropping it here its
        // program dies and its directory disappears: without this line, a shell opened and
        // closed in the same frame would leave no trace at all.
        drop(leftover);
        eprintln!("the pane was not found when splitting; nothing has been split");
        return;
    }

    tab.focus = name;
    // Without rearming, the watcher knows nothing about the directory of the one that has just
    // been born and its panel would stay frozen with nothing saying so. It is the same as
    // opening a tab does.
    window.rearm_the_watcher(ctx);
}

/// OSC 7 uses a URI path; Windows drive paths arrive with a leading slash.
fn published_directory(path: &std::path::Path) -> Option<std::path::PathBuf> {
    #[cfg(windows)]
    {
        let path = path.to_str()?;
        Some(std::path::PathBuf::from(
            path.strip_prefix('/').unwrap_or(path),
        ))
    }
    #[cfg(not(windows))]
    {
        Some(path.to_path_buf())
    }
}

#[cfg(all(test, unix))]
mod tests {
    //! The newborn shell writes pwd to a file: terminal echo cannot forge the assertion.
    use super::*;
    use crate::tests::window_of_tabs;
    use std::{fs, path::PathBuf, time::Duration};

    struct Sandbox(PathBuf);
    impl Sandbox {
        fn new() -> Self {
            let mut nonce = [0u8; 8];
            getrandom::fill(&mut nonce).unwrap();
            let path =
                std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("ivy-split-{}", u64::from_ne_bytes(nonce)));
            fs::create_dir(&path).unwrap();
            Self(path)
        }
        fn directory(&self, name: &str) -> PathBuf {
            let path = self.0.join(name);
            fs::create_dir(&path).unwrap();
            path
        }
    }
    impl Drop for Sandbox {
        fn drop(&mut self) {
            let _ = fs::remove_dir_all(&self.0);
        }
    }
    fn wait(mut ready: impl FnMut() -> bool) {
        let deadline = Instant::now() + Duration::from_secs(5);
        while !ready() {
            assert!(Instant::now() < deadline, "directory operation timed out");
            std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(5));
        }
    }
    fn area() -> egui::Rect {
        egui::Rect::from_min_size(egui::Pos2::ZERO, egui::vec2(1600.0, 900.0))
    }
    fn assert_newborn_pwd(window: &mut Window, sandbox: &Sandbox, expected: &std::path::Path) {
        let output = sandbox.0.join("pwd.txt");
        let name = window.active().focus;
        window
            .all_panes_mut()
            .find(|p| p.name == name)
            .unwrap()
            .pty
            .write(format!("command pwd > '{}'\n", output.display()).as_bytes())
            .unwrap();
        wait(|| {
            fs::read_to_string(&output).is_ok_and(|s| s.trim_end() == expected.to_string_lossy())
        });
    }

    #[test]
    #[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
    fn split_directory_tracks_cd_for_both_orientations() {
        for direction in [Orientation::Columns, Orientation::Rows] {
            let sandbox = Sandbox::new();
            let first = sandbox.directory("first");
            let second = sandbox.directory("second space;$dollar");
            let ready = sandbox.0.join("ready");
            let change = sandbox.0.join("change");
            let changed = sandbox.0.join("changed");
            let script = format!(
                "cd '{}' && touch '{}' && while ! test -f '{}'; do sleep 0.01; done; cd '{}' && touch '{}' && exec sleep 30",
                first.display(),
                ready.display(),
                change.display(),
                second.display(),
                changed.display()
            );
            let mut window = window_of_tabs([[script.as_str(), "sleep 30"]]);
            wait(|| ready.exists());
            let original = std::env::current_dir().unwrap();
            // A stale, valid local report must not override the live cd on Linux.
            window.active_mut().panes_mut()[0]
                .screen
                .advance(format!("\x1b]7;file://{}\x07", first.display()).as_bytes());
            fs::write(&change, "").unwrap();
            wait(|| changed.exists());
            let ctx = egui::Context::default();
            window.split_the_focused(direction, &ctx, area(), &CellMetrics::of(8.0, 16.0));
            assert_eq!(window.active().panes().len(), 3);
            assert_newborn_pwd(&mut window, &sandbox, &second);
            assert_eq!(std::env::current_dir().unwrap(), original);
        }
    }

    #[test]
    #[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
    fn split_directory_uses_named_toolbar_target_instead_of_focus() {
        let sandbox = Sandbox::new();
        let source = sandbox.directory("clicked console");
        let ready = sandbox.0.join("ready");
        let script = format!(
            "cd '{}' && touch '{}' && exec sleep 30",
            source.display(),
            ready.display()
        );
        let mut window = window_of_tabs([["sleep 30", script.as_str()]]);
        wait(|| ready.exists());
        let target = window.active().panes()[1].name;
        assert_ne!(window.active().focus, target);
        split(
            &mut window,
            target,
            Orientation::Rows,
            &egui::Context::default(),
            area(),
            &CellMetrics::of(8.0, 16.0),
        );
        assert_eq!(window.active().panes().len(), 3);
        assert_newborn_pwd(&mut window, &sandbox, &source);
    }

    #[test]
    fn split_directory_osc_report_survives_source_exit_and_deleted_path_refuses_split() {
        let sandbox = Sandbox::new();
        let source = sandbox.directory("published directory");
        let mut window = window_of_tabs([["exit 0", "sleep 30"]]);
        wait(|| {
            window.active_mut().panes_mut()[0]
                .pty
                .working_directory()
                .is_none()
        });
        window.active_mut().panes_mut()[0]
            .screen
            .advance(format!("\x1b]7;file://{}\x07", source.display()).as_bytes());
        let target = window.active().focus;
        split(
            &mut window,
            target,
            Orientation::Columns,
            &egui::Context::default(),
            area(),
            &CellMetrics::of(8.0, 16.0),
        );
        assert_eq!(window.active().panes().len(), 3);
        assert_newborn_pwd(&mut window, &sandbox, &source);
        fs::remove_dir(&source).unwrap();
        let before = window.active().focus;
        split(
            &mut window,
            target,
            Orientation::Rows,
            &egui::Context::default(),
            area(),
            &CellMetrics::of(8.0, 16.0),
        );
        assert_eq!(window.active().panes().len(), 3);
        assert_eq!(window.active().focus, before);
    }

    #[test]
    fn split_directory_unknown_after_source_exit_uses_ordinary_launch_directory() {
        let sandbox = Sandbox::new();
        let output = sandbox.0.join("ordinary-pwd.txt");
        let ctx = egui::Context::default();
        // Observe the ordinary user-shell launch, including its own startup configuration.
        let mut ordinary =
            Pane::new_counting_reads(&ctx, 90, crate::control::ReadActivity::default()).unwrap();
        ordinary
            .pty
            .write(format!("command pwd > '{}'\n", output.display()).as_bytes())
            .unwrap();
        wait(|| fs::read_to_string(&output).is_ok_and(|s| !s.trim().is_empty()));
        let expected = PathBuf::from(fs::read_to_string(&output).unwrap().trim_end());
        let mut window = window_of_tabs([["exit 0", "sleep 30"]]);
        wait(|| {
            window.active_mut().panes_mut()[0]
                .pty
                .working_directory()
                .is_none()
        });
        assert!(
            !window.active().panes()[0]
                .screen
                .working_directory_reported()
        );
        window.split_the_focused(Orientation::Rows, &ctx, area(), &CellMetrics::of(8.0, 16.0));
        assert_eq!(window.active().panes().len(), 3);
        assert_newborn_pwd(&mut window, &sandbox, &expected);
    }

    #[test]
    fn split_directory_fallback_shell_receives_the_same_directory() {
        let sandbox = Sandbox::new();
        let source = sandbox.directory("fallback directory");
        let pane = crate::opening::launch_pane_in(
            &egui::Context::default(),
            "/no-such-ivy-shell",
            &[],
            None,
            0,
            crate::control::ReadActivity::default(),
            Some(&source),
        )
        .unwrap();
        let tab = crate::Tab::with_panes(
            vec![pane],
            &crate::OpeningLayout {
                columns: 1,
                rows: 1,
            },
            0,
        );
        let mut window = Window::with_these_tabs(
            &egui::Context::default(),
            vec![tab],
            None,
            crate::control::WindowActivity::default(),
        )
        .unwrap();
        assert_newborn_pwd(&mut window, &sandbox, &source);
    }

    #[test]
    #[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
    fn split_directory_foreign_report_vetoes_native_and_previous_local_directory() {
        let sandbox = Sandbox::new();
        let source = sandbox.directory("local ssh directory");
        let ready = sandbox.0.join("ready");
        let script = format!(
            "cd '{}' && touch '{}' && exec sleep 30",
            source.display(),
            ready.display()
        );
        let mut window = window_of_tabs([[script.as_str(), "sleep 30"]]);
        wait(|| ready.exists());
        window.active_mut().panes_mut()[0].screen.advance(
            format!(
                "\x1b]7;file://{}\x07\x1b]7;file://remote.invalid/some/project\x07",
                source.display()
            )
            .as_bytes(),
        );
        window.split_the_focused(
            Orientation::Columns,
            &egui::Context::default(),
            area(),
            &CellMetrics::of(8.0, 16.0),
        );
        assert_eq!(window.active().panes().len(), 3);
        let output = sandbox.0.join("remote-pwd.txt");
        let name = window.active().focus;
        window
            .all_panes_mut()
            .find(|p| p.name == name)
            .unwrap()
            .pty
            .write(format!("command pwd > '{}'\n", output.display()).as_bytes())
            .unwrap();
        wait(|| fs::read_to_string(&output).is_ok_and(|s| !s.trim().is_empty()));
        let actual = fs::read_to_string(&output).unwrap();
        assert_ne!(actual.trim_end(), source.to_string_lossy());
        assert_ne!(actual.trim_end(), "/some/project");
    }
}

#[cfg(all(test, windows))]
mod windows_tests {
    #[test]
    fn drive_uri_directory_becomes_an_absolute_native_path() {
        let path =
            super::published_directory(std::path::Path::new("/C:/work/project one")).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(path, std::path::PathBuf::from("C:/work/project one"));
        assert!(path.is_absolute());
    }
}

#[cfg(all(test, windows))]
mod tests {
    macro_rules! windows_unix_omission {
        ($name:ident) => {
            #[test]
            #[ignore = "unix-only: requires Unix shell directory reports and Linux-native split cwd resolution"]
            fn $name() {}
        };
    }

    windows_unix_omission!(split_directory_tracks_cd_for_both_orientations);
    windows_unix_omission!(split_directory_uses_named_toolbar_target_instead_of_focus);
    windows_unix_omission!(
        split_directory_osc_report_survives_source_exit_and_deleted_path_refuses_split
    );
    windows_unix_omission!(
        split_directory_unknown_after_source_exit_uses_ordinary_launch_directory
    );
    windows_unix_omission!(split_directory_fallback_shell_receives_the_same_directory);
    windows_unix_omission!(
        split_directory_foreign_report_vetoes_native_and_previous_local_directory
    );
}
```

```
use super::*;
use std::time::{Duration, Instant};

fn pending() -> (Process, Sender<term_core::Result<Ready>>) {
    let (sender, result) = channel();
    let (output_open, _) = channel();
    (
        Process {
            live: None,
            terminal: term_core::Termination::Pending,
            cost: Duration::ZERO,
            context: None,
            pending: Some(Pending {
                result,
                _output_open: output_open,
                reads: Default::default(),
            }),
            input: vec![],
            startup: 0,
            input_since: None,
            refused: false,
            refusals: 0,
            failure: None,
            delivery_failed: false,
            dismissed: false,
        },
        sender,
    )
}

crate::windows_unix_omission!(
    pending_input_and_latest_geometry_reach_the_real_program_in_order,
    "unix-only: runs /bin/sh and stty to verify queued input and PTY geometry"
);
#[cfg(unix)]
#[test]
fn pending_input_and_latest_geometry_reach_the_real_program_in_order() {
    let (mut process, complete) = pending();
    process.write(b"first ").unwrap();
    process.write(b"second\n").unwrap();
    process.resize(Size { rows: 31, cols: 91 }).unwrap();
    let child = Pty::spawn(
        "/bin/sh",
        &[
            "-c",
            "read -r line; printf 'RECEIVED:%s:' \"$line\"; stty size",
        ],
        Size::default(),
    )
    .unwrap();
    complete.send(Ok(prepare(child))).unwrap();
    let (output, _, _) = process.poll().expect("ready launch");
    process.resize(Size { rows: 31, cols: 91 }).unwrap();
    let input = std::mem::take(&mut process.input);
    process.live.as_mut().unwrap().write(&input).unwrap();
    let deadline = Instant::now() + Duration::from_secs(3);
    let mut text = Vec::new();
    while Instant::now() < deadline {
        if let Ok(bytes) = output.recv_timeout(Duration::from_millis(20)) {
            text.extend(bytes);
        }
        if String::from_utf8_lossy(&text).contains("RECEIVED:first second:31 91") {
            return;
        }
    }
    panic!(
        "queued input/resize not delivered: {}",
        String::from_utf8_lossy(&text)
    );
}

// Probes the child with a Unix signal; the Windows suite is #162.
crate::windows_unix_omission!(
    closing_a_pending_launch_drops_the_late_child_without_resurrecting_the_console,
    "unix-only: requires Unix process signals"
);
#[cfg(unix)]
#[test]
fn closing_a_pending_launch_drops_the_late_child_without_resurrecting_the_console() {
    let (process, complete) = pending();
    drop(process);
    let (pty, output) = Pty::spawn(
        "/bin/sh",
        &["-c", "printf '%s\\n' $$; exec sleep 30"],
        Size::default(),
    )
    .unwrap();
    let bytes = output.recv_timeout(Duration::from_secs(3)).unwrap();
    let pid: i32 = String::from_utf8_lossy(&bytes).trim().parse().unwrap();
    drop(complete.send(Ok(prepare((pty, output)))));
    let deadline = Instant::now() + Duration::from_secs(3);
    while unsafe { libc::kill(pid, 0) } == 0 && Instant::now() < deadline {
        std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(2));
    }
    assert_eq!(
        unsafe { libc::kill(pid, 0) },
        -1,
        "cancelled child's process survived"
    );
}

#[test]
fn many_stalled_launches_accept_input_without_waiting_for_the_workers() {
    let mut panes: Vec<_> = (0..8).map(|_| pending()).collect();
    for _ in 0..3 {
        for (process, _) in &mut panes {
            assert!(process.poll().is_none());
            process.write(b"input").unwrap();
            assert!(process.keeps_open());
        }
    }
    for (process, complete) in &mut panes {
        complete.send(Err("fixture launch failure".into())).unwrap();
        assert!(process.poll().is_none());
        assert_eq!(
            process.notice(),
            Some("This console's program could not be started.")
        );
        assert!(process.keeps_open());
    }
}

crate::windows_unix_omission!(
    a_real_signal_retains_output_and_has_a_dismissible_truthful_notice,
    "unix-only: sends a Unix signal to a POSIX shell and checks signal termination"
);
#[cfg(unix)]
#[test]
fn a_real_signal_retains_output_and_has_a_dismissible_truthful_notice() {
    let mut pane = crate::Pane::with_panel(
        &Default::default(),
        "/bin/sh",
        &[
            "-c".into(),
            "printf 'retained-output'; kill -KILL $$".into(),
        ],
        None,
        0,
    )
    .unwrap();
    let deadline = Instant::now() + Duration::from_secs(3);
    while Instant::now() < deadline {
        pane.pump(Default::default());
        let text: String = pane
            .screen
            .view()
            .cells()
            .iter()
            .map(|cell| cell.c)
            .collect();
        // Process exit and delivery through the reader's bridge are independent observations.
        if pane.pty.termination() == term_core::Termination::Signal
            && text.contains("retained-output")
        {
            break;
        }
        std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(2));
    }
    pane.pump(Default::default());
    assert_eq!(pane.pty.termination(), term_core::Termination::Signal);
    assert_eq!(
        pane.pty.notice(),
        Some("This console was terminated by a signal.")
    );
    assert!(!pane.is_finished(false));
    // The backend's OOM evidence uses this same notice path; signal alone above never earns it.
    pane.pty.terminal = term_core::Termination::Oom;
    pane.pty.delivery_failed = true;
    assert_eq!(
        pane.pty.notice(),
        Some("This console was stopped because of memory pressure.")
    );
    assert_eq!(
        crate::painter::notices::what_the_silence_is(&pane),
        Some(crate::painter::notices::Silence::Termination)
    );
    let before = format!("{:?}", pane.screen.view());
    let text: String = pane
        .screen
        .view()
        .cells()
        .iter()
        .map(|cell| cell.c)
        .collect();
    assert!(text.contains("retained-output"), "{text}");
    pane.pty.dismissed = true;
    assert!(pane.pty.notice().is_none());
    assert_eq!(before, format!("{:?}", pane.screen.view()));
    assert!(!pane.is_finished(false));
}

#[test]
fn pending_input_backpressure_refuses_a_retry_and_keeps_one_large_paste_whole() {
    let (mut process, _complete) = pending();
    let paste = vec![b'x'; 300 * 1024];
    process.write(&paste).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(process.backlog().unwrap().bytes, paste.len());
    assert!(process.write(b"retry").is_err());
    let backlog = process.backlog().unwrap();
    assert!(backlog.refused);
    assert_eq!(backlog.refusals, 1);
    assert_eq!(process.input, paste);
}

#[test]
fn the_real_window_accepts_a_new_tab_while_existing_launches_are_stalled() {
    use eframe::egui;
    let ctx = egui::Context::default();
    let mut window =
        crate::Window::with_tabs(&ctx, 1, crate::OpeningLayout::DEFAULT, None).unwrap();
    let mut keep_pending = Vec::new();
    for pane in window.all_panes_mut() {
        let (release, held) = channel();
        let waker = ctx.clone();
        let (process, output) = start_worker(
            std::sync::Arc::new(move || waker.request_repaint()),
            Default::default(),
            move || {
                held.recv_timeout(Duration::from_secs(2))?;
                Pty::spawn(
                    crate::shell_integration::fallback_shell(),
                    &[],
                    Size::default(),
                )
            },
        )
        .unwrap();
        pane.pty = process;
        pane.pty_output = crate::pty_output::PtyOutput::new(output, || {});
        keep_pending.push(release);
    }
    let mut frame = eframe::Frame::_new_kittest();
    let modifiers = egui::Modifiers::CTRL | egui::Modifiers::SHIFT;
    let input = egui::RawInput {
        screen_rect: Some(egui::Rect::from_min_size(
            egui::Pos2::ZERO,
            egui::vec2(1280.0, 800.0),
        )),
        events: vec![
            egui::Event::ModifiersChanged(modifiers),
            egui::Event::Key {
                key: egui::Key::T,
                physical_key: None,
                pressed: true,
                repeat: false,
                modifiers,
            },
        ],
        ..Default::default()
    };
    let output = ctx.run_ui(input, |ui| eframe::App::ui(&mut window, ui, &mut frame));
    output.drop_without_applying_deltas();
    assert_eq!(
        window.tabs.len(),
        2,
        "the window did not handle its shortcut while launches were pending"
    );
    assert!(window.tabs[0].panes().iter().all(|pane| pane.pty.pending()));
    window.pump_them_all();
    assert!(window.launch_cost.calls > 0);
    assert!(!keep_pending.is_empty());
}

#[test]
fn a_failed_launch_stays_idle_after_its_notice_is_dismissed() {
    let ctx = eframe::egui::Context::default();
    let mut pane = crate::Pane::with_panel(
        &Default::default(),
        crate::shell_integration::fallback_shell(),
        &[],
        None,
        0,
    )
    .unwrap();
    let (mut process, done) = pending();
    process.context = Some(ctx.clone());
    done.send(Err("fixture launch failure".into())).unwrap();
    pane.pty = process;
    let (closed, output) = channel();
    drop(closed);
    pane.pty_output = crate::pty_output::PtyOutput::new(output, || {});
    for _ in 0..4 {
        ctx.run_ui(Default::default(), |_| {})
            .drop_without_applying_deltas();
    }
    for dismissed in [false, true] {
        pane.pty.dismissed = dismissed;
        let output = ctx.run_ui(Default::default(), |_| refresh(&mut pane));
        let delay = output.viewport_output[&eframe::egui::ViewportId::ROOT].repaint_delay;
        output.drop_without_applying_deltas();
        assert_eq!(
            delay,
            Duration::MAX,
            "failed launch armed a repaint: {delay:?}"
        );
        assert!(pane.pty.keeps_open());
    }
}

crate::windows_unix_omission!(
    startup_prefix_is_interpreted_before_resize_even_with_the_bridge_parked,
    "unix-only: uses /bin/sh, stty and cat to test startup input ordering"
);
#[cfg(unix)]
#[test]
fn startup_prefix_is_interpreted_before_resize_even_with_the_bridge_parked() {
    for padding in [0, crate::PUMP_BUDGET + 7] {
        let original = crate::INITIAL_GRID;
        let narrow = Size {
            rows: original.rows,
            cols: original.cols / 2,
        };
        let mut prefix = vec![b' '; padding];
        prefix.extend(format!("abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyzABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ0123456789abcdef\x1b[1;{}H\x1b[10DX", original.cols).bytes());
        let mut expected = term_core::Screen::new(original);
        expected.advance(&prefix);
        expected.resize(narrow);
        let contents = |screen: &term_core::Screen| {
            screen
                .view()
                .cells()
                .iter()
                .map(|cell| cell.c)
                .collect::<Vec<_>>()
        };
        let mut wrong_grid = term_core::Screen::new(narrow);
        wrong_grid.advance(&prefix);
        assert_ne!(
            contents(&expected),
            contents(&wrong_grid),
            "fixture must distinguish the wrong grid"
        );
        let path =
            std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("ivy-startup-{}-{padding}", std::process::id()));
        let script = format!(
            "read -r line; printf '%s ' \"$line\" > '{}'; stty size >> '{}'; exec /bin/cat",
            path.display(),
            path.display()
        );
        let child = Pty::spawn("/bin/sh", &["-c", &script], original).unwrap();
        let (raw, output) = channel();
        raw.send(prefix.clone()).unwrap();
        let (woke, ready) = channel();
        let (mut process, held) = start_worker(
            std::sync::Arc::new(move || {
                let _ = woke.send(());
            }),
            Default::default(),
            move || Ok((child.0, output)),
        )
        .unwrap();
        if padding == 0 {
            process.write(b"first second\n").unwrap();
        }
        ready.recv_timeout(Duration::from_secs(3)).unwrap();
        let mut pane = crate::Pane::with_panel(
            &Default::default(),
            crate::shell_integration::fallback_shell(),
            &[],
            None,
            0,
        )
        .unwrap();
        pane.pty = process;
        pane.pty_output = crate::pty_output::PtyOutput::new(held, || {});
        pane.measured = narrow;
        let (parked, release) = crate::pty_output::hold_next_bridge();
        pane.pump(Default::default());
        parked.recv_timeout(Duration::from_secs(3)).unwrap();
        assert!(
            pane.pty_output.is_alive(),
            "bridge was allowed to finish instead of being parked"
        );
        pane.resize();
        finish_input(&mut pane);
        if prefix.len() > crate::PUMP_BUDGET {
            assert_eq!(
                pane.received.len(),
                crate::PUMP_BUDGET,
                "startup bypassed normal frame budget"
            );
            assert_eq!(
                pane.screen.size(),
                original,
                "resized with startup bytes outstanding"
            );
            assert!(pane.pty.startup_pending());
            pane.pty.write(b"first second\n").unwrap();
            pane.pty.write(b"tail").unwrap();
            finish_input(&mut pane);
            assert_eq!(
                pane.pty.input, b"first second\ntail",
                "live input bypassed startup ordering"
            );
            assert!(
                !path.exists(),
                "pending input reached the program before reflow"
            );
        }
        for _ in 0..prefix.len().div_ceil(crate::PUMP_BUDGET) {
            if !pane.pty.startup_pending() {
                break;
            }
            pane.pump(Default::default());
        }
        assert!(
            !pane.pty.startup_pending(),
            "captured startup bytes never reached the grid"
        );
        pane.resize();
        finish_input(&mut pane);
        assert_eq!(contents(&pane.screen), contents(&expected));
        let expected_input = format!("first second {} {}\n", narrow.rows, narrow.cols);
        let deadline = Instant::now() + Duration::from_secs(3);
        while std::fs::read_to_string(&path).ok().as_deref() != Some(expected_input.as_str())
            && Instant::now() < deadline
        {
            std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(2));
        }
        assert_eq!(
            std::fs::read_to_string(&path).unwrap(),
            format!("first second {} {}\n", narrow.rows, narrow.cols)
        );
        release.send(()).unwrap();
        drop(raw);
        drop(pane);
        std::fs::remove_file(path).unwrap();
    }
}

#[test]
fn startup_snapshot_has_a_finite_chunk_bound_and_preserves_the_remaining_stream() {
    let (pty, _output) = Pty::spawn(
        crate::shell_integration::fallback_shell(),
        &[],
        Size::default(),
    )
    .unwrap();
    let (source, output) = channel();
    for value in 0..9 {
        source.send(vec![value; 8192]).unwrap();
    }
    let ready = prepare((pty, output));
    assert_eq!(ready.startup.len(), 8 * 8192);
    assert_eq!(ready.spawned.1.try_recv().unwrap(), vec![8; 8192]);
}

#[test]
fn a_panicked_launch_worker_wakes_the_window_to_report_failure() {
    use std::sync::{
        Arc,
        atomic::{AtomicUsize, Ordering},
    };
    let wakes = Arc::new(AtomicUsize::new(0));
    let count = wakes.clone();
    let (mut process, _output) = start_worker(
        Arc::new(move || {
            count.fetch_add(1, Ordering::SeqCst);
        }),
        Default::default(),
        || panic!("fixture launch worker panic"),
    )
    .unwrap();
    let deadline = Instant::now() + Duration::from_secs(3);
    while wakes.load(Ordering::SeqCst) == 0 && Instant::now() < deadline {
        std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(2));
    }
    assert_eq!(wakes.load(Ordering::SeqCst), 1);
    assert!(process.poll().is_none());
    assert!(!process.pending());
    assert_eq!(
        process.notice(),
        Some("This console's program could not be started.")
    );
}

#[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
#[test]
#[ignore = "requires the built target/debug/app and a real systemd user manager"]
fn normal_restored_fallback_and_takeover_paths_use_the_explicit_ivy_launcher() {
    use std::os::unix::fs::PermissionsExt;
    let _guard = explicit_ivy_launcher();
    let root = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("ivy-launch-paths-{}", std::process::id()));
    std::fs::create_dir(&root).unwrap();
    let pidfile = root.join("pid");
    let program = root.join("fixture");
    std::fs::write(
        &program,
        format!(
            "#!/bin/sh\nprintf '%s' $$ > '{}'\nexec /bin/cat\n",
            pidfile.display()
        ),
    )
    .unwrap();
    std::fs::set_permissions(&program, std::fs::Permissions::from_mode(0o700)).unwrap();
    let ctx = eframe::egui::Context::default();
    let parent = std::fs::read_to_string("/proc/self/cgroup").unwrap();
    let mut groups = std::collections::HashSet::new();
    let mut check = |pane: &mut crate::Pane, kind: &str| {
        assert!(pane.pty.pending(), "{kind} bypassed the launch worker");
        let deadline = Instant::now() + Duration::from_secs(4);
        while (!pidfile.exists() || pane.pty.pending()) && Instant::now() < deadline {
            refresh(pane);
            finish_input(pane);
            std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(2));
        }
        assert!(pane.pty.failure.is_none(), "{kind}: {:?}", pane.pty.failure);
        let pid: u32 = std::fs::read_to_string(&pidfile).unwrap().parse().unwrap();
        let group = std::fs::read_to_string(format!("/proc/{pid}/cgroup")).unwrap();
        assert!(group.contains("/app.slice/ivy-console-"), "{kind}: {group}");
        assert_ne!(group, parent, "{kind} remained in the GUI scope");
        assert!(
            groups.insert(group),
            "{kind} reused another console's scope"
        );
        std::fs::remove_file(&pidfile).unwrap();
    };
    let mut normal =
        crate::Pane::with_panel(&ctx, program.to_str().unwrap(), &[], None, 0).unwrap();
    check(&mut normal, "normal");
    let saved = crate::session::interpret(
        &serde_json::json!({"v":2,"tabs":[{"root":{"leaf":{"panel_width":30,"folded":false}}}]})
            .to_string(),
    )
    .unwrap();
    let mut tabs = crate::Window::materialize(&ctx, &saved, program.to_str().unwrap()).unwrap();
    check(tabs[0].panes_mut().into_iter().next().unwrap(), "restored");
    let mut fallback =
        crate::Pane::with_panel(&ctx, "/definitely/missing/ivy-client", &[], None, 1).unwrap();
    fallback
        .pty
        .write(format!("printf '%s' $$ > '{}'\n", pidfile.display()).as_bytes())
        .unwrap();
    check(&mut fallback, "fallback");
    normal.restoration.failed = true;
    normal.restoration.fresh_shell_required = true;
    crate::resume::take_over(&mut normal).unwrap();
    normal
        .pty
        .write(format!("printf '%s' $$ > '{}'\n", pidfile.display()).as_bytes())
        .unwrap();
    check(&mut normal, "takeover");
    drop((normal, fallback, tabs));
    std::fs::remove_dir_all(root).unwrap();
}

#[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
pub(crate) struct LauncherGuard;
#[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
impl Drop for LauncherGuard {
    fn drop(&mut self) {
        TEST_LAUNCHER.with(|path| *path.borrow_mut() = None);
    }
}
#[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
pub(crate) fn explicit_ivy_launcher() -> LauncherGuard {
    // A deliberately explicit product executable: never current_exe(), which is the test runner.
    let launcher = Path::new(env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"))
        .join("../../target/debug/app")
        .canonicalize()
        .unwrap();
    assert_eq!(launcher.file_name().unwrap(), "app");
    TEST_LAUNCHER.with(|path| *path.borrow_mut() = Some(launcher));
    LauncherGuard
}

#[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
#[test]
#[ignore = "requires the built target/debug/app and a real systemd user manager"]
fn a_real_scoped_exit_keeps_repainting_until_the_observer_finishes() {
    let _guard = explicit_ivy_launcher();
    let ctx = eframe::egui::Context::default();
    let mut pane = crate::Pane::with_panel(
        &ctx,
        "/bin/sh",
        &["-c".into(), "printf retained; exit 0".into()],
        None,
        0,
    )
    .unwrap();
    let deadline = Instant::now() + Duration::from_secs(3);
    while (pane.pty.pending() || pane.pty_output.is_alive()) && Instant::now() < deadline {
        pane.pump(Default::default());
        std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(2));
    }
    assert!(!pane.pty.pending());
    assert!(!pane.pty_output.is_alive());
    assert_eq!(
        pane.pty.termination(),
        term_core::Termination::Pending,
        "fixture bypassed scope observation"
    );
    assert!(!pane.is_finished(false));
    // Drain output/worker wakes; the observer is the only remaining reason to repaint.
    for _ in 0..4 {
        ctx.run_ui(Default::default(), |_| {})
            .drop_without_applying_deltas();
    }
    let frame = ctx.run_ui(Default::default(), |_| refresh(&mut pane));
    let delay = frame.viewport_output[&eframe::egui::ViewportId::ROOT].repaint_delay;
    frame.drop_without_applying_deltas();
    assert!(
        delay <= Duration::from_millis(20),
        "no wake while observing: {delay:?}"
    );
    while pane.pty.termination() == term_core::Termination::Pending && Instant::now() < deadline {
        pane.pump(Default::default());
        std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(2));
    }
    assert_eq!(pane.pty.termination(), term_core::Termination::Ordinary);
    assert!(
        pane.is_finished(false),
        "ordinary observed exit did not become closable"
    );
    let text: String = pane
        .screen
        .view()
        .cells()
        .iter()
        .map(|cell| cell.c)
        .collect();
    assert!(text.contains("retained"), "observer discarded output");
}

fn normal_restored_fallback_and_takeover_paths_use_the_explicit_ivy_launcher() {}
```
