### crates/app/src/project/tests.rs

```
use super::*;
use std::{
    fs,
    path::Path,
    process::Command,
    sync::atomic::{AtomicUsize, Ordering},
};
static NEXT: AtomicUsize = AtomicUsize::new(0);
struct Temp(PathBuf);
impl Temp {
    fn new() -> Self {
        let path = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!(
            "ivy-project-{}-{}",
            std::process::id(),
            NEXT.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed)
        ));
        fs::create_dir(&path).unwrap();
        Self(path)
    }
}
impl Drop for Temp {
    fn drop(&mut self) {
        let _ = fs::remove_dir_all(&self.0);
    }
}
fn git(path: &Path, args: &[&str]) {
    let out = Command::new("git")
        .arg("-C")
        .arg(path)
        .args(args)
        .output()
        .unwrap();
    assert!(
        out.status.success(),
        "{}",
        String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stderr)
    );
}
#[test]
fn checkout_nested_directory_and_worktree_share_identity() {
    let temp = Temp::new();
    let main = temp.0.join("main");
    fs::create_dir(&main).unwrap();
    git(&main, &["init", "-q"]);
    git(
        &main,
        &[
            "-c",
            "user.name=Test",
            "-c",
            "user.email=test@example.invalid",
            "commit",
            "--allow-empty",
            "-qm",
            "initial",
        ],
    );
    let linked = temp.0.join("linked");
    git(
        &main,
        &["worktree", "add", "-qb", "linked", linked.to_str().unwrap()],
    );
    let nested = main.join("nested");
    fs::create_dir(&nested).unwrap();
    let identity = resolve(&main).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(identity.name, "main");
    assert_eq!(identity, resolve(&nested).unwrap());
    assert_eq!(identity, resolve(&linked).unwrap());
    let second = temp.0.join("second");
    fs::create_dir(&second).unwrap();
    git(&second, &["init", "-q"]);
    assert_ne!(identity.key, resolve(&second).unwrap().key);
}
#[test]
fn bare_and_custom_git_directories_name_from_shared_identity() {
    let temp = Temp::new();
    let bare = temp.0.join("bare.git");
    git(&temp.0, &["init", "--bare", "-q", bare.to_str().unwrap()]);
    assert_eq!(resolve(&bare).unwrap().name, "bare.git");
    let dot_git = temp.0.join(".git");
    git(
        &temp.0,
        &["init", "--bare", "-q", dot_git.to_str().unwrap()],
    );
    assert_eq!(resolve(&dot_git).unwrap().name, ".git");
    fs::remove_dir_all(dot_git).unwrap();
    let checkout = temp.0.join("checkout");
    let metadata = temp.0.join("metadata");
    git(
        &temp.0,
        &[
            "init",
            "-q",
            "--separate-git-dir",
            metadata.to_str().unwrap(),
            checkout.to_str().unwrap(),
        ],
    );
    let identity = resolve(&checkout).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(identity.name, "metadata");
    assert_eq!(identity.path, dunce::canonicalize(&metadata).unwrap());
}
#[test]
fn non_git_is_exact_folder_but_git_failures_stay_neutral() {
    let temp = Temp::new();
    let nested = temp.0.join("nested");
    fs::create_dir(&nested).unwrap();
    let identity = resolve(&temp.0).unwrap();
    assert!(matches!(identity.key, Key::Folder(_)));
    assert_eq!(identity, resolve(&temp.0).unwrap());
    assert_ne!(identity.key, resolve(&nested).unwrap().key);
    assert!(resolve_with_git(&temp.0, std::ffi::OsStr::new("/missing/ivy-project-git")).is_err());
    assert!(!non_repository(
        b"fatal: detected dubious ownership in repository"
    ));
    assert!(!non_repository(b"fatal: not a git repository: broken.git"));
    assert!(resolve(&temp.0.join("missing")).is_err());
    fs::write(nested.join(".git"), "gitdir: /missing/ivy-metadata\n").unwrap();
    assert!(
        resolve(&nested).is_err(),
        "broken Git metadata must stay neutral"
    );
}
fn state(directory: &Path, generation: u64) -> Observation {
    Observation {
        directory: Some(directory.into()),
        generation,
        identity: None,
        requested: true,
        retry_at: Instant::now(),
        last_error: None,
    }
}
fn request(directory: &Path, generation: u64) -> Request {
    Request {
        pane: 1,
        generation,
        directory: directory.into(),
    }
}
fn inert_worker() -> Worker {
    Worker {
        shared: Arc::new(Shared {
            queue: Mutex::new(Queue::default()),
            wake: Condvar::new(),
        }),
    }
}
#[test]
fn requests_coalesce_and_stale_completions_cannot_replace_latest_source() {
    let temp = Temp::new();
    let worker = inert_worker();
    let a = request(&temp.0.join("a"), 1);
    let b = request(&temp.0.join("b"), 2);
    let c = request(&temp.0, 3);
    worker.request(a.clone());
    worker.request(b);
    worker.request(c.clone());
    {
        let queue = worker.shared.queue.lock().unwrap();
        assert_eq!(queue.pending.len(), 1);
        assert_eq!(queue.pending[&1], c);
    }
    let mut projects = Projects::default();
    projects.panes.insert(1, state(&temp.0, 3));
    projects.worker = Some(worker);
    let identity = resolve(&temp.0).unwrap();
    projects
        .worker
        .as_ref()
        .unwrap()
        .shared
        .queue
        .lock()
        .unwrap()
        .completed
        .insert(
            1,
            Completion {
                request: a,
                result: Ok(identity.clone()),
            },
        );
    assert!(!projects.poll());
    assert!(projects.identity(1).is_none());
    projects
        .worker
        .as_ref()
        .unwrap()
        .shared
        .queue
        .lock()
        .unwrap()
        .completed
        .insert(
            1,
            Completion {
                request: c,
                result: Ok(identity.clone()),
            },
        );
    assert!(projects.poll());
    assert_eq!(projects.identity(1), Some(&identity));
    projects.retain(&[]);
    let queue = projects
        .worker
        .as_ref()
        .unwrap()
        .shared
        .queue
        .lock()
        .unwrap();
    assert!(queue.wanted.is_empty() && queue.pending.is_empty() && queue.completed.is_empty());
    assert!(projects.identity(1).is_none());
}
#[test]
fn absent_metadata_is_lazy_and_changed_source_clears_badge() {
    let temp = Temp::new();
    let ctx = egui::Context::default();
    let now = Instant::now();
    let mut projects = Projects::default();
    projects.observe(1, None, now, &ctx);
    assert!(projects.worker.is_none());
    projects.panes.insert(1, state(&temp.0, 1));
    projects.panes.get_mut(&1).unwrap().identity = Some(resolve(&temp.0).unwrap());
    projects.observe(1, None, now, &ctx);
    assert!(projects.identity(1).is_none());
    assert!(projects.worker.is_none());
}
#[test]
fn errors_retry_only_on_later_observations_after_five_seconds() {
    let temp = Temp::new();
    let ctx = egui::Context::default();
    let now = Instant::now();
    let mut projects = Projects::default();
    projects.panes.insert(1, state(&temp.0, 1));
    projects.worker = Some(inert_worker());
    projects
        .worker
        .as_ref()
        .unwrap()
        .shared
        .queue
        .lock()
        .unwrap()
        .completed
        .insert(
            1,
            Completion {
                request: request(&temp.0, 1),
                result: Err("Git timed out".into()),
            },
        );
    assert!(!projects.poll());
    projects.observe(1, Some(temp.0.clone()), now, &ctx);
    assert!(
        projects
            .worker
            .as_ref()
            .unwrap()
            .shared
            .queue
            .lock()
            .unwrap()
            .pending
            .is_empty()
    );
    projects.observe(1, Some(temp.0.clone()), Instant::now() + RETRY, &ctx);
    assert_eq!(
        projects
            .worker
            .as_ref()
            .unwrap()
            .shared
            .queue
            .lock()
            .unwrap()
            .pending
            .len(),
        1
    );
}

#[test]
fn busy_worker_keeps_latest_request_and_does_not_publish_closed_panes() {
    let (started_tx, started_rx) = std::sync::mpsc::channel();
    let (release_tx, release_rx) = std::sync::mpsc::channel();
    let release_rx = Mutex::new(release_rx);
    let worker = Worker::start_with(egui::Context::default(), move |path| {
        started_tx.send(path.to_owned()).unwrap();
        release_rx
            .lock()
            .unwrap()
            .recv_timeout(Duration::from_secs(5))
            .unwrap();
        Ok(Identity {
            key: Key::Folder(path.into()),
            name: basename(path),
            path: path.into(),
        })
    })
    .unwrap();
    let a = request(Path::new("a"), 1);
    let b = request(Path::new("b"), 2);
    let c = request(Path::new("c"), 3);
    worker.request(a);
    assert_eq!(
        started_rx.recv_timeout(Duration::from_secs(2)).unwrap(),
        PathBuf::from("a")
    );
    worker.request(b);
    worker.request(c.clone());
    release_tx.send(()).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(
        started_rx.recv_timeout(Duration::from_secs(2)).unwrap(),
        c.directory
    );
    assert!(worker.shared.queue.lock().unwrap().completed.is_empty());
    worker.forget(1);
    release_tx.send(()).unwrap();
    // A following job is a barrier proving the closed pane's job has completed.
    let mut barrier = request(Path::new("barrier"), 1);
    barrier.pane = 2;
    worker.request(barrier);
    assert_eq!(
        started_rx.recv_timeout(Duration::from_secs(2)).unwrap(),
        PathBuf::from("barrier")
    );
    assert!(
        !worker
            .shared
            .queue
            .lock()
            .unwrap()
            .completed
            .contains_key(&1)
    );
    release_tx.send(()).unwrap();
}

crate::windows_unix_omission!(
    non_utf8_repository_paths_group_checkout_subdirectory_and_worktree,
    "unix-only: constructs non-UTF-8 paths with Unix OsStr encoding"
);

#[cfg(unix)]
#[test]

fn non_utf8_repository_paths_group_checkout_subdirectory_and_worktree() {
    use std::os::unix::ffi::OsStringExt;
    let temp = Temp::new();
    let main = temp
        .0
        .join(std::ffi::OsString::from_vec(b"project-\xff".to_vec()));
    fs::create_dir(&main).unwrap();
    git(&main, &["init", "-q"]);
    git(
        &main,
        &[
            "-c",
            "user.name=Test",
            "-c",
            "user.email=test@example.invalid",
            "commit",
            "--allow-empty",
            "-qm",
            "initial",
        ],
    );
    let nested = main.join("nested");
    fs::create_dir(&nested).unwrap();
    let linked = temp
        .0
        .join(std::ffi::OsString::from_vec(b"linked-\xfe".to_vec()));
    let output = Command::new("git")
        .arg("-C")
        .arg(&main)
        .args(["worktree", "add", "-qb", "linked"])
        .arg(&linked)
        .output()
        .unwrap();
    assert!(
        output.status.success(),
        "{}",
        String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stderr)
    );
    let identity = resolve(&main).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(
        identity.key,
        Key::Repository(dunce::canonicalize(main.join(".git")).unwrap())
    );
    assert_eq!(
        identity.path,
        dunce::canonicalize(main.join(".git")).unwrap()
    );
    assert_eq!(identity.name, "project-\u{fffd}");
    assert_eq!(identity, resolve(&nested).unwrap());
    assert_eq!(identity, resolve(&linked).unwrap());
}
#[cfg(windows)]
#[test]
fn windows_paths_project_identity_has_no_verbatim_prefix() {
    let temp = Temp::new();
    let folder = temp.0.join("folder");
    let repository = temp.0.join("repository");
    fs::create_dir(&folder).unwrap();
    fs::create_dir(&repository).unwrap();
    git(&repository, &["init", "-q"]);
    for directory in [&folder, &repository] {
        let identity = resolve(directory).unwrap();
        let (Key::Folder(key) | Key::Repository(key)) = &identity.key;
        for shown in [identity.path.as_os_str(), key.as_os_str()] {
            let shown = shown.to_string_lossy();
            assert!(!shown.starts_with(r"\\?\"), "{shown}");
            assert!(shown.as_bytes().get(1) == Some(&b':'), "{shown}");
        }
    }
}
```

### crates/app/src/restoration.rs

```
//! Optional startup recovery never turns a secondary window into the snapshot writer.
use crate::{OpenWith, OpeningLayout, Window, session, settings::StartupRestore};
use std::{
    io::Write,
    path::{Path, PathBuf},
    time::{Duration, Instant},
};

#[derive(Default)]
pub(crate) struct PaneState {
    pub(crate) directory: Option<PathBuf>,
    pub(crate) failed: bool,
    pub(crate) resume: Option<panel_control::session::Identity>,
    pub(crate) resuming: bool,
    pub(crate) fresh_shell_required: bool,
    pub(crate) interacted: bool,
    pub(crate) session_status: Option<crate::ai_sessions::Status>,
    pub(crate) session_revision: u64,
    pub(crate) scope_unavailable: bool,
    pub(crate) session_directory: Option<PathBuf>,
    pub(crate) sessions: Option<crate::ai_sessions::Handle>,
    pub(crate) route: crate::resume::Route,
    pub(crate) wake: Option<std::sync::Arc<dyn Fn() + Send + Sync>>,
}

impl PaneState {
    pub(crate) fn take_over(&mut self) {
        if self.resuming {
            self.interacted = true;
        }
        if self.failed {
            self.failed = false;
            self.directory = None;
            self.resume = None;
            self.session_directory = None;
            self.resuming = false;
            self.fresh_shell_required = false;
            self.session_revision = 0;
        }
    }
}

pub(crate) struct State {
    mode: StartupRestore,
    primary: bool,
    measuring: bool,
    _ownership: Option<session::ownership::Ownership>,
    total: Duration,
    frames: usize,
    pending: Option<Duration>,
}

impl Default for State {
    fn default() -> Self {
        // Low-level constructors keep their historical persistence behavior. User startup always
        // replaces this with the settings-resolved policy before the first frame.
        Self {
            mode: StartupRestore::Layout,
            primary: true,
            measuring: true,
            _ownership: None,
            total: Duration::ZERO,
            frames: 0,
            pending: None,
        }
    }
}

impl State {
    fn record(&mut self, cost: Duration) {
        *self.pending.get_or_insert_default() += cost;
    }

    pub(crate) fn close_frame(&mut self) {
        if let Some(cost) = self.pending.take() {
            self.total += cost;
            self.frames += 1;
        }
    }

    pub(crate) fn captures_sessions(&self) -> bool {
        !self.measuring && self.primary && self.mode == StartupRestore::Sessions
    }

    pub(crate) fn can_save(&self) -> bool {
        self.primary && self.mode != StartupRestore::None
    }

    pub(crate) fn publish(&self, to: &mut dyn Write) -> std::io::Result<()> {
        writeln!(
            to,
            "restoration metadata per frame (cached local directories): mean_us={:.3}, frames={}",
            self.total.as_secs_f64() * 1e6 / self.frames.max(1) as f64,
            self.frames
        )
    }
}

pub(crate) fn valid_directory_text(text: &str) -> bool {
    !text.is_empty()
        && text.len() <= 4096
        && Path::new(text).is_absolute()
        && !text.chars().any(char::is_control)
}

/// The measurement path retains its seeded-layout semantics and overwrite witness.
pub(crate) fn prepare(
    persistence: &mut session::Persistence,
    path: Option<&Path>,
    mode: StartupRestore,
    measuring: bool,
    tabs: Option<&str>,
    panes: Option<&str>,
) -> (OpenWith, State) {
    if measuring {
        return (
            crate::open_with(tabs, panes, persistence.load()),
            State::default(),
        );
    }
    let ownership = path.and_then(Path::parent).and_then(|directory| {
        match session::ownership::Ownership::acquire(directory) {
            Ok(ownership) => Some(ownership),
            Err(error) => {
                eprintln!("Ivy cannot reserve its saved layout ({error}); this window opens without restoration or saving");
                None
            }
        }
    });
    let primary = ownership.as_ref().is_some_and(|lease| lease.is_primary());
    let saved = if primary && mode != StartupRestore::None {
        persistence.load()
    } else {
        None
    };
    let saved = match mode {
        StartupRestore::Sessions => saved,
        StartupRestore::Directories => saved.map(session::Validated::without_sessions),
        StartupRestore::None | StartupRestore::Layout => {
            saved.map(session::Validated::without_directories)
        }
    };
    let opening = match saved {
        Some(saved) => OpenWith::Restored(saved),
        None => OpenWith::Factory {
            tabs: 1,
            layout: OpeningLayout {
                columns: 1,
                rows: 1,
            },
        },
    };
    (
        opening,
        State {
            mode,
            primary,
            measuring: false,
            _ownership: ownership,
            total: Duration::ZERO,
            frames: 0,
            pending: None,
        },
    )
}

/// Consume successful Settings saves before a due write or clean shutdown can use the old policy.
pub(crate) fn apply_saved_mode(window: &mut Window) {
    if window.restoration.measuring {
        return;
    }
    let Some(mode) = window.settings_editor.take_saved_startup_restore() else {
        return;
    };
    let before = Instant::now();
    if window.restoration.mode == StartupRestore::None
        && mode != StartupRestore::None
        && window.restoration.primary
    {
        let _ = window.persistence.load();
    }
    if mode != StartupRestore::Sessions {
        for pane in window.all_panes_mut() {
            crate::resume::set_capture(pane, false);
            if pane.restoration.failed && pane.restoration.resume.is_some() {
                pane.restoration.failed = pane.restoration.fresh_shell_required
                    || pane
                        .restoration
                        .directory
                        .as_ref()
                        .is_some_and(|directory| !directory.is_dir());
            }
            pane.restoration.resume = None;
            pane.restoration.session_directory = None;
            pane.restoration.resuming = false;
        }
    }
    window.restoration.mode = mode;
    window.last_seen = None;
    window.persistence.save_due = None;
    if !mode.has_directories() {
        for pane in window.all_panes_mut() {
            pane.restoration.directory = None;
            pane.restoration.failed = pane.restoration.fresh_shell_required;
        }
    }
    let cost = before.elapsed();
    window.restoration.record(cost);
    window.timed_separately += cost;
}

pub(crate) fn refresh(window: &mut Window) {
    if window.restoration.measuring
        || !window.restoration.primary
        || !window.restoration.mode.has_directories()
    {
        return;
    }
    let before = Instant::now();
    let capture_sessions = window.restoration.mode == StartupRestore::Sessions;
    if capture_sessions {
        for pane in window.all_panes_mut() {
            crate::resume::set_capture(pane, !pane.restoration.failed);
            crate::resume::inspect_restored_exit(pane);
        }
    }
    let projects = &window.projects;
    for tab in &mut window.tabs {
        for pane in tab.panes_mut() {
            if !pane.restoration.failed {
                if capture_sessions {
                    crate::resume::capture(pane, None);
                    if pane.restoration.failed {
                        continue;
                    }
                }
                let unusable = pane.screen.working_directory_reported()
                    && pane.screen.working_directory().is_none();
                observe_directory(
                    &mut pane.restoration,
                    projects.directory(pane.name),
                    unusable,
                );
            }
        }
    }
    let cost = before.elapsed();
    window.restoration.record(cost);
    window.timed_separately += cost;
}

fn observe_directory(state: &mut PaneState, observed: Option<&Path>, explicitly_unusable: bool) {
    let observed = state.session_directory.as_deref().or(observed);
    if explicitly_unusable {
        state.directory = None;
    } else if let Some(path) = observed {
        if !path.to_str().is_some_and(valid_directory_text) {
            state.directory = None;
        } else if state.directory.as_deref() != Some(path) {
            state.directory = Some(path.to_path_buf());
        }
    }
}

/// A final native observation covers navigation immediately before closing, without an idle timer.
pub(crate) fn capture_on_exit(window: &mut Window) {
    apply_saved_mode(window);
    if window.restoration.measuring
        || !window.restoration.can_save()
        || !window.restoration.mode.has_directories()
    {
        return;
    }
    for pane in window.all_panes_mut() {
        if pane.restoration.resuming && pane.pty.exit_success() == Some(false) {
            pane.restoration.failed = true;
            pane.restoration.fresh_shell_required = true;
            pane.restoration.resuming = false;
        }
    }
    let snapshots = if window.restoration.mode == StartupRestore::Sessions {
        let handles: Vec<_> = window
            .all_panes()
            .filter_map(|pane| pane.restoration.sessions.as_ref())
            .collect();
        crate::ai_sessions::freeze_all(&handles, Duration::from_millis(1250))
    } else {
        Vec::new()
    };
    let mut snapshots = snapshots.into_iter();
    let capture_sessions = window.restoration.mode == StartupRestore::Sessions;
    for pane in window.all_panes_mut() {
        let snapshot = pane
            .restoration
            .sessions
            .as_ref()
            .and_then(|_| snapshots.next());
        if pane.restoration.failed {
            continue;
        }
        if capture_sessions {
            crate::resume::capture(pane, snapshot);
            if pane.restoration.failed {
                continue;
            }
        }
        let unusable =
            pane.screen.working_directory_reported() && pane.screen.working_directory().is_none();
        let directory = pane
            .pty
            .working_directory()
            .or_else(|| pane.screen.working_directory().map(local_directory));
        observe_directory(&mut pane.restoration, directory.as_deref(), unusable);
        if capture_sessions
            && pane.restoration.resuming
            && pane
                .restoration
                .resume
                .as_ref()
                .is_some_and(|identity| identity.client != panel_control::session::Client::Codex)
        {
            pane.restoration.resume = None;
        }
    }
}

fn local_directory(path: &Path) -> PathBuf {
    #[cfg(windows)]
    {
        let text = path.to_string_lossy();
        PathBuf::from(text.strip_prefix('/').unwrap_or(&text))
    }
    #[cfg(not(windows))]
    {
        path.to_path_buf()
    }
}

pub(crate) fn open_leaf(
    ctx: &eframe::egui::Context,
    leaf: &session::StoredLeaf,
    program: &str,
    name: usize,
    reads: crate::control::ReadActivity,
) -> term_core::Result<crate::Pane> {
    if leaf.resume.is_some() {
        match crate::resume::try_open(ctx, leaf, name, reads.clone()) {
            Ok(pane) => return Ok(pane),
            Err(error) => {
                eprintln!(
                    "The saved AI session could not be started ({error}); opening a fresh console"
                );
                let mut shell = leaf.clone();
                shell.resume = None;
                let mut pane = open_leaf(ctx, &shell, program, name, reads)?;
                pane.restoration.resume = leaf.resume.clone();
                pane.restoration.failed = true;
                return Ok(pane);
            }
        }
    }
    let directory = leaf.cwd.as_deref().filter(|path| path.is_dir());
    let opened = crate::opening::launch_shell_in(
        ctx,
        program,
        crate::panel_or_none(panel_contract::PanelDir::create()),
        name,
        reads.clone(),
        directory,
    );
    let (mut pane, failed) = match opened {
        Ok(pane) => (pane, leaf.cwd.is_some() && directory.is_none()),
        Err(_) if directory.is_some() => (
            crate::opening::launch_shell_in(
                ctx,
                program,
                crate::panel_or_none(panel_contract::PanelDir::create()),
                name,
                reads,
                None,
            )?,
            true,
        ),
        Err(error) => return Err(error),
    };
    pane.restoration.directory = leaf.cwd.clone();
    pane.restoration.failed = failed;
    Ok(pane)
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::session::{StoredDocument, StoredLeaf, StoredNode, StoredTab};

    struct Fixture {
        directory: PathBuf,
        snapshot: PathBuf,
    }
    impl Fixture {
        fn new() -> Self {
            let mut id = [0u8; 8];
            getrandom::fill(&mut id).unwrap();
            let directory =
                std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("ivy-restore-{:x}", u64::from_le_bytes(id)));
            std::fs::create_dir(&directory).unwrap();
            let snapshot = directory.join("layout.json");
            Self {
                directory,
                snapshot,
            }
        }
        fn seed(&self, cwd: Option<PathBuf>) -> Vec<u8> {
            let document = StoredDocument {
                v: session::VERSION,
                tabs: (0..2)
                    .map(|_| StoredTab {
                        label: None,
                        root: StoredNode::Leaf(StoredLeaf {
                            resume: None,
                            panel_width: 37,
                            folded: true,
                            label: Some("Saved console".into()),
                            cwd: cwd.clone(),
                        }),
                    })
                    .collect(),
            };
            let bytes = session::serialize(&document).unwrap().into_bytes();
            std::fs::write(&self.snapshot, &bytes).unwrap();
            bytes
        }
        fn prepare(&self, mode: StartupRestore) -> (OpenWith, State, session::Persistence) {
            let mut persistence = session::Persistence::at(Some(self.snapshot.clone()));
            let (opening, state) = prepare(
                &mut persistence,
                Some(&self.snapshot),
                mode,
                false,
                None,
                None,
            );
            (opening, state, persistence)
        }
    }
    impl Drop for Fixture {
        fn drop(&mut self) {
            let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&self.directory);
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn startup_none_opens_one_console_and_cannot_replace_the_existing_snapshot() {
        let fixture = Fixture::new();
        let bytes = fixture.seed(Some(fixture.directory.clone()));
        let (opening, state, persistence) = fixture.prepare(StartupRestore::None);
        assert!(
            matches!(&opening, OpenWith::Factory { tabs: 1, layout } if layout.columns == 1 && layout.rows == 1)
        );
        assert!(
            persistence.load_time().is_none(),
            "disabled startup must not read the saved document"
        );
        let mut window = Window::opening(
            &Default::default(),
            opening,
            crate::shell_integration::fallback_shell(),
            None,
            persistence,
        )
        .unwrap();
        window.restoration = state;
        window.active_mut().panes_mut()[0].label = Some("A change in the fresh window".into());
        window.check_whether_the_layout_moved();
        window.save_now();
        assert_eq!(std::fs::read(&fixture.snapshot).unwrap(), bytes);
        assert_eq!(window.tabs.len(), 1);
        assert_eq!(window.all_panes().count(), 1);
    }

    #[test]
    fn layout_restores_geometry_but_discards_saved_directories() {
        let fixture = Fixture::new();
        fixture.seed(Some(fixture.directory.clone()));
        let (opening, state, persistence) = fixture.prepare(StartupRestore::Layout);
        let mut window = Window::opening(
            &Default::default(),
            opening,
            crate::shell_integration::fallback_shell(),
            None,
            persistence,
        )
        .unwrap();
        window.restoration = state;
        assert_eq!(window.tabs.len(), 2);
        for pane in window.all_panes() {
            assert_eq!(pane.panel_width, 37);
            assert!(pane.folded);
            assert_eq!(pane.label.as_deref(), Some("Saved console"));
            assert!(pane.restoration.directory.is_none());
        }
        let ids: std::collections::HashSet<_> = window
            .all_panes()
            .map(|pane| pane.panel.as_ref().unwrap().id().to_owned())
            .collect();
        assert_eq!(ids.len(), 2);
    }

    crate::windows_unix_omission!(
        directory_restore_sets_the_real_child_cwd_without_console_input,
        "unix-only: verifies child working directory through Linux process inspection"
    );

    #[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
    #[test]
    fn directory_restore_sets_the_real_child_cwd_without_console_input() {
        let fixture = Fixture::new();
        fixture.seed(Some(fixture.directory.clone()));
        let (opening, state, persistence) = fixture.prepare(StartupRestore::Directories);
        let mut window =
            Window::opening(&Default::default(), opening, "/bin/sh", None, persistence).unwrap();
        window.restoration = state;
        for pane in window.all_panes_mut() {
            let deadline = Instant::now() + Duration::from_secs(2);
            while pane.pty.working_directory().as_deref() != Some(fixture.directory.as_path()) {
                assert!(
                    Instant::now() < deadline,
                    "restored process did not enter its saved directory"
                );
                std::thread::yield_now();
            }
            assert!(pane.keystrokes.is_empty());
            assert!(!pane.restoration.failed);
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn missing_directory_is_visible_and_survives_an_unrelated_save_until_takeover() {
        let fixture = Fixture::new();
        let missing = fixture.directory.join("removed");
        fixture.seed(Some(missing.clone()));
        let (opening, state, persistence) = fixture.prepare(StartupRestore::Directories);
        let mut window = Window::opening(
            &Default::default(),
            opening,
            crate::shell_integration::fallback_shell(),
            None,
            persistence,
        )
        .unwrap();
        window.restoration = state;
        assert!(matches!(
            crate::painter::notices::what_the_silence_is(window.active().panes()[0]),
            Some(crate::painter::notices::Silence::RestoreDirectory)
        ));
        window.active_mut().panes_mut()[0].label = Some("Renamed while recovering".into());
        window.check_whether_the_layout_moved();
        window.save_now();
        let saved = session::read(&fixture.snapshot).unwrap();
        assert!(
            matches!(&saved.tabs()[0].root, StoredNode::Leaf(leaf) if leaf.cwd.as_ref() == Some(&missing))
        );
        window.active_mut().panes_mut()[0].hand_it_back_to_the_person();
        window.check_whether_the_layout_moved();
        window.save_now();
        let saved = session::read(&fixture.snapshot).unwrap();
        assert!(matches!(&saved.tabs()[0].root, StoredNode::Leaf(leaf) if leaf.cwd.is_none()));
        assert!(!window.active().panes()[0].restoration.failed);
    }

    #[test]
    fn startup_child() {
        use std::io::{Read, Write};
        let Ok(snapshot) = std::env::var("IVY_STARTUP_TEST_SNAPSHOT") else {
            return;
        };
        let mut gate = [0u8; 1];
        std::io::stdin().read_exact(&mut gate).unwrap();
        let snapshot = PathBuf::from(snapshot);
        let mut persistence = session::Persistence::at(Some(snapshot.clone()));
        let (opening, _state) = prepare(
            &mut persistence,
            Some(&snapshot),
            StartupRestore::Layout,
            false,
            None,
            None,
        );
        println!(
            "STARTUP_RESTORED={}",
            matches!(opening, OpenWith::Restored(_))
        );
        std::io::stdout().flush().unwrap();
        std::io::stdin().read_exact(&mut gate).unwrap();
    }

    struct StartupChild {
        child: std::process::Child,
        input: Option<std::process::ChildStdin>,
        output: std::io::BufReader<std::process::ChildStdout>,
    }
    impl StartupChild {
        fn spawn(snapshot: &Path) -> Self {
            let mut child = std::process::Command::new(std::env::current_exe().unwrap())
                .args([
                    "restoration::tests::startup_child",
                    "--exact",
                    "--nocapture",
                ])
                .env("IVY_STARTUP_TEST_SNAPSHOT", snapshot)
                .stdin(std::process::Stdio::piped())
                .stdout(std::process::Stdio::piped())
                .spawn()
                .unwrap();
            let input = child.stdin.take();
            let output = std::io::BufReader::new(child.stdout.take().unwrap());
            Self {
                child,
                input,
                output,
            }
        }
        fn restored(&mut self) -> bool {
            use std::io::{BufRead, Write};
            self.input.as_mut().unwrap().write_all(b"s").unwrap();
            loop {
                let mut line = String::new();
                assert!(
                    self.output.read_line(&mut line).unwrap() > 0,
                    "startup helper exited before answering"
                );
                if let Some((_, value)) = line.split_once("STARTUP_RESTORED=") {
                    return value.trim() == "true";
                }
            }
        }
        fn close(&mut self) {
            use std::io::Write;
            self.input.take().unwrap().write_all(b"x").unwrap();
            assert!(self.child.wait().unwrap().success());
        }
    }
    impl Drop for StartupChild {
        fn drop(&mut self) {
            let _ = self.child.kill();
            let _ = self.child.wait();
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn secondary_start_cannot_restore_even_after_the_primary_exits() {
        let fixture = Fixture::new();
        fixture.seed(None);
        let mut primary = StartupChild::spawn(&fixture.snapshot);
        assert!(primary.restored());
        let mut secondary = StartupChild::spawn(&fixture.snapshot);
        assert!(!secondary.restored());
        primary.close();
        let mut third = StartupChild::spawn(&fixture.snapshot);
        assert!(!third.restored());
        secondary.close();
        third.close();
        let mut next = StartupChild::spawn(&fixture.snapshot);
        assert!(next.restored());
        next.close();
    }

    #[test]
    fn saved_directory_shape_rejects_relative_control_and_oversize_paths() {
        for invalid in [
            "relative".to_owned(),
            "/tmp/bad\nname".to_owned(),
            format!("/{}", "a".repeat(4096)),
        ] {
            let fixture = Fixture::new();
            fixture.seed(Some(PathBuf::from(invalid)));
            assert!(session::read(&fixture.snapshot).is_err());
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn oversized_serialized_snapshot_never_replaces_the_previous_file() {
        let fixture = Fixture::new();
        let original = fixture.seed(None);
        let directory = PathBuf::from(format!("/{}", "a".repeat(3000)));
        let doc = StoredDocument {
            v: session::VERSION,
            tabs: (0..session::MAX_LEAVES)
                .map(|_| StoredTab {
                    label: None,
                    root: StoredNode::Leaf(StoredLeaf {
                        resume: None,
                        panel_width: 37,
                        folded: false,
                        label: None,
                        cwd: Some(directory.clone()),
                    }),
                })
                .collect(),
        };
        let validated = session::validate(doc).unwrap();
        let mut persistence = session::Persistence::at(Some(fixture.snapshot.clone()));
        assert!(matches!(
            persistence.save(&validated),
            Err(session::SaveFailure::DoesntFit)
        ));
        assert_eq!(std::fs::read(&fixture.snapshot).unwrap(), original);
        assert_eq!(
            std::fs::read_dir(&fixture.directory).unwrap().count(),
            1,
            "oversize must not create a temporary"
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn verified_client_directory_wins_over_wrapper_cwd_but_not_remote_veto() {
        let declared = PathBuf::from("/tmp/client-project");
        let wrapper = Path::new("/tmp/launch-wrapper");
        let mut state = PaneState {
            session_directory: Some(declared.clone()),
            ..Default::default()
        };
        observe_directory(&mut state, Some(wrapper), false);
        assert_eq!(state.directory, Some(declared));
        observe_directory(&mut state, Some(wrapper), true);
        assert!(state.directory.is_none());
    }

    #[test]
    fn explicit_invalid_directory_clears_old_context_but_missing_observation_preserves_it() {
        let previous = PathBuf::from("/tmp/previous-project");
        let mut state = PaneState {
            directory: Some(previous.clone()),
            ..Default::default()
        };
        observe_directory(&mut state, None, false);
        assert_eq!(state.directory, Some(previous));
        let mut screen = term_core::Screen::new(crate::INITIAL_GRID);
        screen.advance(b"\x1b]7;file:///tmp/a%C2%85b\x07");
        let declared = screen
            .working_directory()
            .expect("OSC 7 accepts this explicit local path");
        observe_directory(&mut state, Some(declared), false);
        assert!(
            state.directory.is_none(),
            "an invalid new path must not retain an old project"
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn long_missing_directory_keeps_a_visible_notice_without_losing_its_full_target() {
        let ctx = eframe::egui::Context::default();
        let path = PathBuf::from(format!("/tmp/{}", "a/".repeat(2000)));
        let leaf = StoredLeaf {
            resume: None,
            panel_width: 37,
            folded: false,
            label: None,
            cwd: Some(path.clone()),
        };
        let pane = open_leaf(
            &ctx,
            &leaf,
            crate::shell_integration::fallback_shell(),
            0,
            Default::default(),
        )
        .unwrap();
        assert!(pane.restoration.failed);
        ctx.run_ui(Default::default(), |ui| {
            let metrics = crate::painter::CellMetrics::measure(
                ui.ctx(),
                eframe::egui::FontId::monospace(14.0),
            );
            let area = eframe::egui::Rect::from_min_size(
                eframe::egui::Pos2::ZERO,
                eframe::egui::vec2(1200.0, 760.0),
            );
            let boxes = crate::painter::notices::the_boxes(
                ui.painter(),
                &pane,
                &crate::theme::Theme::default(),
                &metrics,
                area,
            );
            assert!(
                boxes.silence.is_some(),
                "an accepted long path must not hide the fallback explanation"
            );
        })
        .drop_without_applying_deltas();
        assert_eq!(
            pane.restoration.directory,
            Some(path),
            "only display text may be shortened"
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn repeated_capture_passes_close_one_actual_frame_sample() {
        let fixture = Fixture::new();
        let (opening, state, persistence) = fixture.prepare(StartupRestore::Directories);
        let mut window = Window::opening(
            &Default::default(),
            opening,
            crate::shell_integration::fallback_shell(),
            None,
            persistence,
        )
        .unwrap();
        window.restoration = state;
        refresh(&mut window);
        refresh(&mut window);
        assert_eq!(window.restoration.frames, 0, "passes are not frame samples");
        window.close_the_frame(Duration::from_secs(1));
        assert_eq!(window.restoration.frames, 1);
        let first = window.restoration.total;
        window.close_the_frame(Duration::from_secs(1));
        assert_eq!(
            window.restoration.frames, 1,
            "a frame with no new capture is not another sample"
        );
        assert_eq!(window.restoration.total, first);
    }

    #[test]
    fn legacy_measurement_restores_seeded_layout_despite_the_default_none_mode() {
        let fixture = Fixture::new();
        fixture.seed(None);
        let mut persistence = session::Persistence::at(Some(fixture.snapshot.clone()));
        persistence.forbid_overwrite("measurement");
        let (opening, state) = prepare(
            &mut persistence,
            Some(&fixture.snapshot),
            StartupRestore::None,
            true,
            None,
            None,
        );
        assert!(matches!(opening, OpenWith::Restored(_)));
        assert!(state.can_save());
        assert!(!fixture.directory.join("window-leases").exists());
    }
}
```

### crates/app/src/resume.rs

```
//! Fixed client launches and a revocable bridge to the pane's off-frame session service.
use crate::{control, opening, session};
use panel_control::session::{Client, Identity, SessionHandler};
use std::sync::{Arc, Mutex};

#[derive(Default)]
pub(crate) struct Route {
    handler: Option<Arc<Mutex<Option<SessionHandler>>>>,
}

impl Route {
    pub(crate) fn callback(&mut self) -> SessionHandler {
        let handler = Arc::clone(self.handler.get_or_insert_with(Default::default));
        Arc::new(move |peer, request| {
            let callback = handler
                .lock()
                .unwrap_or_else(|error| error.into_inner())
                .clone();
            match callback {
                Some(callback) => callback(peer, request),
                None => serde_json::json!({"ok":false,"error":"disabled"}),
            }
        })
    }

    pub(crate) fn set(&self, callback: Option<SessionHandler>) {
        if let Some(handler) = &self.handler {
            *handler.lock().unwrap_or_else(|error| error.into_inner()) = callback;
        }
    }
}

pub(crate) fn helper_path() -> Option<std::path::PathBuf> {
    let executable = std::env::current_exe().ok()?;
    let name = if cfg!(windows) {
        "ivy-session.exe"
    } else {
        "ivy-session"
    };
    let path = executable.with_file_name(name);
    if executable_file(&path) {
        return Some(path);
    }
    #[cfg(test)]
    if executable.parent()?.file_name()? == "deps" {
        let path = executable.parent()?.parent()?.join(name);
        if executable_file(&path) {
            return Some(path);
        }
    }
    None
}

fn executable_file(path: &std::path::Path) -> bool {
    let Ok(metadata) = path.metadata() else {
        return false;
    };
    if !metadata.is_file() {
        return false;
    }
    #[cfg(unix)]
    {
        use std::os::unix::fs::PermissionsExt;
        metadata.permissions().mode() & 0o111 != 0
    }
    #[cfg(not(unix))]
    {
        true
    }
}

pub(crate) fn arguments(identity: &Identity) -> Vec<String> {
    let prefix: &[&str] = match identity.client {
        Client::Codex => &["resume", "--no-daemon"],
        Client::Claude => &["--resume"],
        Client::Opencode => &["--session"],
    };
    prefix
        .iter()
        .map(|arg| (*arg).to_owned())
        .chain(std::iter::once(identity.session_id.clone()))
        .collect()
}

pub(crate) fn try_open(
    ctx: &eframe::egui::Context,
    leaf: &session::StoredLeaf,
    name: usize,
    reads: control::ReadActivity,
) -> term_core::Result<crate::Pane> {
    try_open_with(ctx, leaf, name, reads, crate::ai_sessions::resolve_launch)
}

fn try_open_with(
    ctx: &eframe::egui::Context,
    leaf: &session::StoredLeaf,
    name: usize,
    reads: control::ReadActivity,
    resolve: impl FnOnce(Client) -> std::io::Result<crate::ai_sessions::ResolvedLaunch>,
) -> term_core::Result<crate::Pane> {
    let identity = leaf.resume.as_ref().ok_or("no saved client identity")?;
    let directory = leaf
        .cwd
        .as_deref()
        .filter(|path| path.is_dir())
        .ok_or("saved client directory is unavailable")?;
    let resolved = resolve(identity.client)?;
    let program = resolved
        .program
        .to_str()
        .ok_or("client executable path is not text")?;
    let mut args = resolved.prefix_args;
    args.extend(arguments(identity));
    let mut pane = opening::launch_exact(
        ctx,
        opening::Launch {
            program,
            args: &args,
            directory: Some(directory),
            path: Some(&resolved.path),
        },
        crate::panel_or_none(panel_contract::PanelDir::create()),
        name,
        reads,
    )?;
    pane.restoration.directory = leaf.cwd.clone();
    pane.restoration.resume = leaf.resume.clone();
    pane.restoration.resuming = true;
    Ok(pane)
}

pub(crate) fn set_capture(pane: &mut crate::Pane, enabled: bool) {
    if pane.pty.pending() {
        return;
    }
    if !enabled {
        if pane.restoration.sessions.take().is_some() || pane.restoration.scope_unavailable {
            pane.restoration.route.set(None);
            pane.restoration.session_revision = 0;
            pane.restoration.session_status = None;
            pane.restoration.scope_unavailable = false;
        }
        return;
    }
    if pane.restoration.sessions.is_some() || pane.restoration.scope_unavailable {
        return;
    }
    let Some(wake) = &pane.restoration.wake else {
        return;
    };
    match pane.pty.foreground_scope() {
        Ok(scope) => {
            let handle = crate::ai_sessions::Handle::new(scope, Arc::clone(wake));
            pane.restoration.route.set(Some(handle.callback()));
            handle.set_enabled(true);
            pane.restoration.sessions = Some(handle);
        }
        Err(_) => {
            pane.restoration.scope_unavailable = true;
            pane.restoration.session_status = Some(crate::ai_sessions::Status::Unsupported);
        }
    }
}

pub(crate) fn capture(
    pane: &mut crate::Pane,
    final_snapshot: Option<crate::ai_sessions::Snapshot>,
) {
    if pane.restoration.failed {
        return;
    }
    let snapshot = final_snapshot.or_else(|| {
        pane.restoration
            .sessions
            .as_ref()?
            .snapshot_if_changed(pane.restoration.session_revision)
    });
    let Some(snapshot) = snapshot else {
        return;
    };
    pane.restoration.session_revision = snapshot.revision;
    pane.restoration.session_status = Some(snapshot.status);
    if pane.restoration.resuming
        && (!snapshot.sampled || (!pane.restoration.interacted && snapshot.identity.is_none()))
    {
        return;
    }
    if pane.restoration.resuming
        && !pane.restoration.interacted
        && pane.restoration.resume.as_ref() != snapshot.identity.as_ref()
        && snapshot.identity.is_some()
    {
        pane.restoration.failed = true;
        pane.restoration.fresh_shell_required = true;
        pane.restoration.resuming = false;
        return;
    }
    pane.restoration.session_directory = if snapshot.identity.is_some() {
        snapshot.cwd
    } else {
        None
    };
    pane.restoration.resume = snapshot.identity;
    if snapshot.sampled {
        pane.restoration.resuming = false;
    }
}

pub(crate) fn finished(pane: &crate::Pane, queue_left: bool) -> bool {
    !pane.pty.keeps_open()
        && !pane.restoration.failed
        && !pane.restoration.resuming
        && crate::pty_output::is_finished(pane.pty_output.is_alive(), queue_left, pane.pty.ending())
}

pub(crate) fn inspect_restored_exit(pane: &mut crate::Pane) {
    if !pane.restoration.resuming {
        return;
    }
    let Some(success) = pane.pty.exit_success() else {
        return;
    };
    if success {
        pane.restoration.resuming = false;
        pane.restoration.resume = None;
        if let Some(wake) = &pane.restoration.wake {
            wake();
        }
        return;
    }
    let mut nothing = Vec::new();
    let pickup = pane
        .pty_output
        .pick_up(&mut nothing, 0, crate::launch::silences(&pane.pty));
    if pane.pty_output.is_alive() || pickup.left_a_queue {
        return;
    }
    pane.restoration.failed = true;
    pane.restoration.fresh_shell_required = true;
    pane.restoration.resuming = false;
    if let Some(wake) = &pane.restoration.wake {
        wake();
    }
}

/// Preserve the failed client's output until an explicit user action starts a fresh shell.
pub(crate) fn take_over(pane: &mut crate::Pane) -> term_core::Result<()> {
    if pane.restoration.failed
        && (pane.restoration.fresh_shell_required || pane.pty.exit_success().is_some())
    {
        let wake = pane.restoration.wake.as_ref().map(Arc::clone);
        let mut identity: Vec<_> = crate::identity_of(pane.panel.as_ref())
            .into_iter()
            .collect();
        let helper = helper_path();
        identity.push((
            "IVY_SESSION_HELPER",
            helper.as_deref().map(std::path::Path::as_os_str),
        ));
        let directory = pane
            .restoration
            .directory
            .as_deref()
            .filter(|path| path.is_dir());
        let size = pane.screen.size();
        let shell = crate::the_users_shell();
        let integration = opening::prepare_shell(&shell);
        if let Some(prepared) = &integration {
            identity.extend(prepared.env());
        }
        let args: Vec<&str> = integration
            .as_ref()
            .map(|prepared| prepared.args().iter().map(String::as_str).collect())
            .unwrap_or_default();
        let launch_wake = wake.clone().unwrap_or_else(|| Arc::new(|| {}));
        let (mut pty, output) = crate::launch::Process::start(
            launch_wake,
            Default::default(),
            crate::launch::Request {
                program: &shell,
                args: &args,
                size,
                env: &identity,
                directory,
                fallback: false,
            },
        )?;
        pane.restoration.route.set(None);
        pane.restoration.sessions = None;
        pane.restoration.session_revision = 0;
        pane.restoration.session_status = None;
        pane.restoration.scope_unavailable = false;
        pty.context = pane.pty.context.clone();
        pane.pty = pty;
        pane.pty_output = crate::pty_output::PtyOutput::new(output, move || {
            if let Some(wake) = &wake {
                wake();
            }
        });
        pane._shell_integration = integration;
        pane.screen = term_core::Screen::new(size);
        pane.signals.reset_for_new_shell(&mut pane.screen);
        pane.painted_console = None;
        pane.received.clear();
        pane.search = Default::default();
        pane.pending_wheel = Default::default();
    }
    pane.restoration.take_over();
    Ok(())
}

pub(crate) fn dismiss_failure(pane: &mut crate::Pane) {
    if let Err(error) = take_over(pane) {
        eprintln!("A fresh console could not be opened: {error}");
    }
}

pub(crate) fn input_modes(pane: &crate::Pane) -> (bool, crate::keyboard::CursorKeys) {
    if pane.restoration.failed && pane.restoration.fresh_shell_required {
        (false, crate::keyboard::CursorKeys::Normal)
    } else {
        (
            pane.screen.bracketed_paste(),
            crate::keyboard::CursorKeys::of(&pane.screen),
        )
    }
}

/// Old TUI mouse reports cannot become input to the replacement shell.
pub(crate) fn write_report(pane: &mut crate::Pane, bytes: &[u8]) -> term_core::Result<()> {
    if pane.restoration.failed && pane.restoration.fresh_shell_required {
        take_over(pane)
    } else {
        write(pane, bytes)
    }
}

pub(crate) fn write_keys(pane: &mut crate::Pane) -> term_core::Result<()> {
    if pane.restoration.failed {
        take_over(pane)?;
    }
    pane.pty.write(&pane.keystrokes)
}

pub(crate) fn write(pane: &mut crate::Pane, bytes: &[u8]) -> term_core::Result<()> {
    if pane.restoration.failed {
        take_over(pane)?;
    }
    pane.pty.write(bytes)
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use std::path::PathBuf;
    use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicUsize, Ordering};
    use std::time::{Duration, Instant};

    const ID: &str = "11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111";

    fn saved(client: Client, directory: PathBuf) -> session::StoredLeaf {
        session::StoredLeaf {
            panel_width: 30,
            folded: false,
            label: None,
            cwd: Some(directory),
            resume: Some(
                Identity::new(
                    client,
                    if client == Client::Opencode {
                        "ses_Exact123"
                    } else {
                        ID
                    },
                )
                .unwrap(),
            ),
        }
    }

    #[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
    #[test]
    #[ignore = "requires the built target/debug/app and a real systemd user manager"]
    fn restored_client_fixed_arguments_and_directory_reach_a_real_isolated_scope() {
        use std::os::unix::fs::PermissionsExt;
        let _launcher = crate::launch::tests::explicit_ivy_launcher();
        let root = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("ivy-scoped-resume-{}", std::process::id()));
        std::fs::create_dir(&root).unwrap();
        let output = root.join("proof");
        let program = root.join("client");
        std::fs::write(
            &program,
            format!(
                "#!/bin/sh\nprintf '%s\\n' \"$$\" \"$PWD\" \"$@\" > '{}'\nexec /bin/cat\n",
                output.display()
            ),
        )
        .unwrap();
        std::fs::set_permissions(&program, std::fs::Permissions::from_mode(0o700)).unwrap();
        let leaf = saved(Client::Codex, root.clone());
        let mut pane = try_open_with(&Default::default(), &leaf, 0, Default::default(), |_| {
            Ok(crate::ai_sessions::ResolvedLaunch {
                program,
                prefix_args: vec![],
                path: "/usr/bin:/bin".into(),
            })
        })
        .unwrap();
        assert!(pane.pty.pending());
        let deadline = Instant::now() + Duration::from_secs(3);
        while (!output.exists() || pane.pty.pending()) && Instant::now() < deadline {
            crate::launch::refresh(&mut pane);
            std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(2));
        }
        let proof = std::fs::read_to_string(output).unwrap();
        let mut lines = proof.lines();
        let pid: u32 = lines.next().unwrap().parse().unwrap();
        assert_eq!(
            lines.collect::<Vec<_>>(),
            [root.to_str().unwrap(), "resume", "--no-daemon", ID]
        );
        let group = std::fs::read_to_string(format!("/proc/{pid}/cgroup")).unwrap();
        assert!(group.contains("/app.slice/ivy-console-"), "{group}");
        assert_ne!(group, std::fs::read_to_string("/proc/self/cgroup").unwrap());
        assert_eq!(pane.restoration.resume, leaf.resume);
        drop(pane);
        std::fs::remove_dir_all(root).unwrap();
    }

    #[test]
    fn saved_identity_is_typed_and_lower_modes_remove_it() {
        let document = serde_json::json!({"v":2,"tabs":[{"root":{"leaf":{
            "panel_width":30,"folded":false,"cwd":"/tmp/project",
            "resume":{"client":"codex","session_id":ID}
        }}}]});
        let parsed = session::interpret(&document.to_string()).unwrap();
        let session::StoredNode::Leaf(leaf) = &parsed.tabs()[0].root else {
            panic!("leaf");
        };
        assert_eq!(leaf.resume.as_ref().unwrap().session_id, ID);
        let directories = parsed.clone().without_sessions();
        let session::StoredNode::Leaf(leaf) = &directories.tabs()[0].root else {
            panic!("leaf");
        };
        assert!(leaf.resume.is_none());
        assert_eq!(
            leaf.cwd.as_deref(),
            Some(std::path::Path::new("/tmp/project"))
        );
        let layout = parsed.without_directories();
        let session::StoredNode::Leaf(leaf) = &layout.tabs()[0].root else {
            panic!("leaf");
        };
        assert!(leaf.resume.is_none() && leaf.cwd.is_none());
        for resume in [
            serde_json::json!({"client":"sh","session_id":ID}),
            serde_json::json!({"client":"codex","session_id":"--continue"}),
            serde_json::json!({"client":"claude","session_id":ID,"argv":["danger"]}),
            serde_json::json!({"client":"opencode","session_id":"ses_a;touch forbidden"}),
        ] {
            let mut invalid = document.clone();
            invalid["tabs"][0]["root"]["leaf"]["resume"] = resume;
            assert!(session::interpret(&invalid.to_string()).is_err());
        }
    }

    crate::windows_unix_omission!(
        every_client_reaches_the_child_with_only_fixed_arguments_and_a_structured_cwd,
        "unix-only: runs executable POSIX shell fixtures to inspect child arguments and working directory"
    );

    #[cfg(unix)]
    #[test]

    fn every_client_reaches_the_child_with_only_fixed_arguments_and_a_structured_cwd() {
        use std::os::unix::fs::PermissionsExt;
        static NEXT: AtomicUsize = AtomicUsize::new(0);
        let root = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!(
            "ivy-resume-{}-{}",
            std::process::id(),
            NEXT.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed)
        ));
        std::fs::create_dir(&root).unwrap();
        let directory = root.join("directory with spaces;$literal");
        std::fs::create_dir(&directory).unwrap();
        let binary = root.join("client");
        let output = root.join("arguments");
        std::fs::write(
            &binary,
            format!(
                "#!/bin/sh\nprintf '%s\\n' \"$PWD\" \"$@\" > '{}'\nexec /bin/cat\n",
                output.display()
            ),
        )
        .unwrap();
        std::fs::set_permissions(&binary, std::fs::Permissions::from_mode(0o700)).unwrap();
        for client in [Client::Codex, Client::Claude, Client::Opencode] {
            let leaf = saved(client, directory.clone());
            let pane = try_open_with(&Default::default(), &leaf, 0, Default::default(), |_| {
                Ok(crate::ai_sessions::ResolvedLaunch {
                    program: binary.clone(),
                    prefix_args: vec![],
                    path: "/usr/bin:/bin".into(),
                })
            })
            .unwrap();
            let deadline = Instant::now() + Duration::from_secs(2);
            let expected_args: Vec<&str> = match client {
                Client::Codex => vec!["resume", "--no-daemon", ID],
                Client::Claude => vec!["--resume", ID],
                Client::Opencode => vec!["--session", "ses_Exact123"],
            };
            let expected = std::iter::once(directory.to_str().unwrap())
                .chain(expected_args)
                .collect::<Vec<_>>()
                .join("\n")
                + "\n";
            while std::fs::read_to_string(&output).ok().as_deref() != Some(&expected)
                && Instant::now() < deadline
            {
                std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(2));
            }
            let actual = std::fs::read_to_string(&output).unwrap();
            assert_eq!(actual, expected);
            assert!(pane.keystrokes.is_empty());
            assert_eq!(pane.restoration.resume, leaf.resume);
            drop(pane);
            std::fs::remove_file(&output).unwrap();
        }
        std::fs::remove_dir_all(root).unwrap();
    }

    crate::windows_unix_omission!(
        failed_resume_retains_output_until_explicit_fresh_shell_takeover,
        "unix-only: launches /bin/sh with POSIX commands to exercise failed-resume takeover"
    );

    #[cfg(unix)]
    #[test]

    fn failed_resume_retains_output_until_explicit_fresh_shell_takeover() {
        let leaf = saved(Client::Claude, std::env::temp_dir());
        let mut pane = try_open_with(&Default::default(), &leaf, 0, Default::default(), |_| {
            Ok(crate::ai_sessions::ResolvedLaunch {
                program: "/bin/sh".into(),
                prefix_args: vec![
                    "-c".into(),
                    "printf 'resume failed\\r\\n'; exit 7".into(),
                    "fixture".into(),
                ],
                path: "/usr/bin:/bin".into(),
            })
        })
        .unwrap();
        let deadline = Instant::now() + Duration::from_secs(2);
        let mut output = Vec::new();
        while Instant::now() < deadline {
            pane.pty_output
                .pick_up(&mut output, 65536, crate::launch::silences(&pane.pty));
            inspect_restored_exit(&mut pane);
            if pane.restoration.failed {
                break;
            }
            std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(2));
        }
        assert!(pane.restoration.failed);
        assert_eq!(pane.pty.exit_success(), Some(false));
        assert_eq!(pane.restoration.resume, leaf.resume);
        assert!(String::from_utf8_lossy(&output).contains("resume failed"));
        assert!(
            !finished(&pane, false),
            "a failed resume must keep its window open"
        );
        pane.screen.advance(b"\x1b[?2004h\x1b[?1h");
        assert!(pane.screen.bracketed_paste());
        take_over(&mut pane).unwrap();
        assert!(!pane.restoration.failed);
        assert!(pane.restoration.resume.is_none());
        assert!(!pane.screen.bracketed_paste());
        assert!(!pane.screen.application_cursor_keys());
        assert!(
            pane.pty.exit_success().is_none(),
            "the fresh shell is alive"
        );
        pane.pty.write(b"exit\r").unwrap();
    }

    fn wait_for_fresh_shell_output(pane: &mut crate::Pane) {
        let deadline = Instant::now() + Duration::from_secs(2);
        let mut output = Vec::new();
        while Instant::now() < deadline {
            pane.pty_output
                .pick_up(&mut output, 65536, crate::launch::silences(&pane.pty));
            if output
                .windows(b"fresh-shell-output".len())
                .any(|bytes| bytes == b"fresh-shell-output")
            {
                return;
            }
            std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(2));
        }
        panic!("the first personal input did not reach the replacement shell");
    }

    crate::windows_unix_omission!(
        a_wrong_live_session_is_replaced_before_notice_keyboard_clipboard_or_mouse_input,
        "unix-only: uses /bin/cat and a POSIX shell to verify raw input reaches a fresh takeover process"
    );
    #[cfg(unix)]
    #[test]
    fn a_wrong_live_session_is_replaced_before_notice_keyboard_clipboard_or_mouse_input() {
        let _shell = crate::shell_integration::TestShell::use_program("/bin/sh");
        for input in ["notice", "keyboard", "clipboard", "mouse"] {
            let mut pane = crate::Pane::launching(&Default::default(), "/bin/cat", 0).unwrap();
            pane.restoration.resume = saved(Client::Claude, std::env::temp_dir()).resume;
            pane.restoration.directory = Some(std::env::temp_dir());
            pane.restoration.resuming = true;
            pane.screen.advance(b"\x1b[?2004h\x1b[?1h");
            capture(
                &mut pane,
                Some(crate::ai_sessions::Snapshot {
                    identity: Some(
                        Identity::new(Client::Claude, "22222222-2222-4222-8222-222222222222")
                            .unwrap(),
                    ),
                    cwd: Some(std::env::temp_dir()),
                    revision: 1,
                    status: crate::ai_sessions::Status::Registered,
                    sampled: true,
                }),
            );
            assert!(pane.restoration.failed);
            assert!(
                pane.pty.exit_success().is_none(),
                "this is a live mismatching client"
            );
            assert!(!input_modes(&pane).0);
            assert_eq!(input_modes(&pane).1, crate::keyboard::CursorKeys::Normal);
            let command = "printf 'fresh''-shell-output\\n'\r";
            match input {
                "notice" => {
                    dismiss_failure(&mut pane);
                    pane.pty.write(command.as_bytes()).unwrap();
                }
                "keyboard" => {
                    pane.keystrokes = command.as_bytes().to_vec();
                    write_keys(&mut pane).unwrap();
                }
                "clipboard" => crate::keyboard::paste_into(&mut pane, command),
                "mouse" => {
                    write_report(&mut pane, b"UNINTENDED_REPORT").unwrap();
                    pane.pty.write(command.as_bytes()).unwrap();
                }
                _ => unreachable!(),
            }
            assert!(!pane.restoration.failed);
            assert!(pane.restoration.resume.is_none());
            assert!(!pane.screen.bracketed_paste());
            wait_for_fresh_shell_output(&mut pane);
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn attach_without_a_sample_cannot_replace_an_initial_resume_target() {
        let mut pane = crate::Pane::launching(&Default::default(), "/bin/cat", 0).unwrap();
        let identity = saved(Client::Claude, std::env::temp_dir()).resume;
        pane.restoration.resume = identity.clone();
        pane.restoration.resuming = true;
        capture(
            &mut pane,
            Some(crate::ai_sessions::Snapshot {
                identity: None,
                cwd: None,
                revision: 1,
                status: crate::ai_sessions::Status::Registered,
                sampled: false,
            }),
        );
        assert_eq!(pane.restoration.resume, identity);
        assert!(pane.restoration.resuming);
        capture(
            &mut pane,
            Some(crate::ai_sessions::Snapshot {
                identity: identity.clone(),
                cwd: Some(std::env::temp_dir()),
                revision: 2,
                status: crate::ai_sessions::Status::Registered,
                sampled: true,
            }),
        );
        assert_eq!(pane.restoration.resume, identity);
        assert!(!pane.restoration.resuming);
    }

    #[test]
    fn missing_client_directory_keeps_the_target_visible_until_takeover() {
        let missing =
            std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("ivy-missing-ai-directory-{}", std::process::id()));
        let leaf = saved(Client::Claude, missing);
        let mut pane = crate::restoration::open_leaf(
            &Default::default(),
            &leaf,
            crate::shell_integration::fallback_shell(),
            0,
            Default::default(),
        )
        .unwrap();
        assert!(pane.restoration.failed);
        assert_eq!(pane.restoration.resume, leaf.resume);
        assert_eq!(
            crate::painter::notices::what_the_silence_is(&pane),
            Some(crate::painter::notices::Silence::RestoreDirectory)
        );
        pane.restoration.take_over();
        assert!(pane.restoration.resume.is_none());
        assert!(pane.restoration.directory.is_none());
    }

    #[cfg(windows)]
    #[test]
    #[ignore = "unix-only: requires the built target/debug/app and a real systemd user manager"]
    fn restored_client_fixed_arguments_and_directory_reach_a_real_isolated_scope() {}
}

### crates/app/src/session/ownership.rs

```
//! Coordinates independent windows without transferring ownership during their lifetime.

#[cfg(not(unix))]
use std::fs::TryLockError;
use std::fs::{self, File, OpenOptions};
use std::io::{self, ErrorKind};
use std::path::Path;

/// Holds this window's operating-system lease until it is dropped.
pub(crate) struct Ownership {
    _lease: File,
    primary: bool,
}

impl Ownership {
    /// Acquire a lease in the application's configuration directory.
    /// Any failure leaves ownership undecided; callers must disable persistence.
    pub(crate) fn acquire(config_dir: &Path) -> io::Result<Self> {
        let directory = config_dir.join("window-leases");
        super::create_directory(&directory)?;
        let coordinator = OpenOptions::new()
            .read(true)
            .write(true)
            .create(true)
            .truncate(false)
            .open(directory.join("coordinator"))?;
        lock(&coordinator, true)?;

        let mut primary = true;
        for entry in fs::read_dir(&directory)? {
            let entry = entry?;
            let name = entry.file_name();
            let Some(name) = name.to_str() else { continue };
            if !name.starts_with("lease-") {
                continue;
            }
            let file = OpenOptions::new()
                .read(true)
                .write(true)
                .open(entry.path())?;
            match try_lock(&file)? {
                true => {
                    drop(file);
                    fs::remove_file(entry.path())?;
                }
                false => primary = false,
            }
        }

        let lease = loop {
            let mut random = [0u8; 16];
            getrandom::fill(&mut random).map_err(|error| io::Error::other(error.to_string()))?;
            let name = random
                .iter()
                .map(|b| format!("{b:02x}"))
                .collect::<String>();
            match OpenOptions::new()
                .read(true)
                .write(true)
                .create_new(true)
                .open(directory.join(format!("lease-{name}")))
            {
                Ok(file) => break file,
                Err(error) if error.kind() == ErrorKind::AlreadyExists => continue,
                Err(error) => return Err(error),
            }
        };
        lock(&lease, true)?;
        // Dropping the coordinator releases the startup lock only after the lease is held.
        unlock(&coordinator)?;
        drop(coordinator);
        Ok(Self {
            _lease: lease,
            primary,
        })
    }

    pub(crate) fn is_primary(&self) -> bool {
        self.primary
    }
}

impl Drop for Ownership {
    fn drop(&mut self) {
        // Release the lease before closing its handle.
        let _ = unlock(&self._lease);
    }
}

// POSIX record locks belong to the process and are not inherited across fork. A crash
// therefore releases the lease even if a child has inherited its descriptor before exec.
#[cfg(unix)]
fn set_lock(file: &File, kind: libc::c_short, wait: bool) -> io::Result<bool> {
    use std::os::fd::AsRawFd;
    let mut request: libc::flock = unsafe { std::mem::zeroed() };
    request.l_type = kind;
    request.l_whence = libc::SEEK_SET as libc::c_short;
    loop {
        let result = unsafe {
            libc::fcntl(
                file.as_raw_fd(),
                if wait { libc::F_SETLKW } else { libc::F_SETLK },
                &request,
            )
        };
        if result != -1 {
            return Ok(true);
        }
        let error = io::Error::last_os_error();
        if error.kind() == ErrorKind::Interrupted {
            continue;
        }
        if !wait
            && matches!(
                error.raw_os_error(),
                Some(libc::EACCES) | Some(libc::EAGAIN)
            )
        {
            return Ok(false);
        }
        return Err(error);
    }
}

#[cfg(unix)]
fn lock(file: &File, wait: bool) -> io::Result<()> {
    set_lock(file, libc::F_WRLCK as libc::c_short, wait).map(|_| ())
}
#[cfg(unix)]
fn try_lock(file: &File) -> io::Result<bool> {
    set_lock(file, libc::F_WRLCK as libc::c_short, false)
}
#[cfg(unix)]
fn unlock(file: &File) -> io::Result<()> {
    set_lock(file, libc::F_UNLCK as libc::c_short, false).map(|_| ())
}

#[cfg(not(unix))]
fn lock(file: &File, _wait: bool) -> io::Result<()> {
    file.lock()
}
#[cfg(not(unix))]
fn try_lock(file: &File) -> io::Result<bool> {
    match file.try_lock() {
        Ok(()) => Ok(true),
        Err(TryLockError::WouldBlock) => Ok(false),
        Err(TryLockError::Error(error)) => Err(error),
    }
}
#[cfg(not(unix))]
fn unlock(file: &File) -> io::Result<()> {
    file.unlock()
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use std::io::{BufRead, BufReader, Read, Write};
    use std::process::{Child, ChildStdin, Command, Stdio};

    #[test]
    fn ownership_child() {
        let Ok(directory) = std::env::var("IVY_OWNERSHIP_TEST_DIRECTORY") else {
            return;
        };
        let mut gate = [0];
        io::stdin().read_exact(&mut gate).unwrap();
        let ownership = Ownership::acquire(Path::new(&directory)).unwrap();
        println!("OWNERSHIP_ROLE={}", ownership.is_primary());
        io::stdout().flush().unwrap();
        io::stdin().read_exact(&mut gate).unwrap();
    }

    crate::windows_unix_omission!(
        fork_owner_child,
        "unix-only: forks a child process to verify owner lease lifetime"
    );

    #[cfg(unix)]
    #[test]

    fn fork_owner_child() {
        let Ok(directory) = std::env::var("IVY_FORK_TEST_DIRECTORY") else {
            return;
        };
        let owner = Ownership::acquire(Path::new(&directory)).unwrap();
        assert!(owner.is_primary());
        // The child performs only async-signal-safe operations before it exits.
        let pid = unsafe { libc::fork() };
        assert!(pid >= 0);
        if pid == 0 {
            unsafe {
                libc::pause();
                libc::_exit(0)
            }
        }
        println!("FORK_CHILD={pid}");
        io::stdout().flush().unwrap();
        let mut gate = [0];
        let _ = io::stdin().read_exact(&mut gate);
    }

    crate::windows_unix_omission!(
        crashed_owner_releases_lease_while_fork_child_lives,
        "unix-only: forks a child and signals its parent to test owner lease release"
    );

    #[cfg(unix)]
    #[test]

    fn crashed_owner_releases_lease_while_fork_child_lives() {
        let mut random = [0u8; 16];
        getrandom::fill(&mut random).unwrap();
        let directory = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("ivy-ownership-fork-{:?}", random));
        let mut owner = Command::new(std::env::current_exe().unwrap())
            .args(["fork_owner_child", "--nocapture"])
            .env("IVY_FORK_TEST_DIRECTORY", &directory)
            .stdin(Stdio::piped())
            .stdout(Stdio::piped())
            .spawn()
            .unwrap();
        let mut output = BufReader::new(owner.stdout.take().unwrap());
        let child_pid: libc::pid_t = loop {
            let mut line = String::new();
            assert_ne!(output.read_line(&mut line).unwrap(), 0);
            if let Some(pid) = line.split("FORK_CHILD=").nth(1) {
                break pid.trim().parse().unwrap();
            }
        };
        owner.kill().unwrap();
        owner.wait().unwrap();
        let mut replacement = Window::spawn(&directory);
        replacement.start();
        let primary = replacement.role();
        unsafe {
            libc::kill(child_pid, libc::SIGKILL);
        }
        replacement.close();
        fs::remove_dir_all(directory).unwrap();
        assert!(
            primary,
            "a forked child must not retain its crashed parent's lease"
        );
    }

    struct Window {
        child: Child,
        input: ChildStdin,
        output: BufReader<std::process::ChildStdout>,
    }

    impl Window {
        fn spawn(directory: &Path) -> Self {
            let mut child = Command::new(std::env::current_exe().unwrap())
                .args(["ownership_child", "--nocapture"])
                .env("IVY_OWNERSHIP_TEST_DIRECTORY", directory)
                .stdin(Stdio::piped())
                .stdout(Stdio::piped())
                .spawn()
                .unwrap();
            let input = child.stdin.take().unwrap();
            let output = BufReader::new(child.stdout.take().unwrap());
            Self {
                child,
                input,
                output,
            }
        }

        fn start(&mut self) {
            self.input.write_all(b"x").unwrap();
        }

        fn role(&mut self) -> bool {
            loop {
                let mut line = String::new();
                assert_ne!(self.output.read_line(&mut line).unwrap(), 0, "child exited");
                if let Some(role) = line.split("OWNERSHIP_ROLE=").nth(1) {
                    return role.trim() == "true";
                }
            }
        }

        fn close(mut self) {
            self.input.write_all(b"x").unwrap();
            assert!(self.child.wait().unwrap().success());
        }

        fn crash(mut self) {
            self.child.kill().unwrap();
            self.child.wait().unwrap();
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn independent_processes_keep_roles_until_all_exit() {
        let mut random = [0u8; 16];
        getrandom::fill(&mut random).unwrap();
        let directory = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("ivy-ownership-{:?}", random));
        fs::create_dir(&directory).unwrap();

        let mut first = Window::spawn(&directory);
        let mut second = Window::spawn(&directory);
        first.start();
        second.start();
        let first_role = first.role();
        let second_role = second.role();
        assert_ne!(first_role, second_role);
        let (primary, secondary) = if first_role {
            (first, second)
        } else {
            (second, first)
        };
        primary.close();
        let mut third = Window::spawn(&directory);
        third.start();
        assert!(!third.role());
        secondary.close();
        third.crash();

        let mut primary = Window::spawn(&directory);
        primary.start();
        assert!(primary.role());
        let mut secondary = Window::spawn(&directory);
        secondary.start();
        assert!(!secondary.role());
        secondary.close();
        let mut another = Window::spawn(&directory);
        another.start();
        assert!(!another.role());
        primary.crash();
        another.close();
        let mut last = Window::spawn(&directory);
        last.start();
        assert!(last.role());
        last.close();
        fs::remove_dir_all(directory).unwrap();
    }
}
