### crates/app/src/settings/editor.rs

```
//! The window-level configuration transaction and its input ownership.
use super::{document::Document, store::Store};
use eframe::egui;
use std::{
    path::PathBuf,
    time::{Duration, Instant},
};
mod probe;
pub(crate) mod runtime;
mod script;
mod timing;
pub(crate) mod view;
use timing::Timing;

#[derive(Default)]
pub(crate) struct Editor {
    enabled: bool,
    entry_rect: Option<egui::Rect>,
    font_rect: Option<egui::Rect>,
    script: Option<script::Script>,
    probe: Option<probe::Probe>,
    measuring: bool,
    regime: &'static str,
    font_requests: usize,
    pub(super) preview: view::typography::PreviewState,
    pub(super) draft: Option<Document>,
    pub(super) store: Option<Store>,
    pub(super) message: String,
    pub(super) fonts: Option<Vec<String>>,
    pub(super) section: usize,
    pub(super) picker_open: bool,
    pub(super) picker_query: String,
    pub(super) picker_filter: usize,
    blocked_frame: Option<u64>,
    held_pointer: bool,
    timing: Timing,
    application: runtime::Application,
    pub(super) saved_startup_restore: Option<super::StartupRestore>,
}

impl Editor {
    pub(crate) fn open_from_chrome(&mut self) {
        if self.enabled && !self.is_open() {
            self.open(super::path());
        }
    }
    /// Open the same editor on its keyboard bindings page.
    pub(crate) fn open_shortcuts_from_chrome(&mut self) {
        self.open_from_chrome();
        self.section = 2;
    }
    pub fn resolved(entry: super::Entry, mode: Option<&str>) -> Self {
        let measuring = matches!(entry, super::Entry::Measuring);
        Self {
            enabled: !measuring || matches!(mode, Some("closed" | "open" | "fonts" | "apply")),
            measuring,
            probe: (measuring && mode == Some("apply")).then(probe::Probe::default),
            regime: match mode {
                Some("closed") => "closed",
                Some("open") => "open",
                Some("fonts") => "fonts",
                Some("apply") => "apply",
                _ => "historical",
            },
            script: if measuring {
                script::Script::for_mode(mode)
            } else {
                None
            },
            ..Self::default()
        }
    }

    fn open(&mut self, path: Option<PathBuf>) {
        let started = Instant::now();
        self.message.clear();
        self.preview = Default::default();
        self.picker_open = false;
        self.picker_query.clear();
        self.picker_filter = 0;
        self.fonts = None;
        self.store = None;
        match path
            .ok_or_else(|| "The configuration directory is unavailable.".to_owned())
            .and_then(Store::open)
        {
            Ok((store, text)) => {
                if let Ok((_, warnings)) = super::interpret(&text) {
                    self.message = warnings
                        .kept()
                        .iter()
                        .map(ToString::to_string)
                        .collect::<Vec<_>>()
                        .join("\n");
                }
                self.store = Some(store);
                self.draft = Some(Document::new(text));
            }
            Err(error) => {
                self.message = error;
                self.draft = Some(Document::new(String::new()));
            }
        }
        self.timing.record_work(2, started.elapsed());
    }

    pub(super) fn save(&mut self, fonts: &mut crate::fonts::Discoverer) -> bool {
        runtime::save(self, fonts)
    }

    pub(super) fn save_in_frame(
        &mut self,
        fonts: &mut crate::fonts::Discoverer,
        ctx: &egui::Context,
    ) -> bool {
        let frame = ctx.cumulative_frame_nr();
        if let Some((saved_frame, outcome)) = self.application.saved_frame
            && saved_frame == frame
        {
            return outcome;
        }
        let outcome = self.save(fonts);
        self.application.saved_frame = Some((frame, outcome));
        outcome
    }

    pub(crate) fn take_saved_startup_restore(&mut self) -> Option<super::StartupRestore> {
        self.saved_startup_restore.take()
    }

    pub(crate) fn remember_family(&mut self, settings: &super::Settings) {
        self.application.family = settings.font.clone();
    }

    /// Runs before console input. A closing frame and its held release remain owned.
    pub fn show(
        &mut self,
        ui: &mut egui::Ui,
        discoverer: &mut crate::fonts::Discoverer,
        gear: egui::Rect,
    ) -> bool {
        if !self.enabled {
            return false;
        }
        let started = Instant::now();
        let ctx = ui.ctx().clone();
        let frame = ctx.cumulative_frame_nr();
        let was_open = self.is_open();
        let over_row = ctx.input(|i| i.pointer.interact_pos().is_some_and(|p| gear.contains(p)));
        let mut opening = false;
        if gear.is_positive() {
            let response = ui
                .interact(gear, ui.id().with("settings-gear"), egui::Sense::CLICK)
                .on_hover_text("Settings");
            self.entry_rect = Some(gear);
            opening = response.clicked();
            let painter = ui.painter_at(gear);
            painter.rect_filled(gear, 0.0, egui::Color32::from_rgb(35, 41, 52));
            let ink = if opening || response.hovered() {
                egui::Color32::from_rgb(173, 195, 255)
            } else {
                egui::Color32::from_rgb(153, 163, 182)
            };
            // A square of 0.55 of the shorter side, eighteen points at most: the size at which the
            // gear read as the drawn one did at the desktop check.
            let size = (gear.width().min(gear.height()) * 0.55).min(18.0);
            crate::ui::icons::paint(
                &painter,
                gear.center(),
                crate::ui::icons::Icon::Gear,
                size,
                ink,
            );
        }
        self.timing.record_work(0, started.elapsed());
        if opening {
            if self.measuring {
                self.draft = Some(Document::new(String::new()));
                self.message = "Measurement: configuration I/O is disabled.".into();
            } else {
                self.open(super::path());
            }
        }
        let row_gesture = over_row
            && ctx.input(|i| {
                i.pointer.any_pressed()
                    || i.pointer.any_released()
                    || i.smooth_scroll_delta != egui::Vec2::ZERO
            });
        let owned = was_open
            || opening
            || row_gesture
            || self.held_pointer
            || self.blocked_frame == Some(frame);
        if self.draft.is_some() {
            let started = Instant::now();
            let child_before = self.timing.work;
            view::show(self, &ctx, discoverer);
            let children = self.timing.work.saturating_sub(child_before);
            self.timing
                .record_work(1, started.elapsed().saturating_sub(children));
        }
        if owned {
            self.blocked_frame = Some(frame);
            self.held_pointer = ctx.input(|i| i.focused && i.pointer.any_down());
        }
        owned
    }

    #[cfg(test)]
    pub(crate) fn entry_rect(&self) -> egui::Rect {
        self.entry_rect.expect("the entry was painted")
    }

    pub fn is_open(&self) -> bool {
        self.draft.is_some()
    }

    pub(crate) fn chrome_visible(&self) -> bool {
        self.enabled
    }

    pub(crate) fn record_geometry(&mut self, cost: Duration) {
        self.timing.record_work(7, cost);
    }

    pub fn close_frame(&mut self) {
        self.timing.close_frame();
    }
    pub fn take_cost(&mut self) -> Duration {
        std::mem::take(&mut self.timing.work)
    }
    pub fn publish(&self, to: &mut dyn std::io::Write) -> std::io::Result<()> {
        writeln!(
            to,
            "settings UI: visible={}, measurement={}",
            self.enabled, self.measuring
        )?;
        writeln!(
            to,
            "settings chrome: {}",
            if self.enabled {
                "compact-tab-strip"
            } else {
                "historical"
            }
        )?;
        if let Some(probe) = &self.probe {
            probe.publish(to)?;
        }
        writeln!(
            to,
            "settings mode: {}",
            if !self.enabled {
                "historical"
            } else if !self.measuring {
                "interactive"
            } else {
                self.regime
            }
        )?;
        writeln!(
            to,
            "settings installed families: {}",
            self.fonts.as_ref().map_or(0, Vec::len)
        )?;
        writeln!(to, "settings font requests: {}", self.font_requests)?;
        runtime::publish(&self.application, to)?;
        self.timing.publish(to)
    }
}

/// All settings gestures are consumed before the terminal's event dispatcher sees them.
pub(crate) fn before_keys(
    window: &mut crate::Window,
    ui: &mut egui::Ui,
    metrics: &crate::painter::CellMetrics,
) -> Vec<crate::WindowRequest> {
    if !window.settings_editor.enabled {
        return window.send_keys_to_the_focused(ui.ctx());
    }
    let started = Instant::now();
    let gear = crate::tab_bar::geometry(window, ui.max_rect(), metrics)
        .gear
        .expect("enabled settings has a gear");
    let owned = window
        .settings_editor
        .show(ui, &mut window.discoverer, gear);
    let measured = window.settings_editor.timing.work;
    if owned {
        let ctx = ui.ctx();
        window.modifiers = ctx.input(|i| {
            if i.focused {
                i.modifiers
            } else {
                egui::Modifiers::NONE
            }
        });
        window
            .header_input
            .block_for_settings(ctx.cumulative_frame_nr());
        ctx.input_mut(|i| {
            i.events.retain(|e| {
                matches!(
                    e,
                    egui::Event::ModifiersChanged(_)
                        | egui::Event::WindowFocused(_)
                        | egui::Event::PointerButton { pressed: false, .. }
                )
            })
        });
    }
    window
        .settings_editor
        .timing
        .record_work(4, started.elapsed().saturating_sub(measured));
    window.timed_separately += window.settings_editor.take_cost();
    if owned {
        Vec::new()
    } else {
        window.send_keys_to_the_focused(ui.ctx())
    }
}

pub(crate) fn inject(window: &mut crate::Window, raw: &mut egui::RawInput) {
    let Some(script) = &mut window.settings_editor.script else {
        return;
    };
    let started = Instant::now();
    raw.events.extend(script.next(
        window.settings_editor.entry_rect,
        window.settings_editor.font_rect,
    ));
    window.spent_injecting += started.elapsed();
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use std::{
        fs,
        sync::atomic::{AtomicU64, Ordering},
    };
    static NEXT: AtomicU64 = AtomicU64::new(0);
    #[test]
    fn startup_save_and_cancel_keep_the_saved_choice() {
        let path = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!(
            "startup-editor-{}-{}",
            std::process::id(),
            NEXT.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed)
        ));
        fs::write(&path, "# keep\nstartup.restore = layout\n").unwrap();
        let mut editor = Editor::resolved(super::super::Entry::Normal, None);
        let mut fonts = crate::fonts::Discoverer::default();
        editor.open(Some(path.clone()));
        editor
            .draft
            .as_mut()
            .unwrap()
            .fields
            .iter_mut()
            .find(|f| f.key == "startup.restore")
            .unwrap()
            .value = "directories".into();
        assert!(editor.save(&mut fonts));
        assert_eq!(
            editor.take_saved_startup_restore(),
            Some(super::super::StartupRestore::Directories)
        );
        assert_eq!(
            super::super::interpret(&fs::read_to_string(&path).unwrap())
                .unwrap()
                .0
                .startup_restore,
            super::super::StartupRestore::Directories
        );
        editor.draft = None;
        editor.store = None;
        editor.open(Some(path.clone()));
        editor
            .draft
            .as_mut()
            .unwrap()
            .fields
            .iter_mut()
            .find(|f| f.key == "startup.restore")
            .unwrap()
            .value = "none".into();
        let ctx = egui::Context::default();
        ctx.run_ui(
            egui::RawInput {
                events: vec![egui::Event::Key {
                    key: egui::Key::Escape,
                    physical_key: None,
                    pressed: true,
                    repeat: false,
                    modifiers: egui::Modifiers::NONE,
                }],
                ..Default::default()
            },
            |ui| {
                editor.show(ui, &mut fonts, ui.max_rect());
            },
        )
        .drop_without_applying_deltas();
        assert_eq!(editor.take_saved_startup_restore(), None);
        assert_eq!(
            super::super::interpret(&fs::read_to_string(&path).unwrap())
                .unwrap()
                .0
                .startup_restore,
            super::super::StartupRestore::Directories
        );
        fs::remove_file(path).unwrap();
    }

    struct RestorePolicyFixture {
        directory: PathBuf,
        snapshot: PathBuf,
        config: PathBuf,
    }

    impl RestorePolicyFixture {
        fn new(mode: super::super::StartupRestore) -> Self {
            let directory = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!(
                "settings-restore-policy-{}-{}",
                std::process::id(),
                NEXT.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed)
            ));
            fs::create_dir(&directory).unwrap();
            let fixture = Self {
                snapshot: directory.join("layout.json"),
                config: directory.join("config"),
                directory,
            };
            let value = match mode {
                super::super::StartupRestore::None => "none",
                super::super::StartupRestore::Layout => "layout",
                super::super::StartupRestore::Directories => "directories",
                super::super::StartupRestore::Sessions => "sessions",
            };
            fs::write(&fixture.config, format!("startup.restore = {value}\n")).unwrap();
            fixture
        }

        fn seed(&self, version: u64) -> Vec<u8> {
            let document = crate::session::StoredDocument {
                v: version,
                tabs: vec![crate::session::StoredTab {
                    label: None,
                    root: crate::session::StoredNode::Leaf(crate::session::StoredLeaf {
                        resume: None,
                        panel_width: 37,
                        folded: true,
                        label: Some("Saved console".into()),
                        cwd: Some(self.directory.clone()),
                    }),
                }],
            };
            let bytes = crate::session::serialize(&document).unwrap().into_bytes();
            fs::write(&self.snapshot, &bytes).unwrap();
            bytes
        }

        fn open(&self, mode: super::super::StartupRestore) -> crate::Window {
            let mut persistence = crate::session::Persistence::at(Some(self.snapshot.clone()));
            let (opening, state) = crate::restoration::prepare(
                &mut persistence,
                Some(&self.snapshot),
                mode,
                false,
                None,
                None,
            );
            let mut window = crate::Window::opening(
                &Default::default(),
                opening,
                crate::shell_integration::fallback_shell(),
                None,
                persistence,
            )
            .unwrap();
            window.restoration = state;
            window
        }

        fn save_mode(&self, window: &mut crate::Window, mode: &str) {
            window.settings_editor.open(Some(self.config.clone()));
            window
                .settings_editor
                .draft
                .as_mut()
                .unwrap()
                .fields
                .iter_mut()
                .find(|field| field.key == "startup.restore")
                .unwrap()
                .value = mode.into();
            assert!(window.settings_editor.save(&mut window.discoverer));
            assert!(window.settings_editor.message.starts_with("Saved."));
        }

        fn queue_expired_change(window: &mut crate::Window) {
            window.active_mut().panes_mut()[0].label = Some("Changed console".into());
            window.check_whether_the_layout_moved();
            assert!(window.persistence.save_due.is_some());
            window.persistence.save_due = Some(Instant::now() - Duration::from_secs(1));
        }
    }

    impl Drop for RestorePolicyFixture {
        fn drop(&mut self) {
            let _ = fs::remove_dir_all(&self.directory);
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn saving_directories_removes_ai_identity_before_an_expired_snapshot_write() {
        use panel_control::session::{Client, Identity};
        let fixture = RestorePolicyFixture::new(super::super::StartupRestore::Sessions);
        fixture.seed(crate::session::VERSION);
        let mut window = fixture.open(super::super::StartupRestore::Directories);
        fixture.save_mode(&mut window, "sessions");
        crate::restoration::apply_saved_mode(&mut window);
        window.active_mut().panes_mut()[0].restoration.resume =
            Some(Identity::new(Client::Claude, "11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111").unwrap());
        window.check_whether_the_layout_moved();
        window.save_now();
        let stored = crate::session::read(&fixture.snapshot).unwrap();
        assert!(
            matches!(&stored.tabs()[0].root, crate::session::StoredNode::Leaf(leaf) if leaf.resume.is_some())
        );
        RestorePolicyFixture::queue_expired_change(&mut window);
        fixture.save_mode(&mut window, "directories");
        window.save_now();
        assert!(window.active().panes()[0].restoration.resume.is_none());
        window.check_whether_the_layout_moved();
        window.save_now();
        let stored = crate::session::read(&fixture.snapshot).unwrap();
        assert!(
            matches!(&stored.tabs()[0].root, crate::session::StoredNode::Leaf(leaf) if leaf.resume.is_none() && leaf.cwd.as_ref() == Some(&fixture.directory))
        );
        assert!(window.active().panes()[0].restoration.resume.is_none());
    }

    crate::windows_unix_omission!(
        lowering_recovery_after_a_wrong_live_session_still_replaces_it_before_input,
        "unix-only: uses /bin/cat and a POSIX shell to verify keyboard and clipboard input after recovery"
    );
    #[cfg(unix)]
    #[test]
    fn lowering_recovery_after_a_wrong_live_session_still_replaces_it_before_input() {
        let _shell = crate::shell_integration::TestShell::use_program("/bin/sh");
        use panel_control::session::{Client, Identity};
        for mode in ["directories", "layout", "none"] {
            for clipboard in [false, true] {
                let fixture = RestorePolicyFixture::new(super::super::StartupRestore::Sessions);
                fixture.seed(crate::session::VERSION);
                let mut window = fixture.open(super::super::StartupRestore::Sessions);
                let mut pane = crate::Pane::launching(&Default::default(), "/bin/cat", 0).unwrap();
                pane.restoration.resume = Some(
                    Identity::new(Client::Claude, "11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111").unwrap(),
                );
                pane.restoration.directory = Some(fixture.directory.clone());
                pane.restoration.resuming = true;
                pane.screen.advance(b"\x1b[?2004h");
                crate::resume::capture(
                    &mut pane,
                    Some(crate::ai_sessions::Snapshot {
                        identity: Some(
                            Identity::new(Client::Claude, "22222222-2222-4222-8222-222222222222")
                                .unwrap(),
                        ),
                        cwd: Some(fixture.directory.clone()),
                        revision: 1,
                        status: crate::ai_sessions::Status::Registered,
                        sampled: true,
                    }),
                );
                assert!(pane.restoration.failed);
                *window.active_mut().panes_mut()[0] = pane;
                fixture.save_mode(&mut window, mode);
                crate::restoration::apply_saved_mode(&mut window);
                assert!(window.active().panes()[0].restoration.resume.is_none());
                let pane = &mut window.active_mut().panes_mut()[0];
                let command = "printf 'downgrade''-shell-output\\n'\r";
                if clipboard {
                    crate::keyboard::paste_into(pane, command);
                } else {
                    pane.keystrokes = command.as_bytes().to_vec();
                    crate::resume::write_keys(pane).unwrap();
                }
                let deadline = Instant::now() + std::time::Duration::from_secs(2);
                let mut output = Vec::new();
                let expected = b"downgrade-shell-output";
                while Instant::now() < deadline {
                    pane.pty_output
                        .pick_up(&mut output, 65536, crate::launch::silences(&pane.pty));
                    if output
                        .windows(expected.len())
                        .any(|bytes| bytes == expected)
                    {
                        break;
                    }
                    std::thread::sleep(std::time::Duration::from_millis(2));
                }
                assert!(
                    output
                        .windows(expected.len())
                        .any(|bytes| bytes == expected),
                    "{mode}, clipboard={clipboard}: input did not reach a fresh shell"
                );
                assert!(!pane.restoration.failed);
            }
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn saved_none_cancels_an_expired_layout_write_before_the_next_frame() {
        let fixture = RestorePolicyFixture::new(super::super::StartupRestore::Directories);
        let original = fixture.seed(crate::session::VERSION);
        let mut window = fixture.open(super::super::StartupRestore::Directories);
        RestorePolicyFixture::queue_expired_change(&mut window);
        fixture.save_mode(&mut window, "none");

        window.save_if_due();

        assert_eq!(fs::read(&fixture.snapshot).unwrap(), original);
        assert!(window.persistence.save_due.is_none());
        assert!(!window.restoration.can_save());
    }

    #[test]
    fn saved_none_preserves_the_snapshot_when_the_window_closes_immediately() {
        let fixture = RestorePolicyFixture::new(super::super::StartupRestore::Directories);
        let original = fixture.seed(crate::session::VERSION);
        let mut window = fixture.open(super::super::StartupRestore::Directories);
        RestorePolicyFixture::queue_expired_change(&mut window);
        fixture.save_mode(&mut window, "none");

        crate::restoration::capture_on_exit(&mut window);
        window.check_whether_the_layout_moved();
        window.save_now();

        assert_eq!(fs::read(&fixture.snapshot).unwrap(), original);
        assert!(!window.restoration.can_save());
        assert!(window.persistence.save_due.is_none());
    }

    #[test]
    fn saved_layout_removes_cached_directories_from_the_next_snapshot() {
        let fixture = RestorePolicyFixture::new(super::super::StartupRestore::Directories);
        fixture.seed(crate::session::VERSION);
        let mut window = fixture.open(super::super::StartupRestore::Directories);
        assert!(window.active().panes()[0].restoration.directory.is_some());
        RestorePolicyFixture::queue_expired_change(&mut window);
        fixture.save_mode(&mut window, "layout");

        window.save_if_due();
        window.check_whether_the_layout_moved();
        window.save_now();

        let saved = crate::session::read(&fixture.snapshot).unwrap();
        let crate::session::StoredNode::Leaf(leaf) = &saved.tabs()[0].root else {
            panic!("expected the saved console");
        };
        assert_eq!(leaf.label.as_deref(), Some("Changed console"));
        assert!(leaf.cwd.is_none());
        assert!(window.active().panes()[0].restoration.directory.is_none());
    }

    #[test]
    fn enabling_layout_after_disabled_startup_respects_a_newer_snapshot_version() {
        let fixture = RestorePolicyFixture::new(super::super::StartupRestore::None);
        let original = fixture.seed(99);
        let mut window = fixture.open(super::super::StartupRestore::None);
        assert!(window.persistence.load_time().is_none());
        fixture.save_mode(&mut window, "layout");

        window.save_if_due();
        window.active_mut().panes_mut()[0].label = Some("Changed fresh console".into());
        window.check_whether_the_layout_moved();
        window.save_now();

        assert!(window.restoration.can_save());
        assert!(window.persistence.load_time().is_some());
        assert_eq!(fs::read(&fixture.snapshot).unwrap(), original);
    }

    #[test]
    fn opening_and_cancelling_never_write_or_enumerate_fonts() {
        let path = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!(
            "settings-editor-{}-{}",
            std::process::id(),
            NEXT.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed)
        ));
        fs::write(&path, "# keep\nsize=18").unwrap();
        let mut editor = Editor::resolved(super::super::Entry::Normal, None);
        editor.open(Some(path.clone()));
        editor.draft.as_mut().unwrap().defaults();
        let ctx = egui::Context::default();
        let mut fonts = crate::fonts::Discoverer::default();
        let output = ctx.run_ui(
            egui::RawInput {
                events: vec![egui::Event::Key {
                    key: egui::Key::Escape,
                    physical_key: None,
                    pressed: true,
                    repeat: false,
                    modifiers: egui::Modifiers::NONE,
                }],
                ..Default::default()
            },
            |ui| assert!(editor.show(ui, &mut fonts, ui.max_rect())),
        );
        output.drop_without_applying_deltas();
        assert!(!editor.is_open());
        assert!(editor.fonts.is_none());
        assert_eq!(fs::read_to_string(&path).unwrap(), "# keep\nsize=18");
        editor.close_frame();
        let mut report = Vec::new();
        editor.publish(&mut report).unwrap();
        assert!(
            String::from_utf8(report)
                .unwrap()
                .contains("settings font enumeration: frames=0")
        );
        fs::remove_file(path).unwrap();
    }
    #[test]
    fn save_reports_conflicts_and_never_claims_success() {
        let path = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!(
            "settings-editor-{}-{}",
            std::process::id(),
            NEXT.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed)
        ));
        fs::write(&path, "size=18").unwrap();
        let mut editor = Editor::resolved(super::super::Entry::Normal, None);
        editor.open(Some(path.clone()));
        editor
            .draft
            .as_mut()
            .unwrap()
            .fields
            .iter_mut()
            .find(|f| f.key == "size")
            .unwrap()
            .value = "20".into();
        fs::write(&path, "size=19").unwrap();
        editor.save(&mut crate::fonts::Discoverer::default());
        assert!(editor.message.contains("changed outside"));
        assert!(!editor.message.contains("Saved."));
        assert!(editor.is_open());
        assert_eq!(fs::read_to_string(&path).unwrap(), "size=19");
        fs::remove_file(path).unwrap();
    }
    #[test]
    fn closing_and_reopening_discards_edits_and_reads_a_fresh_external_snapshot() {
        let path = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!(
            "settings-reopen-{}-{}",
            std::process::id(),
            NEXT.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed)
        ));
        fs::write(&path, "size=18").unwrap();
        let mut editor = Editor::resolved(super::super::Entry::Normal, None);
        editor.open(Some(path.clone()));
        editor
            .draft
            .as_mut()
            .unwrap()
            .fields
            .iter_mut()
            .find(|f| f.key == "size")
            .unwrap()
            .value = "20".into();
        editor.fonts = Some(vec!["old snapshot".into()]);
        let ctx = egui::Context::default();
        let mut fonts = crate::fonts::Discoverer::default();
        ctx.run_ui(
            egui::RawInput {
                events: vec![egui::Event::Key {
                    key: egui::Key::Escape,
                    physical_key: None,
                    pressed: true,
                    repeat: false,
                    modifiers: egui::Modifiers::NONE,
                }],
                ..Default::default()
            },
            |ui| assert!(editor.show(ui, &mut fonts, ui.max_rect())),
        )
        .drop_without_applying_deltas();
        assert!(editor.draft.is_none() && editor.store.is_none() && editor.fonts.is_none());
        assert_eq!(fs::read_to_string(&path).unwrap(), "size=18");
        fs::write(&path, "size=21").unwrap();
        editor.open(Some(path.clone()));
        assert_eq!(
            editor
                .draft
                .as_ref()
                .unwrap()
                .fields
                .iter()
                .find(|f| f.key == "size")
                .unwrap()
                .value,
            "15.75"
        );
        assert!(editor.message.is_empty() && editor.fonts.is_none() && editor.store.is_some());
        editor.save(&mut crate::fonts::Discoverer::default());
        assert!(editor.message.starts_with("Saved."));
        fs::write(&path, "size=22").unwrap();
        editor.save(&mut crate::fonts::Discoverer::default());
        assert!(editor.message.contains("changed outside"));
        fs::remove_file(path).unwrap();
    }

    #[test]
    fn defaults_are_staged_then_saved_for_a_fresh_startup() {
        let path = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!(
            "settings-defaults-{}-{}",
            std::process::id(),
            NEXT.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed)
        ));
        let source = "# retain\nversion=1\nfuture=yes\nsize=18\ntheme=tokyonight\nforeground=#abcdef\nfont=Custom\ncursor.blink=on\nkey.copy=none\nkey.new-tab=none\n";
        fs::write(&path, source).unwrap();
        let mut editor = Editor::resolved(super::super::Entry::Normal, None);
        editor.open(Some(path.clone()));
        editor.draft.as_mut().unwrap().defaults();
        assert_eq!(fs::read_to_string(&path).unwrap(), source);
        editor.save(&mut crate::fonts::Discoverer::default());
        assert!(editor.message.contains("Applying settings"));
        assert_eq!(
            fs::read_to_string(&path).unwrap(),
            "# retain\nversion=2\nfuture=yes\n"
        );
        assert_eq!(
            super::super::read(&path).unwrap().0,
            super::super::Settings::default()
        );
        fs::remove_file(path).unwrap();
    }

    #[test]
    fn invalid_draft_and_unsupported_file_cannot_be_saved() {
        let path = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!(
            "settings-invalid-{}-{}",
            std::process::id(),
            NEXT.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed)
        ));
        fs::write(&path, "size=18").unwrap();
        let mut editor = Editor::resolved(super::super::Entry::Normal, None);
        editor.open(Some(path.clone()));
        editor
            .draft
            .as_mut()
            .unwrap()
            .fields
            .iter_mut()
            .find(|f| f.key == "size")
            .unwrap()
            .value = "97".into();
        editor.save(&mut crate::fonts::Discoverer::default());
        assert_eq!(fs::read_to_string(&path).unwrap(), "size=18");
        assert!(!editor.message.contains("Saved."));
        assert!(editor.is_open());
        fs::write(&path, "version=999").unwrap();
        editor.open(Some(path.clone()));
        assert!(editor.store.is_none());
        editor.draft.as_mut().unwrap().defaults();
        editor.save(&mut crate::fonts::Discoverer::default());
        assert_eq!(fs::read_to_string(&path).unwrap(), "version=999");
        assert!(!editor.message.contains("Saved."));
        fs::remove_file(path).unwrap();
    }
}
```

### crates/app/src/settings/store.rs

```
//! Snapshot checking and replacement of the human-owned settings file.
use super::{MAX_BYTES, interpret};
use std::{
    fs::{self, OpenOptions},
    io::{Read, Write},
    path::{Path, PathBuf},
    sync::atomic::{AtomicU64, Ordering},
};
static NEXT_TEMP: AtomicU64 = AtomicU64::new(0);

pub(crate) struct Store {
    path: PathBuf,
    original: Option<Vec<u8>>,
}

fn snapshot(path: &Path) -> Result<Option<Vec<u8>>, String> {
    let metadata = match fs::symlink_metadata(path) {
        Ok(value) => value,
        Err(e) if e.kind() == std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound => return Ok(None),
        Err(e) => return Err(e.to_string()),
    };
    if metadata.file_type().is_symlink() {
        return Err(
            "Saving requires a regular config file; edit the symbolic link target by hand.".into(),
        );
    }
    if !metadata.is_file() {
        return Err("The configuration path is not a regular file.".into());
    }
    let mut bytes = Vec::new();
    fs::File::open(path)
        .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?
        .take(MAX_BYTES as u64 + 1)
        .read_to_end(&mut bytes)
        .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    if bytes.len() > MAX_BYTES {
        return Err("The configuration exceeds 64 KiB.".into());
    }
    let text = std::str::from_utf8(&bytes).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    interpret(text).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    Ok(Some(bytes))
}

impl Store {
    pub fn open(path: PathBuf) -> Result<(Self, String), String> {
        let original = snapshot(&path)?;
        let text = original
            .as_ref()
            .map(|b| String::from_utf8_lossy(b).into_owned())
            .unwrap_or_default();
        Ok((Self { path, original }, text))
    }

    pub fn save(&mut self, text: &str) -> Result<(), String> {
        self.save_replacing(text, |from, to| fs::rename(from, to))
    }

    fn save_replacing(
        &mut self,
        text: &str,
        replace: impl FnOnce(&Path, &Path) -> std::io::Result<()>,
    ) -> Result<(), String> {
        if text.len() > MAX_BYTES {
            return Err("The configuration exceeds 64 KiB.".into());
        }
        interpret(text).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        if snapshot(&self.path)? != self.original {
            return Err(
                "The configuration changed outside Settings. Cancel and reopen to reload it."
                    .into(),
            );
        }
        let parent = self
            .path
            .parent()
            .ok_or("The configuration has no directory.")?;
        fs::create_dir_all(parent).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        let serial = NEXT_TEMP.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
        let temporary = parent.join(format!(".config-{}-{serial}.tmp", std::process::id()));
        let mut file = OpenOptions::new()
            .write(true)
            .create_new(true)
            .open(&temporary)
            .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        let result = (|| {
            if let Ok(metadata) = fs::metadata(&self.path) {
                file.set_permissions(metadata.permissions())?;
            }
            file.write_all(text.as_bytes())?;
            file.sync_all()?;
            // Recheck after preparation: a hand editor may have saved while the temporary file was written.
            if snapshot(&self.path).map_err(std::io::Error::other)? != self.original {
                return Err(std::io::Error::other(
                    "The configuration changed outside Settings. Cancel and reopen to reload it.",
                ));
            }
            replace(&temporary, &self.path)
        })();
        drop(file);
        if let Err(error) = result {
            let _ = fs::remove_file(&temporary);
            return Err(error.to_string());
        }
        self.original = Some(text.as_bytes().to_vec());
        Ok(())
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    fn dir() -> PathBuf {
        let path = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!(
            "terminal-settings-{}-{}",
            std::process::id(),
            NEXT_TEMP.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed)
        ));
        fs::create_dir_all(&path).unwrap();
        path
    }
    #[test]
    fn save_detects_external_creation_edit_and_deletion() {
        let dir = dir();
        let path = dir.join("config");
        let (mut store, _) = Store::open(path.clone()).unwrap();
        fs::write(&path, "size=19").unwrap();
        assert!(store.save("size=20").is_err());
        assert_eq!(fs::read_to_string(&path).unwrap(), "size=19");
        let (mut store, _) = Store::open(path.clone()).unwrap();
        fs::remove_file(&path).unwrap();
        assert!(store.save("size=20").is_err());
        assert!(!path.exists());
        fs::write(&path, "size=18").unwrap();
        let (mut store, _) = Store::open(path.clone()).unwrap();
        fs::write(&path, "size=21").unwrap();
        assert!(store.save("size=20").is_err());
        assert_eq!(fs::read_to_string(&path).unwrap(), "size=21");
        fs::remove_dir_all(dir).unwrap();
    }
    #[test]
    fn absent_file_save_and_failure_keep_the_previous_bytes() {
        let dir = dir();
        let path = dir.join("new/config");
        let (mut store, text) = Store::open(path.clone()).unwrap();
        assert!(text.is_empty());
        assert!(!path.parent().unwrap().exists());
        store.save("size=18").unwrap();
        assert_eq!(super::super::read(&path).unwrap().0.font_size, Some(18.0));
        assert!(store.save(&"x".repeat(MAX_BYTES + 1)).is_err());
        assert_eq!(fs::read_to_string(&path).unwrap(), "size=18");
        fs::remove_dir_all(dir).unwrap();
    }
    #[test]
    fn failed_replacement_preserves_the_file_and_cleans_the_temporary() {
        let dir = dir();
        let path = dir.join("config");
        fs::write(&path, "# original\nsize=14").unwrap();
        let (mut store, _) = Store::open(path.clone()).unwrap();
        let error = store
            .save_replacing("size=20", |temporary, destination| {
                assert_eq!(fs::read_to_string(temporary).unwrap(), "size=20");
                assert_eq!(
                    fs::read_to_string(destination).unwrap(),
                    "# original\nsize=14"
                );
                Err(std::io::Error::new(
                    std::io::ErrorKind::PermissionDenied,
                    "replacement denied",
                ))
            })
            .unwrap_err();
        assert!(error.contains("replacement denied"));
        assert_eq!(fs::read_to_string(&path).unwrap(), "# original\nsize=14");
        assert_eq!(fs::read_dir(&dir).unwrap().count(), 1);
        store.save("size=18").unwrap();
        assert_eq!(fs::read_to_string(&path).unwrap(), "size=18");
        fs::remove_dir_all(dir).unwrap();
    }

    #[test]
    fn invalid_whole_files_are_never_overwritten() {
        let dir = dir();
        let path = dir.join("config");
        for bytes in [
            b"version=999".as_slice(),
            &[255],
            &vec![b'x'; MAX_BYTES + 1],
        ] {
            fs::write(&path, bytes).unwrap();
            assert!(Store::open(path.clone()).is_err());
            assert_eq!(fs::read(&path).unwrap(), bytes);
        }
        fs::remove_dir_all(dir).unwrap();
    }
    crate::windows_unix_omission!(
        symbolic_link_save_is_refused_and_permissions_survive,
        "unix-only: requires Unix symlink or permission semantics"
    );
    #[cfg(unix)]
    #[test]
    fn symbolic_link_save_is_refused_and_permissions_survive() {
        use std::os::unix::fs::{PermissionsExt, symlink};
        let dir = dir();
        let path = dir.join("config");
        let target = dir.join("target");
        fs::write(&target, "size=18").unwrap();
        symlink(&target, &path).unwrap();
        assert!(Store::open(path.clone()).is_err());
        assert!(path.is_symlink());
        fs::remove_file(&path).unwrap();
        fs::write(&path, "size=14").unwrap();
        fs::set_permissions(&path, fs::Permissions::from_mode(0o600)).unwrap();
        let (mut store, _) = Store::open(path.clone()).unwrap();
        store.save("size=20").unwrap();
        assert_eq!(
            fs::metadata(&path).unwrap().permissions().mode() & 0o777,
            0o600
        );
        fs::remove_dir_all(dir).unwrap();
    }
}

### crates/app/src/shell_integration.rs

```
//! Per-console startup files for interactive shells. No user configuration is changed.
use std::ffi::{OsStr, OsString};
use std::io;
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};

const BASH: &str = include_str!("../../../resources/agent-integrations/codex/shell/bash");
const ZSH: &str = include_str!("../../../resources/agent-integrations/codex/shell/zsh");
const FISH: &str = include_str!("../../../resources/agent-integrations/codex/shell/fish");

/// The program that opens a console: the shell of whoever launched the application.
///
/// **It is what a terminal does**: without this a shell opens with no prompt and no history, and
/// this project exists to look at what comes out on screen. It is still not outside input —it is
/// the user's own session—, but the day the program comes from **configuration** or from a file
/// from outside, this line becomes sensitive surface and has to be reviewed as such. That is why
/// saved labels and directories **never supply a program**: there is nothing
/// to connect here.
///
/// **It has been in a function of its own since restoring exists**, and not for pleasure: restoring
/// opens N consoles at once, and reading the environment inside each one would read it a hundred
/// and twenty-eight times in the worst case.
pub(crate) fn the_users_shell() -> String {
    #[cfg(test)]
    if let Some(shell) = TEST_SHELL.with(|shell| shell.borrow().clone()) {
        return shell;
    }
    // Resolved once per process: on Windows it searches the disk, and a console opens on the
    // interface thread.
    static SHELL: std::sync::OnceLock<String> = std::sync::OnceLock::new();
    SHELL
        .get_or_init(|| {
            #[cfg(windows)]
            return windows_shell(&WindowsSession::read(), Path::is_file);
            #[cfg(not(windows))]
            std::env::var("SHELL").unwrap_or_else(|_| fallback_shell().to_owned())
        })
        .clone()
}

/// The program that is launched when the first one cannot start: one every machine of the platform
/// has. Where it is also what was tried first, the launch does not retry it.
pub(crate) fn fallback_shell() -> &'static str {
    #[cfg(windows)]
    {
        static CMD: std::sync::OnceLock<String> = std::sync::OnceLock::new();
        CMD.get_or_init(|| windows_cmd(&WindowsSession::read()))
    }
    #[cfg(not(windows))]
    "/bin/sh"
}

/// What a Windows session says about its shells, read from its environment.
#[cfg_attr(not(windows), allow(dead_code))]
#[derive(Default)]
struct WindowsSession {
    shell: Option<OsString>,
    path: Option<OsString>,
    comspec: Option<OsString>,
    system_root: Option<OsString>,
}

#[cfg_attr(not(windows), allow(dead_code))]
impl WindowsSession {
    fn read() -> Self {
        Self {
            shell: std::env::var_os("SHELL"),
            path: std::env::var_os("PATH"),
            comspec: std::env::var_os("ComSpec"),
            system_root: std::env::var_os("SystemRoot"),
        }
    }
}

/// Windows has no `/bin/sh`, and the shell variable a Git Bash or MSYS session exports is a path in
/// that environment's own namespace (`/usr/bin/bash`) that Windows cannot launch, so it counts only
/// as an absolute Windows path to a file. Otherwise the newer PowerShell when it is installed, else
/// the one every Windows ships, else `cmd.exe`, as Windows Terminal picks. Looked up on the
/// session's own search path, never in configuration, and only in absolute directories so the
/// folder a console starts in cannot plant one.
#[cfg_attr(not(windows), allow(dead_code))]
fn windows_shell(session: &WindowsSession, is_file: impl Fn(&Path) -> bool) -> String {
    let chosen = session
        .shell
        .as_deref()
        .map(Path::new)
        .filter(|shell| shell.is_absolute() && is_file(shell))
        .map(Path::to_path_buf);
    chosen
        .or_else(|| {
            ["pwsh.exe", "powershell.exe"].into_iter().find_map(|name| {
                session
                    .path
                    .iter()
                    .flat_map(std::env::split_paths)
                    .filter(|directory| directory.is_absolute())
                    .map(|directory| directory.join(name))
                    .find(|candidate| is_file(candidate))
            })
        })
        .map_or_else(
            || windows_cmd(session),
            |found| found.to_string_lossy().into_owned(),
        )
}

/// `cmd.exe` by an absolute path: a bare name would be searched for again by the launcher, current
/// directory and relative entries included.
#[cfg_attr(not(windows), allow(dead_code))]
fn windows_cmd(session: &WindowsSession) -> String {
    let absolute = |value: &Option<OsString>| {
        value
            .as_deref()
            .map(PathBuf::from)
            .filter(|path| path.is_absolute())
    };
    absolute(&session.comspec)
        .or_else(|| {
            absolute(&session.system_root).map(|root| root.join("System32").join("cmd.exe"))
        })
        .map_or_else(
            || r"C:\Windows\System32\cmd.exe".to_owned(),
            |path| path.to_string_lossy().into_owned(),
        )
}

#[cfg(test)]
thread_local! {
    static TEST_SHELL: std::cell::RefCell<Option<String>> = const { std::cell::RefCell::new(None) };
}

/// Keeps input-routing fixtures independent of user startup files without changing process env.
#[cfg(test)]
pub(crate) struct TestShell(Option<String>);
#[cfg(test)]
impl TestShell {
    pub(crate) fn use_program(program: &str) -> Self {
        Self(TEST_SHELL.with(|shell| shell.replace(Some(program.to_owned()))))
    }
}
#[cfg(test)]
impl Drop for TestShell {
    fn drop(&mut self) {
        TEST_SHELL.with(|shell| *shell.borrow_mut() = self.0.take());
    }
}

pub(crate) fn helper_path() -> Option<PathBuf> {
    std::env::current_exe()
        .ok()?
        .parent()
        .map(|p| p.join("ivy-codex"))
}

pub(crate) struct Prepared {
    dir: PathBuf,
    file: PathBuf,
    args: Vec<String>,
    env: Vec<(&'static str, Option<OsString>)>,
}

impl Prepared {
    pub(crate) fn prepare(program: &str, helper: Option<&Path>) -> io::Result<Option<Self>> {
        Self::prepare_with_zdotdir(program, helper, std::env::var_os("ZDOTDIR"))
    }

    fn prepare_with_zdotdir(
        program: &str,
        helper: Option<&Path>,
        original_zdotdir: Option<OsString>,
    ) -> io::Result<Option<Self>> {
        let shell = Path::new(program)
            .file_name()
            .and_then(OsStr::to_str)
            .unwrap_or("");
        let (name, script) = match shell {
            "bash" => ("bashrc", BASH),
            "zsh" => (".zshenv", ZSH),
            "fish" => ("fish-init", FISH),
            _ => {
                eprintln!("Ivy: unsupported shell; automatic Codex panel routing is inactive.");
                return Ok(None);
            }
        };
        let Some(helper) = helper.filter(|p| p.is_absolute() && p.is_file()) else {
            eprintln!("Ivy: codex helper unavailable; automatic panel routing is inactive.");
            return Ok(None);
        };
        let mut random = [0u8; 16];
        getrandom::fill(&mut random).map_err(|error| io::Error::other(error.to_string()))?;
        let suffix: String = random.iter().map(|b| format!("{b:02x}")).collect();
        let dir = panel_contract::base_dir().join(format!("ivy-shell-{suffix}"));
        #[cfg(unix)]
        {
            use std::os::unix::fs::DirBuilderExt;
            std::fs::DirBuilder::new().mode(0o700).create(&dir)?;
        }
        #[cfg(not(unix))]
        std::fs::create_dir(&dir)?;
        let file = dir.join(name);
        if let Err(error) = std::fs::write(&file, script) {
            let _ = std::fs::remove_file(&file);
            let _ = std::fs::remove_dir(&dir);
            return Err(error);
        }
        let mut env = vec![("IVY_CODEX_HELPER", Some(helper.as_os_str().to_owned()))];
        let args = match shell {
            "bash" => vec!["--rcfile".into(), file.to_string_lossy().into_owned()],
            "zsh" => {
                let original = original_zdotdir;
                env.push((
                    "IVY_ZDOTDIR_WAS_SET",
                    Some(if original.is_some() { "1" } else { "0" }.into()),
                ));
                env.push(("IVY_ORIGINAL_ZDOTDIR", original));
                env.push(("ZDOTDIR", Some(dir.as_os_str().to_owned())));
                vec![]
            }
            "fish" => {
                env.push(("IVY_FISH_INIT", Some(file.as_os_str().to_owned())));
                vec!["--init-command".into(), "source \"$IVY_FISH_INIT\"".into()]
            }
            _ => unreachable!(),
        };
        Ok(Some(Self {
            dir,
            file,
            args,
            env,
        }))
    }
    pub(crate) fn args(&self) -> &[String] {
        &self.args
    }
    pub(crate) fn env(&self) -> Vec<(&str, Option<&OsStr>)> {
        self.env
            .iter()
            .map(|(name, value)| (*name, value.as_deref()))
            .collect()
    }
}
impl Drop for Prepared {
    fn drop(&mut self) {
        let _ = std::fs::remove_file(&self.file);
        let _ = std::fs::remove_dir(&self.dir);
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod default_shell_tests {
    use super::*;

    fn path_of(directories: &[&Path]) -> OsString {
        std::env::join_paths(directories).unwrap()
    }

    fn session(shell: Option<&str>, path: &OsStr) -> WindowsSession {
        let root = std::env::temp_dir().join("Windows");
        WindowsSession {
            shell: shell.map(OsString::from),
            path: Some(path.to_owned()),
            comspec: None,
            system_root: Some(root.into_os_string()),
        }
    }

    fn system_cmd() -> String {
        let root = std::env::temp_dir().join("Windows");
        root.join("System32")
            .join("cmd.exe")
            .to_string_lossy()
            .into_owned()
    }

    #[test]
    fn windows_startup_prefers_pwsh_then_windows_powershell_then_cmd() {
        let base = std::env::temp_dir();
        let (first, second) = (base.join("first"), base.join("second"));
        let path = path_of(&[&first, &second]);
        let pwsh = second.join("pwsh.exe");
        let legacy = first.join("powershell.exe");
        let s = session(None, &path);
        // pwsh wins even when Windows PowerShell sits in an earlier directory.
        let both = windows_shell(&s, |p| p == pwsh || p == legacy);
        assert_eq!(both, pwsh.to_string_lossy());
        assert_eq!(windows_shell(&s, |p| p == legacy), legacy.to_string_lossy());
        assert_eq!(windows_shell(&s, |_| false), system_cmd());
    }

    #[test]
    fn windows_startup_ignores_relative_path_entries() {
        let path = path_of(&[Path::new("planted")]);
        assert_eq!(windows_shell(&session(None, &path), |_| true), system_cmd());
    }

    #[test]
    fn windows_startup_takes_cmd_by_absolute_path_only() {
        let path = path_of(&[]);
        let comspec = std::env::temp_dir().join("cmd.exe");
        let mut s = session(None, &path);
        s.comspec = Some(comspec.clone().into_os_string());
        assert_eq!(windows_cmd(&s), comspec.to_string_lossy());
        s.comspec = Some("cmd.exe".into());
        assert_eq!(windows_cmd(&s), system_cmd());
        s.system_root = Some("Windows".into());
        assert_eq!(windows_cmd(&s), r"C:\Windows\System32\cmd.exe");
    }

    #[test]
    fn windows_startup_keeps_a_launchable_shell_and_skips_a_unix_one() {
        let pwsh = std::env::temp_dir().join("pwsh.exe");
        let path = path_of(&[&std::env::temp_dir()]);
        let bash = std::env::temp_dir().join("bash.exe");
        let named = session(Some(&bash.to_string_lossy()), &path);
        assert_eq!(windows_shell(&named, |_| true), bash.to_string_lossy());
        let msys = session(Some("/usr/bin/bash"), &path);
        assert_eq!(windows_shell(&msys, |p| p == pwsh), pwsh.to_string_lossy());
        let missing = session(Some(&bash.to_string_lossy()), &path);
        assert_eq!(
            windows_shell(&missing, |p| p == pwsh),
            pwsh.to_string_lossy()
        );
    }

    /// Runs one command in a console of `program` and waits for its result, not its echo: ConPTY
    /// writes its own sequences before any program does, so bytes alone prove nothing.
    #[cfg(windows)]
    fn answers(program: &str) {
        let (mut pty, output) =
            term_core::Pty::spawn(program, &[], term_core::Size::default()).unwrap();
        let name = program.to_ascii_lowercase();
        let command: &[u8] = if name.ends_with("cmd.exe") {
            b"echo IVY^_OK\r"
        } else if name.ends_with("pwsh.exe") || name.ends_with("powershell.exe") {
            b"Write-Output ('IVY' + '_OK')\r"
        } else {
            b"echo IVY\"\"_OK\r"
        };
        pty.write(command).unwrap();
        let deadline = std::time::Instant::now() + std::time::Duration::from_secs(30);
        let (mut seen, mut answered) = (String::new(), 0);
        while !seen.contains("IVY_OK") {
            let left = deadline.saturating_duration_since(std::time::Instant::now());
            assert!(!left.is_zero(), "{program} never ran the command: {seen:?}");
            if let Ok(bytes) = output.recv_timeout(left) {
                seen.push_str(&String::from_utf8_lossy(&bytes));
            }
            // ConPTY asks where the cursor is and waits for the answer, which the window's
            // screen gives in the app; here nobody else would.
            for _ in answered..seen.matches("\x1b[6n").count() {
                pty.write(b"\x1b[1;1R").unwrap();
                answered += 1;
            }
        }
    }

    /// The reported failure was this spawn: the default program and the fallback both run.
    #[cfg(windows)]
    #[test]
    fn windows_startup_default_and_fallback_run_a_command() {
        answers(&the_users_shell());
        answers(fallback_shell());
    }
}

#[cfg(all(test, unix))]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use std::io::Write;
    use std::os::unix::fs::PermissionsExt;
    use std::process::{Command, Stdio};

    fn executable(path: &Path, body: &str) {
        std::fs::write(path, body).unwrap();
        std::fs::set_permissions(path, std::fs::Permissions::from_mode(0o755)).unwrap();
    }

    #[test]
    fn relative_tmpdir_uses_absolute_shared_base_and_warns_once() {
        const CHILD: &str = "IVY_TEST_RELATIVE_TMPDIR_CHILD";
        if std::env::var_os(CHILD).is_some() {
            for _ in 0..3 {
                assert_eq!(panel_contract::base_dir(), PathBuf::from("/tmp"));
            }
            let prepared = Prepared::prepare_with_zdotdir("bash", Some(Path::new("/bin/sh")), None)
                .unwrap()
                .unwrap();
            assert!(
                prepared.dir.starts_with("/tmp"),
                "{}",
                prepared.dir.display()
            );
            return;
        }

        let output = Command::new(std::env::current_exe().unwrap())
            .args([
                "--exact",
                "shell_integration::tests::relative_tmpdir_uses_absolute_shared_base_and_warns_once",
                "--nocapture",
            ])
            .env(CHILD, "1")
            .env("TMPDIR", "relative-temp")
            .env_remove("XDG_RUNTIME_DIR")
            .output()
            .unwrap();
        assert!(
            output.status.success(),
            "{}",
            String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stderr)
        );
        let stderr = String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stderr);
        assert_eq!(
            stderr
                .matches("TMPDIR set but not an absolute path")
                .count(),
            1,
            "{stderr}"
        );
        assert!(!stderr.contains("XDG_RUNTIME_DIR set"), "{stderr}");
    }

    #[test]
    fn real_shells_use_post_configuration_path_and_remain_alive() {
        let fish = std::env::var("IVY_TEST_FISH").unwrap_or_else(|_| "/usr/bin/fish".into());
        for shell in ["/bin/bash", "/usr/bin/zsh", fish.as_str()] {
            if !Path::new(shell).exists() {
                eprintln!("shell integration test skipped: {shell} unavailable");
                continue;
            }
            let mut random = [0; 8];
            getrandom::fill(&mut random).unwrap();
            let home = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!(
                "ivy test ' $(touch sentinel) {:x}",
                u64::from_ne_bytes(random)
            ));
            let bin = home.join("bin");
            std::fs::create_dir_all(&bin).unwrap();
            let helper = home.join("helper ' $()");
            executable(
                &helper,
                "#!/bin/sh\nprintf 'helper\\n' >> \"$LOG\"\nexec \"$@\"\n",
            );
            executable(
                &bin.join("codex"),
                "#!/bin/sh\nprintf 'client:%s:%s:%s\\n' \"$PANEL_ID\" \"$1\" \"$2\" >> \"$LOG\"\n",
            );
            let setup = "export PATH=\"$PREPEND_BIN:$PATH\"\n";
            if shell.ends_with("bash") {
                std::fs::write(home.join(".bashrc"), setup).unwrap();
            } else if shell.ends_with("zsh") {
                std::fs::write(home.join(".zshrc"), setup).unwrap();
            } else {
                std::fs::create_dir_all(home.join(".config/fish")).unwrap();
                std::fs::write(
                    home.join(".config/fish/config.fish"),
                    "set -gx PATH $PREPEND_BIN $PATH\n",
                )
                .unwrap();
            }
            let prepared = Prepared::prepare_with_zdotdir(shell, Some(&helper), None)
                .unwrap()
                .unwrap();
            let mut command = Command::new(shell);
            command
                .args(prepared.args())
                .arg("-i")
                .env("HOME", &home)
                .env("PREPEND_BIN", &bin)
                .env("LOG", home.join("log"))
                .env("PANEL_ID", "pane-a")
                .stdin(Stdio::piped())
                .stdout(Stdio::piped())
                .stderr(Stdio::piped());
            for (key, value) in prepared.env() {
                match value {
                    Some(value) => {
                        command.env(key, value);
                    }
                    None => {
                        command.env_remove(key);
                    }
                }
            }
            let mut child = command.spawn().unwrap();
            child
                .stdin
                .take()
                .unwrap()
                .write_all(
                    b"codex 'one space' 'two'\ncodex 'again' 'done'\ncommand codex bypass command\n\"$HOME/bin/codex\" bypass path\necho STILL_ALIVE\nexit\n",
                )
                .unwrap();
            let output = child.wait_with_output().unwrap();
            assert!(
                output.status.success(),
                "{shell}: {}",
                String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stderr)
            );
            assert!(String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stdout).contains("STILL_ALIVE"));
            assert_eq!(
                std::fs::read_to_string(home.join("log")).unwrap(),
                "helper\nclient:pane-a:one space:two\nhelper\nclient:pane-a:again:done\nclient:pane-a:bypass:command\nclient:pane-a:bypass:path\n",
                "{shell}: {}",
                String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stderr)
            );
            let dir = prepared.dir.clone();
            drop(prepared);
            assert!(!dir.exists());
            std::fs::remove_dir_all(home).unwrap();
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn zsh_restores_original_directory_and_honors_changes_from_zshenv() {
        if !Path::new("/usr/bin/zsh").exists() {
            eprintln!("zsh unavailable");
            return;
        }
        for mode in ["unset", "empty", "present", "changed"] {
            let mut random = [0; 8];
            getrandom::fill(&mut random).unwrap();
            let home =
                std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("ivy-zsh-{:x}", u64::from_ne_bytes(random)));
            let original = home.join("original");
            let changed = home.join("changed");
            std::fs::create_dir_all(&original).unwrap();
            std::fs::create_dir_all(&changed).unwrap();
            let helper = home.join("helper");
            executable(&helper, "#!/bin/sh\nexec \"$@\"\n");
            let initial = if mode == "unset" || mode == "empty" {
                &home
            } else {
                &original
            };
            if mode == "changed" {
                std::fs::write(
                    initial.join(".zshenv"),
                    "export ZDOTDIR=\"$TEST_CHANGED\"\n",
                )
                .unwrap();
            }
            let expected = if mode == "changed" { &changed } else { initial };
            std::fs::write(expected.join(".zshrc"), "echo USER_RC\n").unwrap();
            let inherited = if mode == "unset" {
                None
            } else if mode == "empty" {
                Some(OsString::new())
            } else {
                Some(original.as_os_str().to_owned())
            };
            let prepared = Prepared::prepare_with_zdotdir("/usr/bin/zsh", Some(&helper), inherited)
                .unwrap()
                .unwrap();
            let mut cmd = Command::new("/usr/bin/zsh");
            cmd.arg("-i")
                .current_dir(&home)
                .env("HOME", &home)
                .env("TEST_CHANGED", &changed)
                .env("TERM", "dumb")
                .stdin(Stdio::piped())
                .stdout(Stdio::piped())
                .stderr(Stdio::piped());
            for (key, value) in prepared.env() {
                if let Some(value) = value {
                    cmd.env(key, value);
                } else {
                    cmd.env_remove(key);
                }
            }
            let mut child = cmd.spawn().unwrap();
            child
                .stdin
                .take()
                .unwrap()
                .write_all(b"echo RESULT:${ZDOTDIR-unset}\nexit\n")
                .unwrap();
            let output = child.wait_with_output().unwrap();
            let out = String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stdout);
            if mode == "empty" {
                assert!(!out.contains("USER_RC"), "{mode}: {out}");
            } else {
                assert!(out.contains("USER_RC"), "{mode}: {out}");
            }
            let value = if mode == "unset" {
                "unset".to_owned()
            } else if mode == "empty" {
                String::new()
            } else {
                expected.to_string_lossy().into_owned()
            };
            assert!(out.contains(&format!("RESULT:{value}")), "{mode}: {out}");
            drop(prepared);
            std::fs::remove_dir_all(home).unwrap();
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn cleanup_never_removes_unknown_contents() {
        let prepared = Prepared::prepare_with_zdotdir("bash", Some(Path::new("/bin/sh")), None)
            .unwrap()
            .unwrap();
        let dir = prepared.dir.clone();
        std::fs::write(dir.join("foreign"), "kept").unwrap();
        drop(prepared);
        assert_eq!(
            std::fs::read_to_string(dir.join("foreign")).unwrap(),
            "kept"
        );
        std::fs::remove_file(dir.join("foreign")).unwrap();
        std::fs::remove_dir(dir).unwrap();
    }

    #[test]
    fn user_commands_win_with_diagnostic() {
        let fish = std::env::var("IVY_TEST_FISH").unwrap_or_else(|_| "/usr/bin/fish".into());
        for shell in ["/bin/bash", "/usr/bin/zsh", fish.as_str()] {
            if !Path::new(shell).exists() {
                eprintln!("shell unavailable: {shell}");
                continue;
            }
            for user_definition in [
                "alias codex='echo USER_COMMAND'\n",
                "codex() { echo USER_COMMAND; }\n",
            ] {
                let mut random = [0; 8];
                getrandom::fill(&mut random).unwrap();
                let home = std::env::temp_dir()
                    .join(format!("ivy-conflict-{:x}", u64::from_ne_bytes(random)));
                std::fs::create_dir_all(&home).unwrap();
                if shell.ends_with("bash") {
                    std::fs::write(home.join(".bashrc"), user_definition).unwrap();
                } else if shell.ends_with("zsh") {
                    std::fs::write(home.join(".zshrc"), user_definition).unwrap();
                } else {
                    std::fs::create_dir_all(home.join(".config/fish")).unwrap();
                    std::fs::write(
                        home.join(".config/fish/config.fish"),
                        "function codex; echo USER_COMMAND; end\n",
                    )
                    .unwrap();
                }
                let prepared =
                    Prepared::prepare_with_zdotdir(shell, Some(Path::new("/bin/sh")), None)
                        .unwrap()
                        .unwrap();
                let mut command = Command::new(shell);
                command
                    .args(prepared.args())
                    .arg("-i")
                    .env("HOME", &home)
                    .env("TERM", "dumb")
                    .stdin(Stdio::piped())
                    .stdout(Stdio::piped())
                    .stderr(Stdio::piped());
                for (key, value) in prepared.env() {
                    if let Some(value) = value {
                        command.env(key, value);
                    } else {
                        command.env_remove(key);
                    }
                }
                let mut child = command.spawn().unwrap();
                child
                    .stdin
                    .take()
                    .unwrap()
                    .write_all(b"codex\nexit\n")
                    .unwrap();
                let output = child.wait_with_output().unwrap();
                assert!(
                    String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stdout).contains("USER_COMMAND"),
                    "{shell}"
                );
                assert!(
                    String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stderr)
                        .contains("existing codex alias or function preserved"),
                    "{shell}"
                );
                drop(prepared);
                std::fs::remove_dir_all(home).unwrap();
            }
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn zsh_disabling_rcs_does_not_install_codex_function() {
        if !Path::new("/usr/bin/zsh").exists() {
            eprintln!("zsh unavailable");
            return;
        }
        let mut random = [0; 8];
        getrandom::fill(&mut random).unwrap();
        let home =
            std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("ivy-zsh-rcs-{:x}", u64::from_ne_bytes(random)));
        std::fs::create_dir_all(&home).unwrap();
        std::fs::write(home.join(".zshenv"), "unsetopt rcs\n").unwrap();
        let prepared =
            Prepared::prepare_with_zdotdir("/usr/bin/zsh", Some(Path::new("/bin/sh")), None)
                .unwrap()
                .unwrap();
        let mut command = Command::new("/usr/bin/zsh");
        command
            .arg("-i")
            .current_dir(&home)
            .env("HOME", &home)
            .env("TERM", "dumb")
            .stdin(Stdio::piped())
            .stdout(Stdio::piped())
            .stderr(Stdio::piped());
        for (key, value) in prepared.env() {
            if let Some(value) = value {
                command.env(key, value);
            } else {
                command.env_remove(key);
            }
        }
        let mut child = command.spawn().unwrap();
        child
            .stdin
            .take()
            .unwrap()
            .write_all(
                b"(( ${+functions[codex]} )) && echo INSTALLED || echo NOT_INSTALLED\nexit\n",
            )
            .unwrap();
        let output = child.wait_with_output().unwrap();
        assert!(String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stdout).contains("NOT_INSTALLED"));
        assert!(String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stderr).contains("zsh startup files disabled"));
        drop(prepared);
        std::fs::remove_dir_all(home).unwrap();
    }

    #[test]
    fn fish_preserves_newlines_in_executable_path_and_arguments() {
        let fish = std::env::var("IVY_TEST_FISH").unwrap_or_else(|_| "/usr/bin/fish".into());
        if !Path::new(&fish).exists() {
            eprintln!("fish integration test skipped: {fish} unavailable");
            return;
        }
        let mut random = [0; 8];
        getrandom::fill(&mut random).unwrap();
        let home =
            std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("ivy-fish-newline-{:x}", u64::from_ne_bytes(random)));
        let bin = home.join("bin\n`touch sentinel`$(touch sentinel)");
        std::fs::create_dir_all(&bin).unwrap();
        std::fs::create_dir_all(home.join(".config/fish")).unwrap();
        std::fs::write(
            home.join(".config/fish/config.fish"),
            "set -gx PATH $PREPEND_BIN $PATH\n",
        )
        .unwrap();
        let helper = home.join("helper");
        executable(
            &helper,
            "#!/bin/sh\nprintf '%s\\0' \"$@\" > \"$HELPER_LOG\"\nexec \"$@\"\n",
        );
        executable(
            &bin.join("codex"),
            "#!/bin/sh\nprintf '%s\\0' \"$#\" \"$@\" > \"$CLIENT_LOG\"\n",
        );
        let prepared = Prepared::prepare_with_zdotdir(&fish, Some(&helper), None)
            .unwrap()
            .unwrap();
        let mut command = Command::new(&fish);
        command
            .args(prepared.args())
            .arg("-i")
            .current_dir(&home)
            .env("HOME", &home)
            .env("PREPEND_BIN", &bin)
            .env("HELPER_LOG", home.join("helper-log"))
            .env("CLIENT_LOG", home.join("client-log"))
            .env("TERM", "dumb")
            .stdin(Stdio::piped())
            .stdout(Stdio::piped())
            .stderr(Stdio::piped());
        for (key, value) in prepared.env() {
            if let Some(value) = value {
                command.env(key, value);
            } else {
                command.env_remove(key);
            }
        }
        let mut child = command.spawn().unwrap();
        child
            .stdin
            .take()
            .unwrap()
            .write_all(b"codex '' 'line\nbreak' 'space value'\nexit\n")
            .unwrap();
        let output = child.wait_with_output().unwrap();
        assert!(
            output.status.success(),
            "{}",
            String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stderr)
        );
        let mut expected = bin.join("codex").as_os_str().as_encoded_bytes().to_vec();
        expected.extend_from_slice(b"\0\0line\nbreak\0space value\0");
        assert_eq!(std::fs::read(home.join("helper-log")).unwrap(), expected);
        assert_eq!(
            std::fs::read(home.join("client-log")).unwrap(),
            b"3\0\0line\nbreak\0space value\0"
        );
        assert!(!home.join("sentinel").exists());
        drop(prepared);
        std::fs::remove_dir_all(home).unwrap();
    }

    #[test]
    fn missing_helper_at_preparation_or_runtime_keeps_real_shells_usable() {
        let fish = std::env::var("IVY_TEST_FISH").unwrap_or_else(|_| "/usr/bin/fish".into());
        for shell in ["/bin/bash", "/usr/bin/zsh", fish.as_str()] {
            if !Path::new(shell).exists() {
                eprintln!("shell integration test skipped: {shell} unavailable");
                continue;
            }
            for removed_after_preparation in [false, true] {
                let mut random = [0; 8];
                getrandom::fill(&mut random).unwrap();
                let home = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!(
                    "ivy-missing-helper-{:x}",
                    u64::from_ne_bytes(random)
                ));
                let bin = home.join("bin");
                std::fs::create_dir_all(&bin).unwrap();
                let (rc, contents) = if shell.ends_with("bash") {
                    (home.join(".bashrc"), "export PATH=\"$PREPEND_BIN:$PATH\"\n")
                } else if shell.ends_with("zsh") {
                    (home.join(".zshrc"), "export PATH=\"$PREPEND_BIN:$PATH\"\n")
                } else {
                    std::fs::create_dir_all(home.join(".config/fish")).unwrap();
                    (
                        home.join(".config/fish/config.fish"),
                        "set -gx PATH $PREPEND_BIN $PATH\n",
                    )
                };
                std::fs::write(&rc, contents).unwrap();
                let helper = home.join("helper");
                if removed_after_preparation {
                    executable(&helper, "#!/bin/sh\nexit 99\n");
                }
                let prepared = Prepared::prepare_with_zdotdir(shell, Some(&helper), None).unwrap();
                assert_eq!(prepared.is_some(), removed_after_preparation);
                if removed_after_preparation {
                    std::fs::remove_file(&helper).unwrap();
                }
                executable(
                    &bin.join("codex"),
                    "#!/bin/sh\nprintf '%s\\0' \"$PANEL_ID\" \"$PANEL_DIR\" \"$@\" > \"$LOG\"\nexit 37\n",
                );
                let mut command = Command::new(shell);
                if let Some(prepared) = &prepared {
                    command.args(prepared.args());
                    for (key, value) in prepared.env() {
                        if let Some(value) = value {
                            command.env(key, value);
                        } else {
                            command.env_remove(key);
                        }
                    }
                } else {
                    command.env_remove("ZDOTDIR");
                }
                command
                    .arg("-i")
                    .current_dir(&home)
                    .env("HOME", &home)
                    .env("PREPEND_BIN", &bin)
                    .env("LOG", home.join("log"))
                    .env("PANEL_ID", "fallback pane")
                    .env("PANEL_DIR", "fallback directory")
                    .env("TERM", "dumb")
                    .stdin(Stdio::piped())
                    .stdout(Stdio::piped())
                    .stderr(Stdio::piped());
                let mut child = command.spawn().unwrap();
                let script = if shell.ends_with("fish") {
                    b"codex resume 'session one' ''\necho CLIENT_STATUS:$status\necho STILL_ALIVE\nexit\n".as_slice()
                } else {
                    b"codex resume 'session one' ''\necho CLIENT_STATUS:$?\necho STILL_ALIVE\nexit\n"
                        .as_slice()
                };
                child.stdin.take().unwrap().write_all(script).unwrap();
                let output = child.wait_with_output().unwrap();
                let out = String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stdout);
                let err = String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stderr);
                assert!(output.status.success(), "{shell}: {err}");
                assert!(out.contains("CLIENT_STATUS:37"), "{shell}: {out}, {err}");
                assert!(out.contains("STILL_ALIVE"), "{shell}: {out}, {err}");
                assert_eq!(
                    std::fs::read(home.join("log")).unwrap(),
                    b"fallback pane\0fallback directory\0resume\0session one\0\0"
                );
                if removed_after_preparation {
                    assert!(err.contains("codex helper unavailable"), "{shell}: {err}");
                }
                assert_eq!(std::fs::read(&rc).unwrap(), contents.as_bytes());
                drop(prepared);
                std::fs::remove_dir_all(home).unwrap();
            }
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn unsupported_shell_reports_limitation_and_still_runs_commands() {
        const CHILD: &str = "IVY_TEST_UNSUPPORTED_SHELL_CHILD";
        if std::env::var_os(CHILD).is_some() {
            let prepared =
                Prepared::prepare_with_zdotdir("/bin/sh", Some(Path::new("/bin/sh")), None)
                    .unwrap();
            assert!(prepared.is_none());
            let mut command = Command::new("/bin/sh");
            command
                .arg("-i")
                .stdin(Stdio::piped())
                .stdout(Stdio::piped())
                .stderr(Stdio::piped());
            let mut child = command.spawn().unwrap();
            child
                .stdin
                .take()
                .unwrap()
                .write_all(b"printf '%s\\n' SHELL_USABLE\nexit 0\n")
                .unwrap();
            let output = child.wait_with_output().unwrap();
            assert!(output.status.success());
            assert!(String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stdout).contains("SHELL_USABLE"));
            return;
        }
        let output = Command::new(std::env::current_exe().unwrap())
            .args(["--exact", "shell_integration::tests::unsupported_shell_reports_limitation_and_still_runs_commands", "--nocapture"])
            .env(CHILD, "1")
            .output().unwrap();
        assert!(
            output.status.success(),
            "{}",
            String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stdout)
        );
        assert!(
            String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stderr)
                .contains("unsupported shell; automatic Codex panel routing is inactive.")
        );
    }
}
