### crates/app/src/sidebar/tests.rs

```
#![cfg(test)]

use super::*;
use crate::tests::{one_frame_in, window_of_tabs};
use crate::tree::Node;

#[test]
fn board_fills_content_at_every_width_without_resizing_consoles() {
    let mut window = window_of_tabs([["sleep 30", "sleep 30"]]);
    let ctx = egui::Context::default();
    for width in [500.0, 1600.0] {
        let area = egui::Rect::from_min_size(egui::Pos2::ZERO, egui::vec2(width, 700.0));
        close(&mut window, &ctx);
        one_frame_in(&mut window, &ctx, area, vec![]);
        let closed: Vec<_> = window.all_panes().map(|p| p.measured).collect();
        let metrics = CellMetrics::measure(&ctx, egui::FontId::monospace(window.font_size));
        let baseline = crate::tab_bar::geometry(&window, area, &metrics);
        select(&mut window, Tool::Board);
        one_frame_in(&mut window, &ctx, area, vec![]);
        let opened = crate::tab_bar::geometry(&window, area, &metrics);
        assert_eq!(opened.dock, Some(baseline.consoles));
        assert!(
            opened.divider.is_none(),
            "Board must not have a resize handle"
        );
        assert_eq!(opened.consoles, baseline.consoles);
        assert_eq!(window.sidebar.last_dock, opened.dock);
        assert_eq!(
            window.all_panes().map(|p| p.measured).collect::<Vec<_>>(),
            closed
        );
    }
}

#[test]
fn files_and_the_drawer_keep_their_minimums_or_step_aside_for_the_consoles() {
    use layout::{DRAWER_MARGIN, DRAWER_MINIMUM, FILES_MINIMUM, Floors, layout};
    let floors = Floors {
        width: 320.0,
        height: 120.0,
    };
    // 286 is the drawer's boundary here: its minimum, the divider and the consoles' floor.
    for height in [0.0, 20.0, 285.0, 286.0, 600.0, 900.0] {
        for width in [0.0, 12.0, 300.0, 525.0, 526.0, 1000.0, 1400.0] {
            let area = egui::Rect::from_min_size(egui::pos2(7.0, 11.0), egui::vec2(width, height));
            let result = layout(area, Some(264.0), Some(370.0), false, floors);
            assert!(area.contains_rect(result.consoles), "{width}x{height}");
            match result.files {
                Some(files) => {
                    assert!(files.width() >= FILES_MINIMUM);
                    assert_eq!(files.left(), area.left());
                    assert!(result.consoles.width() >= floors.width);
                    assert!(files.right() < result.consoles.left());
                }
                None => assert!(width < FILES_MINIMUM + DIVIDER + floors.width, "{width}"),
            }
            match result.dock {
                Some(drawer) => {
                    assert!(drawer.height() + DRAWER_MARGIN >= DRAWER_MINIMUM);
                    assert!(result.consoles.height() >= floors.height);
                    assert!(result.consoles.bottom() < drawer.top());
                    assert!(area.contains_rect(drawer));
                    assert_eq!(drawer.left(), result.consoles.left() + DRAWER_MARGIN);
                }
                None => assert!(
                    height < DRAWER_MINIMUM + DIVIDER + floors.height
                        || result.consoles.width() <= 2.0 * DRAWER_MARGIN,
                    "{width}x{height}"
                ),
            }
        }
    }
    let area = egui::Rect::from_min_size(egui::Pos2::ZERO, egui::vec2(1400.0, 900.0));
    let open = layout(area, Some(264.0), Some(370.0), false, floors);
    assert_eq!(open.files.unwrap().width(), 264.0);
    assert_eq!(
        open.dock.unwrap().height() + DRAWER_MARGIN,
        370.0,
        "the drawer keeps its preferred height"
    );
    let maximized = layout(area, Some(264.0), Some(370.0), true, floors);
    let closed = layout(area, Some(264.0), None, false, floors);
    assert_eq!(
        maximized.consoles, open.consoles,
        "maximizing the drawer resizes no console"
    );
    assert_eq!(
        maximized.dock,
        Some(closed.consoles),
        "a maximized drawer covers the consoles edge to edge"
    );
    assert!(maximized.divider.is_none());
    // Too short for the drawer beside the consoles, it still maximizes over them.
    let short = egui::Rect::from_min_size(egui::Pos2::ZERO, egui::vec2(1400.0, 200.0));
    assert!(
        layout(short, None, Some(370.0), false, floors)
            .dock
            .is_none()
    );
    assert_eq!(
        layout(short, None, Some(370.0), true, floors).dock,
        Some(short)
    );
    assert!(layout(short, None, None, true, floors).dock.is_none());
}

crate::windows_unix_omission!(
    board_blocks_all_mouse_buttons_and_motion_to_covered_programs,
    "unix-only: uses stty raw mode and cat to verify input never reaches the child"
);

#[cfg(unix)]
#[test]

fn board_blocks_all_mouse_buttons_and_motion_to_covered_programs() {
    let received = term_core::received::Received::new("board-covered-mouse");
    let script = received.script("stty raw -echo");
    let mut window = window_of_tabs([[script.as_str(), "sleep 30"]]);
    received.wait_until_ready(Duration::from_secs(5));
    let ctx = egui::Context::default();
    let area = egui::Rect::from_min_size(egui::Pos2::ZERO, egui::vec2(1600.0, 700.0));
    let focus = window.active().focus;
    window
        .pane_called(focus)
        .unwrap()
        .screen
        .advance(b"\x1b[?1003h\x1b[?1006h");
    select(&mut window, Tool::Board);
    one_frame_in(&mut window, &ctx, area, vec![]);
    one_frame_in(&mut window, &ctx, area, vec![]);
    let pos = window
        .pane_called(focus)
        .unwrap()
        .painted_console
        .as_ref()
        .unwrap()
        .area
        .center();
    one_frame_in(
        &mut window,
        &ctx,
        area,
        vec![egui::Event::PointerMoved(pos)],
    );
    for button in [egui::PointerButton::Secondary, egui::PointerButton::Middle] {
        for pressed in [true, false] {
            one_frame_in(
                &mut window,
                &ctx,
                area,
                vec![egui::Event::PointerButton {
                    pos,
                    button,
                    pressed,
                    modifiers: egui::Modifiers::NONE,
                }],
            );
        }
    }
    close(&mut window, &ctx);
    one_frame_in(&mut window, &ctx, area, vec![]);
    one_frame_in(
        &mut window,
        &ctx,
        area,
        vec![egui::Event::Text("after".into())],
    );
    assert_eq!(received.wait_for(5, Duration::from_secs(5)), b"after");
}

#[test]
fn opening_resizing_switching_and_closing_preserve_consoles_and_update_their_grids() {
    let mut window = window_of_tabs([["sleep 30", "sleep 30"], ["sleep 30", "sleep 30"]]);
    let ctx = egui::Context::default();
    let area = egui::Rect::from_min_size(egui::Pos2::ZERO, egui::vec2(1600.0, 800.0));
    one_frame_in(&mut window, &ctx, area, vec![]);
    let identities: Vec<_> = window.all_panes().map(|p| p.name).collect();
    let measured = |window: &Window| window.all_panes().map(|p| p.measured).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    let closed = measured(&window);
    select(&mut window, Tool::Files);
    one_frame_in(&mut window, &ctx, area, vec![]);
    let files = window.sidebar.last_files.expect("Files opens at the left");
    assert_eq!(files.left(), 0.0);
    assert_eq!(files.width(), 264.0);
    assert!(window.sidebar.last_dock.is_none());
    assert_ne!(measured(&window), closed);
    window.sidebar.set_files_width(300.0);
    one_frame_in(&mut window, &ctx, area, vec![]);
    assert_eq!(window.sidebar.last_files.unwrap().width(), 300.0);
    let beside = measured(&window);
    crate::viewer::activate(&mut window, false);
    one_frame_in(&mut window, &ctx, area, vec![]);
    let drawer = window.sidebar.last_dock.expect("the drawer opens below");
    assert!(files_visible(&window), "the drawer does not close Files");
    assert_eq!(window.sidebar.last_files.unwrap().width(), 300.0);
    assert!(
        drawer.left() > 300.0,
        "the drawer sits beside Files, below the consoles"
    );
    assert!((drawer.bottom() - (area.bottom() - layout::DRAWER_MARGIN)).abs() < 0.01);
    assert_ne!(
        measured(&window),
        beside,
        "the consoles give up their bottom rows"
    );
    select(&mut window, Tool::Board);
    one_frame_in(&mut window, &ctx, area, vec![]);
    assert_eq!(active(&window), Some(Tool::Board));
    assert!(
        window.sidebar.last_files.is_none(),
        "the Board covers Files"
    );
    close(&mut window, &ctx);
    one_frame_in(&mut window, &ctx, area, vec![]);
    assert!(files_visible(&window), "closing the Board gives Files back");
    assert_eq!(measured(&window), beside);
    let narrow = egui::Rect::from_min_size(egui::Pos2::ZERO, egui::vec2(500.0, 800.0));
    one_frame_in(&mut window, &ctx, narrow, vec![]);
    assert!(window.sidebar.last_files.is_none());
    assert!(
        files_visible(&window),
        "a narrow window suppresses Files without closing it"
    );
    one_frame_in(&mut window, &ctx, area, vec![]);
    assert_eq!(window.sidebar.last_files.unwrap().width(), 300.0);
    close_files(&mut window);
    one_frame_in(&mut window, &ctx, area, vec![]);
    assert_eq!(measured(&window), closed);
    assert_eq!(
        window.all_panes().map(|p| p.name).collect::<Vec<_>>(),
        identities
    );
}

/// Click `pos` with the primary button over two frames.
fn click(window: &mut Window, ctx: &egui::Context, area: egui::Rect, pos: egui::Pos2) {
    for pressed in [true, false] {
        one_frame_in(
            window,
            ctx,
            area,
            vec![
                egui::Event::PointerMoved(pos),
                egui::Event::PointerButton {
                    pos,
                    button: egui::PointerButton::Primary,
                    pressed,
                    modifiers: egui::Modifiers::NONE,
                },
            ],
        );
    }
}

fn button_of(window: &Window, tool: Tool) -> egui::Pos2 {
    window
        .sidebar
        .buttons
        .iter()
        .find(|(which, _)| *which == tool)
        .unwrap_or_else(|| panic!("the title bar paints no {tool:?} button"))
        .1
        .center()
}

#[test]
fn title_bar_buttons_open_and_close_each_tool_without_forwarding_the_gesture() {
    let mut window = window_of_tabs([["sleep 30", "sleep 30"]]);
    window.chrome.enable();
    let ctx = egui::Context::default();
    let area = egui::Rect::from_min_size(egui::Pos2::ZERO, egui::vec2(1400.0, 800.0));
    for _ in 0..2 {
        one_frame_in(&mut window, &ctx, area, vec![]);
    }
    let files = button_of(&window, Tool::Files);
    assert!(files.y < 40.0, "the Files button sits in the title bar");
    assert!(
        files.x < 90.0,
        "the Files button sits at the bar's left end, after the logo"
    );
    for expected in [true, false] {
        click(&mut window, &ctx, area, files);
        assert_eq!(files_visible(&window), expected);
        // Placed in the very frame of the click: painting it a frame late made the window flicker.
        assert_eq!(
            window.sidebar.last_files.is_some(),
            expected,
            "Files was placed a frame after its button"
        );
        assert!(window.presses_for_the_program.is_empty());
    }
    for tool in [Tool::Document, Tool::Board] {
        let pos = button_of(&window, tool);
        assert!(pos.x > 1000.0, "{tool:?} sits in the right-hand group");
        for expected in [Some(tool), None] {
            let at = button_of(&window, tool);
            click(&mut window, &ctx, area, at);
            assert_eq!(active(&window), expected, "{tool:?}");
            assert!(window.presses_for_the_program.is_empty());
        }
    }
}

crate::windows_unix_omission!(
    divider_drags_retain_consoles_and_cannot_reach_their_mouse_protocol,
    "unix-only: uses stty raw mode and cat to inspect child PTY input"
);

#[cfg(unix)]
#[test]

fn divider_drags_retain_consoles_and_cannot_reach_their_mouse_protocol() {
    let received = term_core::received::Received::new("sidebar-divider");
    let script = received.script("stty raw -echo && printf '\\033[?1000h\\033[?1006h'");
    let mut window = window_of_tabs([[script.as_str(), "sleep 30"]]);
    received.wait_until_ready(Duration::from_secs(5));
    let ctx = egui::Context::default();
    let area = egui::Rect::from_min_size(egui::Pos2::ZERO, egui::vec2(1600.0, 800.0));
    select(&mut window, Tool::Files);
    crate::viewer::activate(&mut window, false);
    for _ in 0..2 {
        one_frame_in(&mut window, &ctx, area, vec![]);
    }
    let names: Vec<_> = window.all_panes().map(|p| p.name).collect();
    let weights = window.active().divider_fractions();
    let button = |pos, pressed| egui::Event::PointerButton {
        pos,
        button: egui::PointerButton::Primary,
        pressed,
        modifiers: egui::Modifiers::NONE,
    };
    let drag = |window: &mut Window, from: egui::Pos2, to: egui::Pos2| {
        one_frame_in(
            window,
            &ctx,
            area,
            vec![egui::Event::PointerMoved(from), button(from, true)],
        );
        one_frame_in(window, &ctx, area, vec![egui::Event::PointerMoved(to)]);
        one_frame_in(window, &ctx, area, vec![button(to, false)]);
    };
    let files = window.sidebar.last_files.unwrap();
    let from = egui::pos2(files.right() + DIVIDER / 2.0, files.top() + 100.0);
    drag(&mut window, from, from + egui::vec2(90.0, 0.0));
    assert!(window.sidebar.last_files.unwrap().width() > files.width() + 80.0);
    let drawer = window.sidebar.last_dock.unwrap();
    let from = egui::pos2(drawer.center().x, drawer.top() - DIVIDER / 2.0);
    drag(&mut window, from, from - egui::vec2(0.0, 90.0));
    assert!(window.sidebar.last_dock.unwrap().height() > drawer.height() + 80.0);
    assert_eq!(
        window.all_panes().map(|p| p.name).collect::<Vec<_>>(),
        names
    );
    assert_eq!(window.active().divider_fractions(), weights);
    assert!(
        received.so_far().is_empty(),
        "a divider's input reached the program"
    );
}

#[test]
fn host_accounting_counts_a_touched_frame_once_even_below_clock_resolution() {
    let mut sidebar = Sidebar::default();
    sidebar.record(Duration::ZERO);
    sidebar.close_frame();
    sidebar.close_frame();
    let mut report = Vec::new();
    sidebar.publish(&mut report).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(
        String::from_utf8(report).unwrap(),
        "sidebar application: frames=1, total=0ns\n"
    );
}

#[test]
fn sidebar_state_is_transient_and_each_new_window_starts_closed_at_default_sizes() {
    let mut previous = window_of_tabs([["sleep 30", "sleep 30"]]);
    select(&mut previous, Tool::Git);
    select(&mut previous, Tool::Files);
    previous.sidebar.set_files_width(735.0);
    previous.sidebar.set_height(735.0);

    let fresh = window_of_tabs([["sleep 30", "sleep 30"]]);
    assert_eq!(active(&fresh), None);
    assert!(!files_visible(&fresh));
    assert_eq!(fresh.sidebar.files_width, layout::FILES_WIDTH);
    assert_eq!(fresh.sidebar.height, layout::DRAWER_HEIGHT);

    let saved = crate::session::serialize(&crate::session::StoredDocument {
        v: crate::session::VERSION,
        tabs: vec![crate::session::StoredTab {
            root: crate::session::StoredNode::Leaf(crate::session::StoredLeaf {
                panel_width: 80,
                folded: false,
                label: None,
                cwd: None,
                resume: None,
            }),
            label: None,
        }],
    })
    .expect("the ordinary session layout serializes");
    for transient in [
        "sidebar",
        "visibility",
        "files_width",
        "drawer",
        "735",
        "Git",
    ] {
        assert!(!saved.contains(transient), "session serialized {transient}");
    }
}

crate::windows_unix_omission!(
    console_mouse_release_crossing_into_files_or_the_drawer_reaches_its_program,
    "unix-only: uses stty raw mode and cat to inspect child PTY input"
);

#[cfg(unix)]
#[test]

fn console_mouse_release_crossing_into_files_or_the_drawer_reaches_its_program() {
    for (tool, maximized) in [
        (Tool::Files, false),
        (Tool::Document, false),
        (Tool::Git, false),
        (Tool::Document, true),
    ] {
        let received = term_core::received::Received::new("sidebar-console-release");
        let script = received.script("stty raw -echo");
        let mut window = window_of_tabs([[script.as_str(), "sleep 30"]]);
        received.wait_until_ready(Duration::from_secs(5));
        let ctx = egui::Context::default();
        let area = egui::Rect::from_min_size(egui::Pos2::ZERO, egui::vec2(1600.0, 800.0));
        match tool {
            Tool::Document => crate::viewer::activate(&mut window, true),
            Tool::Git => crate::viewer::activate(&mut window, false),
            _ => select(&mut window, tool),
        }
        let name = window.active().panes()[0].name;
        window
            .pane_called(name)
            .unwrap()
            .screen
            .advance(b"\x1b[?1000h\x1b[?1006h");
        for _ in 0..2 {
            one_frame_in(&mut window, &ctx, area, vec![]);
        }
        let from = window
            .pane_called(name)
            .unwrap()
            .painted_console
            .as_ref()
            .unwrap()
            .area
            .center();
        let button = |pos, pressed| egui::Event::PointerButton {
            pos,
            button: egui::PointerButton::Primary,
            pressed,
            modifiers: egui::Modifiers::NONE,
        };
        one_frame_in(
            &mut window,
            &ctx,
            area,
            vec![egui::Event::PointerMoved(from), button(from, true)],
        );
        assert!(
            !window.presses_for_the_program.is_empty(),
            "{tool:?}: program never received press"
        );
        let press = received.wait_for(1, Duration::from_secs(5));
        if maximized {
            toggle_maximize(&mut window);
        }
        assert!(press.ends_with(b"M"), "missing SGR press: {press:?}");
        let metrics = CellMetrics::measure(&ctx, egui::FontId::monospace(window.font_size));
        let geometry = crate::tab_bar::geometry(&window, area, &metrics);
        let to = match tool {
            Tool::Files => geometry.files.unwrap().center(),
            _ => geometry.dock.unwrap().center(),
        };
        one_frame_in(
            &mut window,
            &ctx,
            area,
            vec![egui::Event::PointerMoved(to), button(to, false)],
        );
        assert!(
            window.presses_for_the_program.is_empty(),
            "{tool:?}: release captured (maximized={maximized})"
        );
        let complete = received.wait_for(press.len() + 1, Duration::from_secs(5));
        assert!(
            complete.ends_with(b"m"),
            "{tool:?}: missing SGR release: {complete:?}"
        );
    }
}

#[test]
fn tab_click_repartitions_files_for_the_new_tree_in_the_same_frame() {
    let mut window = window_of_tabs([["sleep 30", "sleep 30"], ["sleep 30", "sleep 30"]]);
    if let Node::Branch { children, .. } = &mut window.tabs[1].root {
        children[0].weight = crate::tiling::Weight::new(0.02).unwrap();
        children[1].weight = crate::tiling::Weight::new(0.98).unwrap();
    } else {
        panic!("two-pane fixture needs a split");
    }
    let ctx = egui::Context::default();
    let area = egui::Rect::from_min_size(egui::Pos2::ZERO, egui::vec2(1600.0, 700.0));
    select(&mut window, Tool::Files);
    for _ in 0..2 {
        one_frame_in(&mut window, &ctx, area, vec![]);
    }
    assert!(window.sidebar.last_files.is_some());
    let metrics = CellMetrics::measure(&ctx, egui::FontId::monospace(window.font_size));
    let strip = crate::tab_bar::geometry(&window, area, &metrics)
        .cards
        .unwrap();
    click(
        &mut window,
        &ctx,
        area,
        crate::tab_bar::tab_rect(strip, 1, 2).center(),
    );
    assert_eq!(window.active, 1);
    assert!(
        window.sidebar.last_files.is_none(),
        "new tree needs the full console width immediately"
    );
    let grids: Vec<_> = window.all_panes().map(|pane| pane.measured).collect();
    one_frame_in(&mut window, &ctx, area, vec![]);
    assert_eq!(
        window
            .all_panes()
            .map(|pane| pane.measured)
            .collect::<Vec<_>>(),
        grids,
        "tab switch published a transient grid"
    );
}

#[test]
fn the_drawer_maximizes_over_the_consoles_restores_its_height_and_keeps_files_beside_it() {
    let mut window = window_of_tabs([["sleep 30", "sleep 30"], ["sleep 30", "sleep 30"]]);
    let ctx = egui::Context::default();
    let area = egui::Rect::from_min_size(egui::Pos2::ZERO, egui::vec2(1500.0, 800.0));
    window.sidebar.set_height(300.0);
    crate::viewer::activate(&mut window, true);
    for _ in 0..2 {
        one_frame_in(&mut window, &ctx, area, vec![]);
    }
    let before: Vec<_> = window.all_panes().map(|p| (p.name, p.measured)).collect();
    let drawer = window.sidebar.last_dock.unwrap();
    let maximize =
        crate::viewer::strip_place(&window, drawer, crate::viewer::StripAction::Maximize)
            .expect("the strip paints maximize");
    click(&mut window, &ctx, area, maximize);
    assert!(
        window.sidebar.maximized,
        "the painted maximize control did not change the presentation"
    );
    let covering = window.sidebar.last_dock.unwrap();
    assert!(covering.height() > 600.0);
    assert_eq!(
        window
            .all_panes()
            .map(|p| (p.name, p.measured))
            .collect::<Vec<_>>(),
        before,
        "a maximized drawer resizes no console"
    );
    let metrics = CellMetrics::measure(&ctx, egui::FontId::monospace(window.font_size));
    let cards = crate::tab_bar::geometry(&window, area, &metrics)
        .cards
        .unwrap();
    click(
        &mut window,
        &ctx,
        area,
        crate::tab_bar::tab_rect(cards, 1, 2).center(),
    );
    assert_eq!(
        window.active, 1,
        "the maximized drawer captured a tab click"
    );
    select(&mut window, Tool::Files);
    one_frame_in(&mut window, &ctx, area, vec![]);
    let files = window
        .sidebar
        .last_files
        .expect("Files opens beside the drawer");
    assert!(files.right() < window.sidebar.last_dock.unwrap().left());
    assert!(window.sidebar.maximized);
    let restore = crate::viewer::strip_place(
        &window,
        window.sidebar.last_dock.unwrap(),
        crate::viewer::StripAction::Maximize,
    )
    .unwrap();
    click(&mut window, &ctx, area, restore);
    assert!(!window.sidebar.maximized);
    assert!(files_visible(&window), "restoring keeps Files");
    assert_eq!(
        window.sidebar.last_dock.unwrap().height() + layout::DRAWER_MARGIN,
        300.0
    );
    let close_at = crate::viewer::strip_place(
        &window,
        window.sidebar.last_dock.unwrap(),
        crate::viewer::StripAction::Close,
    )
    .unwrap();
    click(&mut window, &ctx, area, close_at);
    assert_eq!(active(&window), None, "close shuts the drawer");
    assert!(files_visible(&window), "closing the drawer keeps Files");
}

/// The maximized drawer covers the consoles' headers; a press on it must never reach the Git,
/// maximize or close buttons under it (the Board's lesson, D72).
#[test]
fn a_press_on_the_maximized_drawer_never_reaches_the_console_header_it_covers() {
    let mut window = window_of_tabs([["sleep 30", "sleep 30"]]);
    window.chrome.enable();
    window.headers = window.headers.integrated(true);
    let ctx = egui::Context::default();
    let area = egui::Rect::from_min_size(egui::Pos2::ZERO, egui::vec2(1400.0, 800.0));
    one_frame_in(&mut window, &ctx, area, vec![]);
    let metrics = CellMetrics::measure(&ctx, egui::FontId::monospace(window.font_size));
    let work = crate::tab_bar::geometry(&window, area, &metrics).consoles;
    let rect = window.active().root.place(work, metrics.width()).parts[0];
    let strip = crate::header::set_the_header_aside(rect, &metrics, window.headers)
        .0
        .expect("the console carries a header strip");
    crate::viewer::activate(&mut window, true);
    toggle_maximize(&mut window);
    for _ in 0..2 {
        one_frame_in(&mut window, &ctx, area, vec![]);
    }
    let panes = window.all_panes().count();
    assert!(window.sidebar.last_dock.unwrap().contains(strip.center()));
    // The header's top edge too: a band the drawer leaves uncovered would hand it the press.
    for y in [strip.top() + 1.0, strip.center().y] {
        for offset in (2..=150).step_by(12) {
            let at = egui::pos2(strip.right() - offset as f32, y);
            if crate::viewer::strip_action(&window, window.sidebar.last_dock.unwrap(), at).is_some()
            {
                continue;
            }
            click(&mut window, &ctx, area, at);
            assert_eq!(
                window.all_panes().count(),
                panes,
                "a header close button took the press at {at:?}"
            );
            assert_eq!(
                active(&window),
                Some(Tool::Document),
                "a header button under the drawer took the press at {at:?}"
            );
        }
    }
}

#[test]
fn the_board_covers_files_without_resizing_a_console_and_the_files_button_leaves_it() {
    let mut window = window_of_tabs([["sleep 30", "sleep 30"]]);
    window.chrome.enable();
    let ctx = egui::Context::default();
    let area = egui::Rect::from_min_size(egui::Pos2::ZERO, egui::vec2(1400.0, 800.0));
    select(&mut window, Tool::Files);
    for _ in 0..2 {
        one_frame_in(&mut window, &ctx, area, vec![]);
    }
    assert!(window.sidebar.last_files.is_some());
    let beside_files: Vec<_> = window.all_panes().map(|p| p.measured).collect();
    select(&mut window, Tool::Board);
    one_frame_in(&mut window, &ctx, area, vec![]);
    assert!(
        window.sidebar.last_files.is_none(),
        "the Board covers Files"
    );
    assert_eq!(
        window.all_panes().map(|p| p.measured).collect::<Vec<_>>(),
        beside_files,
        "opening the Board over Files resized a console"
    );
    let files = button_of(&window, Tool::Files);
    click(&mut window, &ctx, area, files);
    assert_eq!(
        active(&window),
        None,
        "the Files button left the Board open"
    );
    assert!(files_visible(&window));
    assert!(window.sidebar.last_files.is_some(), "Files is shown again");
}

#[test]
fn the_drawer_steps_aside_for_stacked_consoles_in_a_short_window() {
    let mut window = window_of_tabs([["sleep 30", "sleep 30"]]);
    let ctx = egui::Context::default();
    let large = egui::Rect::from_min_size(egui::Pos2::ZERO, egui::vec2(1000.0, 1400.0));
    one_frame_in(&mut window, &ctx, large, vec![]);
    let metrics = CellMetrics::measure(&ctx, egui::FontId::monospace(window.font_size));
    for _ in 0..2 {
        window.split_the_focused(crate::tiling::Orientation::Rows, &ctx, large, &metrics);
    }
    assert_eq!(
        window.all_panes().count(),
        4,
        "three rows beside one column"
    );
    crate::viewer::activate(&mut window, false);
    // The grid never reports fewer rows than its floor, so the rectangles tiling hands out are
    // what show a squeeze.
    let floor = crate::tiling::MINIMUM_ROWS * metrics.height();
    let mut stepped_aside = false;
    for height in (200..=1400).step_by(8) {
        let area = egui::Rect::from_min_size(egui::Pos2::ZERO, egui::vec2(1000.0, height as f32));
        for _ in 0..2 {
            one_frame_in(&mut window, &ctx, area, vec![]);
        }
        let consoles = crate::tab_bar::geometry(&window, area, &metrics).consoles;
        let parts = window.active().root.place(consoles, metrics.width()).parts;
        let fits = parts.iter().all(|r| r.height() >= floor);
        if window.sidebar.last_dock.is_some() {
            assert!(fits, "{height}: the drawer squeezed a console to {parts:?}");
        } else if fits {
            stepped_aside = true;
        }
    }
    assert!(
        stepped_aside,
        "no height where the rows fit alone and the drawer stepped aside for them"
    );
    assert!(
        window.sidebar.last_dock.is_some(),
        "a tall window keeps the drawer"
    );
}

/// AC16 of #93: Files and the drawer never take a console under its twenty columns.
#[test]
fn no_console_drops_below_twenty_columns_beside_files_and_the_drawer() {
    let mut window = window_of_tabs([["sleep 30", "sleep 30"]]);
    let ctx = egui::Context::default();
    select(&mut window, Tool::Files);
    crate::viewer::activate(&mut window, false);
    let mut shown = false;
    for width in (300..=1600).step_by(20) {
        let area = egui::Rect::from_min_size(egui::Pos2::ZERO, egui::vec2(width as f32, 800.0));
        for _ in 0..2 {
            one_frame_in(&mut window, &ctx, area, vec![]);
        }
        let metrics = CellMetrics::measure(&ctx, egui::FontId::monospace(window.font_size));
        let consoles = crate::tab_bar::geometry(&window, area, &metrics).consoles;
        let floor = crate::panel::MINIMUM_CONSOLE * metrics.width();
        let beside = window.sidebar.last_files.is_some();
        for part in window.active().root.place(consoles, metrics.width()).parts {
            if beside {
                assert!(
                    part.width() >= floor,
                    "{width}: Files left a console {} points wide, under {floor}",
                    part.width()
                );
            }
        }
        shown |= beside && window.sidebar.last_dock.is_some();
    }
    assert!(shown, "Files and the drawer never opened together");
}

/// One real application pass, returning what it painted.
fn painted(
    window: &mut Window,
    ctx: &egui::Context,
    area: egui::Rect,
    events: Vec<egui::Event>,
) -> Vec<egui::epaint::ClippedShape> {
    let mut frame = eframe::Frame::_new_kittest();
    eframe::App::logic(window, ctx, &mut frame);
    let mut output = ctx.run_ui(
        egui::RawInput {
            screen_rect: Some(area),
            events,
            ..Default::default()
        },
        |ui| eframe::App::ui(window, ui, &mut frame),
    );
    output.textures_delta.clear();
    output.shapes
}

/// The colours the consoles paint inside `consoles`: their fills and their text, each with the
/// opacity the pass painted it at — what a dimmed pass changes.
fn header_inks(shapes: &[egui::epaint::ClippedShape], consoles: egui::Rect) -> Vec<egui::Color32> {
    let mut inks: Vec<_> = shapes
        .iter()
        .filter_map(|s| match &s.shape {
            egui::Shape::Text(text) if consoles.contains(text.pos) => Some(
                text.override_text_color
                    .unwrap_or(text.galley.job.sections[0].format.color)
                    .gamma_multiply(text.opacity_factor),
            ),
            egui::Shape::Rect(rect) if consoles.contains_rect(rect.rect) => Some(rect.fill),
            _ => None,
        })
        .collect();
    inks.sort_by_key(|c| c.to_array());
    inks.dedup();
    inks
}

/// The drawer and a press on the title bar are not modal: the consoles beside them keep their
/// colours. Dimming on them shaded the window beside the drawer and made every title-bar button
/// blink (owner, 17 November 2026).
#[test]
fn neither_the_drawer_nor_a_title_bar_press_dims_the_consoles() {
    let mut window = window_of_tabs([["sleep 30", "sleep 30"]]);
    window.chrome.enable();
    window.headers = window.headers.integrated(true);
    let ctx = egui::Context::default();
    let area = egui::Rect::from_min_size(egui::Pos2::ZERO, egui::vec2(1400.0, 900.0));
    for _ in 0..2 {
        painted(&mut window, &ctx, area, vec![]);
    }
    let metrics = CellMetrics::measure(&ctx, egui::FontId::monospace(window.font_size));
    let consoles = crate::tab_bar::geometry(&window, area, &metrics).consoles;
    let plain = header_inks(&painted(&mut window, &ctx, area, vec![]), consoles);
    assert!(!plain.is_empty(), "no header text was found");
    // Hold a press on the title bar's File button.
    let markdown = button_of(&window, Tool::Document);
    let held = painted(
        &mut window,
        &ctx,
        area,
        vec![
            egui::Event::PointerMoved(markdown),
            egui::Event::PointerButton {
                pos: markdown,
                button: egui::PointerButton::Primary,
                pressed: true,
                modifiers: egui::Modifiers::NONE,
            },
        ],
    );
    assert_eq!(
        header_inks(&held, consoles),
        plain,
        "a held title-bar press dimmed the consoles"
    );
    painted(
        &mut window,
        &ctx,
        area,
        vec![egui::Event::PointerButton {
            pos: markdown,
            button: egui::PointerButton::Primary,
            pressed: false,
            modifiers: egui::Modifiers::NONE,
        }],
    );
    assert_eq!(active(&window), Some(Tool::Document));
    let open = painted(&mut window, &ctx, area, vec![]);
    let beside = window.sidebar.last_consoles;
    assert_eq!(
        header_inks(&open, beside),
        header_inks(&painted(&mut window, &ctx, area, vec![]), beside),
    );
    assert_eq!(
        header_inks(&open, beside),
        plain,
        "the open drawer shaded the consoles beside it"
    );
}
```

### crates/app/src/startup/migration.rs

```
//! One-time namespace migration before preferences or recovery are read (D87).
use std::{io, path::Path};

pub(super) fn prepare(measuring: bool) -> io::Result<()> {
    if !measuring && let Some(directory) = crate::session::directory() {
        migrate(&directory)?;
    }
    Ok(())
}

fn migrate(destination: &Path) -> io::Result<()> {
    // Any existing entry wins, including a symlink or an unreadable entry. `exists()` would
    // mistake a dangling symlink for an absent destination and permit replacing it.
    match std::fs::symlink_metadata(destination) {
        Ok(_) => return Ok(()),
        Err(error) if error.kind() == io::ErrorKind::NotFound => {}
        Err(error) => return Err(error),
    }
    let legacy = destination.with_file_name("agent-terminal");
    match std::fs::symlink_metadata(&legacy) {
        Ok(metadata) if metadata.is_dir() => {}
        Ok(_) => {
            return Err(io::Error::other(
                "ivy: previous configuration is not a directory",
            ));
        }
        Err(error) if error.kind() == io::ErrorKind::NotFound => return Ok(()),
        Err(error) => return Err(error),
    }
    std::fs::rename(legacy, destination).map_err(|error| {
        io::Error::new(
            error.kind(),
            format!("ivy: could not migrate configuration: {error}"),
        )
    })
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    struct Directory(std::path::PathBuf);
    impl Directory {
        fn new() -> Self {
            let path = std::env::temp_dir()
                .join(format!("ivy-migration-{:016x}", getrandom::u64().unwrap()));
            std::fs::create_dir(&path).unwrap();
            Self(path)
        }
        fn seed(&self, name: &str, contents: &[u8]) {
            let path = self.0.join(name);
            std::fs::create_dir_all(path.parent().unwrap()).unwrap();
            std::fs::write(path, contents).unwrap();
        }
    }
    impl Drop for Directory {
        fn drop(&mut self) {
            let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&self.0);
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn preferences_layout_and_other_data_move_without_rewriting() {
        let dir = Directory::new();
        for name in ["config", "layout.json", "recovery.json", "extra/state"] {
            dir.seed(&format!("agent-terminal/{name}"), b"saved data\0\xff");
        }
        let destination = dir.0.join("ivy");
        migrate(&destination).unwrap();
        for name in ["config", "layout.json", "recovery.json", "extra/state"] {
            assert_eq!(
                std::fs::read(destination.join(name)).unwrap(),
                b"saved data\0\xff"
            );
        }
        assert!(!dir.0.join("agent-terminal").exists());
        migrate(&destination).unwrap();
    }

    #[test]
    fn existing_ivy_data_wins_without_merging() {
        let dir = Directory::new();
        dir.seed("agent-terminal/config", b"old");
        dir.seed("agent-terminal/layout.json", b"old layout");
        dir.seed("ivy/config", b"current");
        migrate(&dir.0.join("ivy")).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(std::fs::read(dir.0.join("ivy/config")).unwrap(), b"current");
        assert!(!dir.0.join("ivy/layout.json").exists());
        assert_eq!(
            std::fs::read(dir.0.join("agent-terminal/config")).unwrap(),
            b"old"
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn first_launch_creates_nothing_and_an_invalid_legacy_entry_fails() {
        let dir = Directory::new();
        migrate(&dir.0.join("ivy")).unwrap();
        assert!(!dir.0.join("ivy").exists());
        dir.seed("agent-terminal", b"not a directory");
        assert!(migrate(&dir.0.join("ivy")).is_err());
        assert!(!dir.0.join("ivy").exists());
    }

    crate::windows_unix_omission!(
        dangling_destination_is_not_replaced,
        "unix-only: requires Unix symlink or permission semantics"
    );

    #[cfg(unix)]
    #[test]

    fn dangling_destination_is_not_replaced() {
        let dir = Directory::new();
        dir.seed("agent-terminal/config", b"old");
        std::os::unix::fs::symlink("missing", dir.0.join("ivy")).unwrap();
        migrate(&dir.0.join("ivy")).unwrap();
        assert!(dir.0.join("ivy").is_symlink());
        assert!(dir.0.join("agent-terminal/config").is_file());
    }
}
```

### crates/app/src/tests/agent_signals/native_transport.rs

```
//! Native negotiation, backpressure and client handoff over the real PTY boundary.
use super::*;

#[test]
fn deferred_capability_reply_is_one_value_and_disable_withdraws_it() {
    let mut pane = pane_with("sleep 8", 0);
    configure(&mut pane);
    for _ in 0..100 {
        assert!(!pane.signals.take_capability_reply(true, false));
    }
    assert!(pane.signals.take_capability_reply(false, true));
    assert!(!pane.signals.take_capability_reply(false, true));
    assert!(!pane.signals.take_capability_reply(true, false));
    pane.signals.configure(Default::default(), &mut pane.screen);
    assert!(!pane.signals.take_capability_reply(false, true));
}

crate::windows_unix_omission!(
    automatic_capability_does_not_refuse_human_input_when_writer_is_backpressured,
    "unix-only: requires stty raw mode and sleep to hold a PTY writer backlog"
);

#[cfg(unix)]
#[test]

fn automatic_capability_does_not_refuse_human_input_when_writer_is_backpressured() {
    let mut pane = pane_with("stty raw -echo && printf ready && exec sleep 8", 0);
    let deadline = Instant::now() + Duration::from_secs(2);
    while !pane.screen.to_text().contains("ready") {
        pane.pump(enabled());
        assert!(
            Instant::now() < deadline,
            "raw fixture did not become ready"
        );
        std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(1));
    }
    pane.pty.write(&vec![b'x'; 512 * 1024]).unwrap();
    for _ in 0..100 {
        pane.screen.advance(b"\x1b]7501;?\x07");
        pane.pump(enabled());
    }
    let backlog = pane
        .pty
        .backlog()
        .expect("the raw program never reads its input");
    assert!(
        backlog.bytes > 256 * 1024,
        "fixture did not hold enough input to reject an extra write"
    );
    assert_eq!(
        backlog.refusals, 0,
        "automatic metadata falsely reported a refused user keystroke"
    );
    assert!(!backlog.refused);
}

crate::windows_unix_omission!(
    healthy_native_client_receives_the_exact_capability_response,
    "unix-only: requires stty raw mode and dd to capture PTY input bytes"
);

#[cfg(unix)]
#[test]

fn healthy_native_client_receives_the_exact_capability_response() {
    let path = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!(
        "ivy-capability-{}-{}",
        std::process::id(),
        SystemTime::now()
            .duration_since(std::time::UNIX_EPOCH)
            .unwrap()
            .as_nanos()
    ));
    let response = b"\x1b]7501;?\x07";
    let quoted = path.to_string_lossy().replace('\'', "'\\''");
    let script = format!(
        "stty raw -echo && printf '\\033]7501;?\\007' && dd bs=1 count={} of='{}' 2>/dev/null && exec sleep 8",
        response.len(),
        quoted
    );
    let mut pane = pane_with(&script, 0);
    let deadline = Instant::now() + Duration::from_secs(2);
    loop {
        pane.pump(enabled());
        if std::fs::read(&path).ok().as_deref() == Some(response) {
            break;
        }
        assert!(
            Instant::now() < deadline,
            "client never received the expected capability bytes"
        );
        std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(1));
    }
    assert_eq!(std::fs::read(&path).unwrap(), response);
    std::fs::remove_file(path).unwrap();
}

#[test]
fn a_fresh_native_client_can_replace_a_disconnected_tui_but_a_clear_cannot() {
    let mut pane = pane_with("sleep 8", 0);
    configure(&mut pane);
    let document = publish_flow(&mut pane, "block");
    let now = Instant::now();
    pane.signals
        .receive(81, Some(snapshot(false, true)), now)
        .unwrap();
    pane.signals.closed(81, now);
    native(&mut pane, "state=clear", now);
    assert!(pane.signals.view(now).unwrap().waiting);
    assert!(!pane.signals.view(now).unwrap().backed);
    pane.screen.advance(b"\x1b]7501;?\x07");
    let query = pane.signals.sync_native(&mut pane.screen, now);
    assert!(
        pane.signals.take_capability_reply(query, true),
        "a stale disconnected reporter blocked the next client's negotiation"
    );
    assert_eq!(
        pane.signals.view(now).unwrap().client,
        Client::OpenCode,
        "a probe alone withdrew an unconfirmed question"
    );
    native(&mut pane, "state=working:app=claude-code", now);
    let view = pane.signals.view(now).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(view.client, Client::Claude);
    assert!(view.backed && view.busy && !view.waiting);
    assert_eq!(
        pane.effective_attention(SystemTime::now()).unwrap().class,
        Attention::Block
    );
    still_flow_owned(&mut pane, &document);
}
```

```
#[cfg(test)]
use eframe::egui;

#[cfg(test)]
pub(super) fn split_menu_targets(
    shapes: &[egui::epaint::ClippedShape],
    popup: egui::Rect,
) -> [egui::Rect; 2] {
    fn collect(shape: &egui::Shape, popup: egui::Rect, targets: &mut Vec<egui::Rect>) {
        match shape {
            egui::Shape::Rect(rect)
                if popup.contains_rect(rect.rect)
                    && rect.rect.width() > 40.0
                    && (28.0..=32.0).contains(&rect.rect.height()) =>
            {
                targets.push(rect.rect);
            }
            egui::Shape::Vec(shapes) => {
                for shape in shapes {
                    collect(shape, popup, targets);
                }
            }
            _ => {}
        }
    }
    let mut targets = Vec::new();
    for shape in shapes {
        collect(&shape.shape, popup, &mut targets);
    }
    targets.sort_by(|a, b| {
        a.top()
            .total_cmp(&b.top())
            .then(a.left().total_cmp(&b.left()))
    });
    targets.dedup();
    assert!(
        targets.len() >= 2,
        "the two visible split buttons are missing"
    );
    assert!((targets[0].center().y - targets[1].center().y).abs() < 1.0);
    [targets[0], targets[1]]
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::super::*;

    fn warmed() -> (Window, egui::Context) {
        let mut window = window_with("sleep 30", "sleep 30");
        window.chrome.enable();
        window.headers = window.headers.integrated(true);
        let ctx = egui::Context::default();
        ctx.global_style_mut(|style| style.animation_time = 0.0);
        (window, ctx)
    }

    #[test]
    fn rename_dialogs_stay_centered_after_viewport_resize() {
        for tab_name in [false, true] {
            let (mut window, ctx) = warmed();
            let tab = window.active().name;
            let pane = window.active().focus;
            let title = if tab_name {
                window.pane_view.begin_tab_rename(tab, "Review".into());
                "Rename tab"
            } else {
                window.pane_view.begin_rename(tab, pane, "Review".into());
                "Rename console"
            };
            for size in [egui::vec2(1400.0, 900.0), egui::vec2(950.0, 720.0)] {
                let area = egui::Rect::from_min_size(egui::Pos2::ZERO, size);
                ui_pass(&mut window, &ctx, area, egui::RawInput::default());
                let shapes = ui_pass(&mut window, &ctx, area, egui::RawInput::default());
                let rect = dialog_rect(
                    &shapes,
                    title,
                    crate::theme::Roles::of(&window.theme).raised,
                );
                assert!(
                    rect.center().distance(area.center()) < 2.0,
                    "{title}: {rect:?}"
                );
            }
        }
    }

    fn dialog_rect(
        shapes: &[egui::epaint::ClippedShape],
        title: &str,
        fill: egui::Color32,
    ) -> egui::Rect {
        fn heading(shape: &egui::Shape, title: &str) -> Option<egui::Pos2> {
            match shape {
                egui::Shape::Text(text) if text.galley.text() == title => {
                    Some(text.pos + text.galley.size() / 2.0)
                }
                egui::Shape::Vec(shapes) => shapes.iter().find_map(|shape| heading(shape, title)),
                _ => None,
            }
        }
        let center = shapes
            .iter()
            .find_map(|shape| heading(&shape.shape, title))
            .expect("dialog heading is painted");
        fn frames(
            shape: &egui::Shape,
            center: egui::Pos2,
            fill: egui::Color32,
            result: &mut Vec<egui::Rect>,
        ) {
            match shape {
                egui::Shape::Rect(rect)
                    if rect.fill == fill
                        && rect.rect.contains(center)
                        && rect.rect.height() > 80.0 =>
                {
                    result.push(rect.rect)
                }
                egui::Shape::Vec(shapes) => {
                    for shape in shapes {
                        frames(shape, center, fill, result);
                    }
                }
                _ => {}
            }
        }
        let mut rects = Vec::new();
        for shape in shapes {
            frames(&shape.shape, center, fill, &mut rects);
        }
        rects
            .into_iter()
            .min_by(|a, b| a.area().total_cmp(&b.area()))
            .expect("dialog frame is painted")
    }

    fn mouse(
        at: egui::Pos2,
        button: egui::PointerButton,
        modifiers: egui::Modifiers,
    ) -> Vec<egui::Event> {
        let mut events = vec![egui::Event::PointerMoved(at)];
        events.extend(
            [true, false]
                .into_iter()
                .map(|pressed| egui::Event::PointerButton {
                    pos: at,
                    button,
                    pressed,
                    modifiers,
                }),
        );
        events
    }

    fn header_point(
        window: &Window,
        ctx: &egui::Context,
        area: egui::Rect,
        pane: usize,
    ) -> egui::Pos2 {
        let metrics = CellMetrics::measure(ctx, egui::FontId::monospace(DEFAULT_FONT_SIZE));
        let work = tab_bar::geometry(window, area, &metrics).consoles;
        let rect = window.active().root.place(work, metrics.width()).parts[pane];
        header::set_the_header_aside(rect, &metrics, window.headers)
            .0
            .unwrap()
            .center()
    }

    /// Hovering a row of the console menu fills it and moves nothing: the rows below stay put.
    #[test]
    fn hovering_a_menu_row_does_not_push_the_rows_below() {
        fn text_at(shapes: &[egui::epaint::ClippedShape], label: &str) -> egui::Pos2 {
            shapes
                .iter()
                .find_map(|s| match &s.shape {
                    egui::Shape::Text(t) if t.galley.text() == label => Some(t.pos),
                    _ => None,
                })
                .unwrap_or_else(|| panic!("{label} not painted"))
        }
        let (mut window, ctx) = warmed();
        // With egui's own font the row's height is the minimum and hides the stroke; Geist at a
        // desktop's 1.25 scale is where the hovered row grew two points.
        crate::fonts::install_bundled(&ctx);
        ctx.set_zoom_factor(1.25);
        let area = egui::Rect::from_min_size(egui::Pos2::ZERO, egui::vec2(1280.0, 900.0));
        one_frame_in(&mut window, &ctx, area, vec![]);
        let at = header_point(&window, &ctx, area, 1);
        one_frame_in(
            &mut window,
            &ctx,
            area,
            mouse(at, egui::PointerButton::Secondary, egui::Modifiers::NONE),
        );
        one_frame_in(&mut window, &ctx, area, vec![]);
        let still = ui_pass(&mut window, &ctx, area, egui::RawInput::default());
        let rename = text_at(&still, "Rename") + egui::vec2(4.0, 4.0);
        let below = text_at(&still, "Close console");
        let hover = || egui::RawInput {
            events: vec![egui::Event::PointerMoved(rename)],
            ..Default::default()
        };
        ui_pass(&mut window, &ctx, area, hover());
        let hovered = ui_pass(&mut window, &ctx, area, hover());
        // The hover landed: the row under the pointer is filled with the theme's `hover`.
        let hover_fill = crate::theme::Roles::of(&window.theme).hover;
        assert!(
            hovered.iter().any(|s| matches!(&s.shape,
                egui::Shape::Rect(r) if r.fill == hover_fill && r.rect.contains(rename))),
            "the pointer did not hover Rename"
        );
        assert_eq!(text_at(&hovered, "Close console"), below);
    }

    #[test]
    fn menu_opens_at_pointer_and_stays_inside_viewport() {
        let (mut window, ctx) = warmed();
        let area = egui::Rect::from_min_size(egui::Pos2::ZERO, egui::vec2(1280.0, 900.0));
        one_frame_in(&mut window, &ctx, area, vec![]);
        let at = header_point(&window, &ctx, area, 1);
        one_frame_in(
            &mut window,
            &ctx,
            area,
            mouse(at, egui::PointerButton::Secondary, egui::Modifiers::NONE),
        );
        one_frame_in(&mut window, &ctx, area, vec![]);
        let shapes = ui_pass(&mut window, &ctx, area, egui::RawInput::default());
        let rect = dialog_rect(
            &shapes,
            "Paste",
            crate::theme::Roles::of(&window.theme).raised,
        );
        assert!(area.contains_rect(rect), "menu escapes viewport: {rect:?}");
        assert!(
            rect.distance_to_pos(at) < 3.0,
            "menu detached from click: {rect:?}, {at:?}"
        );
    }

    crate::windows_unix_omission!(
        dismissing_menu_with_escape_or_outside_click_never_reaches_program,
        "unix-only: requires POSIX raw-mode child byte capture to verify clipboard and keyboard delivery"
    );

    #[cfg(unix)]
    #[test]
    fn dismissing_menu_with_escape_or_outside_click_never_reaches_program() {
        for outside in [false, true] {
            let received = term_core::received::Received::new(if outside {
                "menu-outside"
            } else {
                "menu-escape"
            });
            let ctx = egui::Context::default();
            let mut window = window_with(&received.script("stty raw -echo"), "sleep 30");
            window.chrome.enable();
            window.headers = window.headers.integrated(true);
            received.wait_until_ready(Duration::from_secs(5));
            let area = area_of(1280.0);
            one_frame_in(&mut window, &ctx, area, vec![]);
            let at = header_point(&window, &ctx, area, 1);
            one_frame_in(
                &mut window,
                &ctx,
                area,
                mouse(at, egui::PointerButton::Secondary, egui::Modifiers::NONE),
            );
            one_frame_in(&mut window, &ctx, area, vec![]);
            let open = ui_pass(&mut window, &ctx, area, egui::RawInput::default());
            assert!(the_text_queued_up(&open, "Paste"), "menu did not open");
            window.active_mut().panes_mut()[0]
                .screen
                .advance(b"\x1b[?1000h\x1b[?1006h");
            for key in [egui::Key::ArrowDown, egui::Key::Home, egui::Key::PageUp] {
                one_frame_in(
                    &mut window,
                    &ctx,
                    area,
                    vec![key_press(key, egui::Modifiers::NONE)],
                );
            }
            if outside {
                let at = console_point(&window, &ctx, area, 0);
                for pressed in [true, false] {
                    one_frame_in(
                        &mut window,
                        &ctx,
                        area,
                        vec![
                            egui::Event::PointerMoved(at),
                            egui::Event::PointerButton {
                                pos: at,
                                button: egui::PointerButton::Primary,
                                pressed,
                                modifiers: egui::Modifiers::NONE,
                            },
                        ],
                    );
                }
            } else {
                for pressed in [true, false] {
                    one_frame_in(
                        &mut window,
                        &ctx,
                        area,
                        vec![egui::Event::Key {
                            key: egui::Key::Escape,
                            physical_key: None,
                            pressed,
                            repeat: false,
                            modifiers: egui::Modifiers::NONE,
                        }],
                    );
                }
            }
            one_frame_in(&mut window, &ctx, area, vec![]);
            one_frame_in(
                &mut window,
                &ctx,
                area,
                vec![egui::Event::Text("sentinel".into())],
            );
            assert_eq!(received.wait_for(8, Duration::from_secs(5)), b"sentinel");
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn opening_menu_preserves_unrelated_console_colors() {
        let (mut window, ctx) = warmed();
        let area = area_of(1280.0);
        for _ in 0..2 {
            one_frame_in(&mut window, &ctx, area, vec![]);
        }
        window.active_mut().panes_mut()[0]
            .screen
            .advance(b"\x1b[2J\x1b[H\x1b[38;2;17;231;83mQ\x1b[0m");
        let colors = |shapes: &[egui::epaint::ClippedShape]| {
            shapes
                .iter()
                .find_map(|shape| match &shape.shape {
                    egui::Shape::Text(text) if text.galley.text() == "Q" => Some(
                        text.galley
                            .rows
                            .iter()
                            .flat_map(|row| {
                                row.row
                                    .visuals
                                    .mesh
                                    .vertices
                                    .iter()
                                    .map(|vertex| vertex.color)
                            })
                            .collect::<Vec<_>>(),
                    ),
                    _ => None,
                })
                .expect("colored terminal glyph is painted")
        };
        let baseline = colors(&ui_pass(&mut window, &ctx, area, egui::RawInput::default()));
        assert!(!baseline.is_empty());
        assert!(
            baseline
                .iter()
                .all(|color| *color == egui::Color32::from_rgb(17, 231, 83))
        );
        let at = header_point(&window, &ctx, area, 1);
        one_frame_in(
            &mut window,
            &ctx,
            area,
            mouse(at, egui::PointerButton::Secondary, egui::Modifiers::NONE),
        );
        one_frame_in(&mut window, &ctx, area, vec![]);
        let open = ui_pass(&mut window, &ctx, area, egui::RawInput::default());
        assert!(the_text_queued_up(&open, "Paste"));
        assert_eq!(colors(&open), baseline, "opening a menu dimmed the console");
    }

    fn console_point(
        window: &Window,
        ctx: &egui::Context,
        area: egui::Rect,
        index: usize,
    ) -> egui::Pos2 {
        let metrics = CellMetrics::measure(ctx, egui::FontId::monospace(DEFAULT_FONT_SIZE));
        let work = tab_bar::geometry(window, area, &metrics).consoles;
        let rect = pane_view::view_tiling(window, work, &metrics).parts[index];
        window.active().panes()[index]
            .chrome_within(rect, &metrics, SystemTime::now(), window.headers)
            .console()
            .center()
    }

    fn text_center(shapes: &[egui::epaint::ClippedShape], label: &str) -> egui::Pos2 {
        fn find(shape: &egui::Shape, label: &str) -> Option<egui::Pos2> {
            match shape {
                egui::Shape::Text(text) if text.galley.text() == label => {
                    Some(text.pos + text.galley.size() / 2.0)
                }
                egui::Shape::Vec(shapes) => shapes.iter().find_map(|shape| find(shape, label)),
                _ => None,
            }
        }
        shapes
            .iter()
            .find_map(|shape| find(&shape.shape, label))
            .expect("menu item is painted")
    }

    fn menu_at_console(
        window: &mut Window,
        ctx: &egui::Context,
        area: egui::Rect,
        index: usize,
    ) -> Vec<egui::epaint::ClippedShape> {
        let at = console_point(window, ctx, area, index);
        one_frame_in(
            window,
            ctx,
            area,
            mouse(at, egui::PointerButton::Secondary, egui::Modifiers::NONE),
        );
        one_frame_in(window, ctx, area, vec![]);
        ui_pass(window, ctx, area, egui::RawInput::default())
    }

    #[test]
    fn console_menu_copy_preserves_selection_and_copies_it() {
        let (mut window, ctx) = warmed();
        let area = area_of(1280.0);
        one_frame_in(&mut window, &ctx, area, vec![]);
        one_frame_in(&mut window, &ctx, area, vec![]);
        let pane = &mut window.active_mut().panes_mut()[0];
        pane.screen.advance(b"selected text");
        pane.screen.start_selection(Cell {
            row: 0,
            col: 0,
            half: Half::Left,
        });
        pane.screen.extend_selection(Cell {
            row: 0,
            col: 7,
            half: Half::Right,
        });
        let selected = pane.screen.selected_text().expect("fixture selection");
        let shapes = menu_at_console(&mut window, &ctx, area, 0);
        assert_eq!(
            window.active().panes()[0].screen.selected_text().as_deref(),
            Some(selected.as_str())
        );
        let at = text_center(&shapes, "Copy");
        one_frame_in(&mut window, &ctx, area, vec![egui::Event::PointerMoved(at)]);
        one_frame_in(
            &mut window,
            &ctx,
            area,
            vec![egui::Event::PointerButton {
                pos: at,
                button: egui::PointerButton::Primary,
                pressed: true,
                modifiers: egui::Modifiers::NONE,
            }],
        );
        let mut frame = eframe::Frame::_new_kittest();
        let output = ctx.run_ui(
            egui::RawInput {
                screen_rect: Some(area),
                events: vec![egui::Event::PointerButton {
                    pos: at,
                    button: egui::PointerButton::Primary,
                    pressed: false,
                    modifiers: egui::Modifiers::NONE,
                }],
                ..Default::default()
            },
            |ui| eframe::App::ui(&mut window, ui, &mut frame),
        );
        assert!(
        output.platform_output.commands.iter().any(
            |command| matches!(command, egui::OutputCommand::CopyText(text) if text == &selected)
        ),
        "Copy did not publish the selected text"
    );
        output.drop_without_applying_deltas();
        assert_eq!(
            window.active().panes()[0].screen.selected_text().as_deref(),
            Some(selected.as_str())
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn copy_without_selection_does_not_write_clipboard() {
        let (mut window, ctx) = warmed();
        let area = area_of(1280.0);
        one_frame_in(&mut window, &ctx, area, vec![]);
        let shapes = menu_at_console(&mut window, &ctx, area, 0);
        let at = text_center(&shapes, "Copy");
        one_frame_in(&mut window, &ctx, area, vec![egui::Event::PointerMoved(at)]);
        one_frame_in(
            &mut window,
            &ctx,
            area,
            vec![egui::Event::PointerButton {
                pos: at,
                button: egui::PointerButton::Primary,
                pressed: true,
                modifiers: egui::Modifiers::NONE,
            }],
        );
        let mut frame = eframe::Frame::_new_kittest();
        let output = ctx.run_ui(
            egui::RawInput {
                screen_rect: Some(area),
                events: vec![egui::Event::PointerButton {
                    pos: at,
                    button: egui::PointerButton::Primary,
                    pressed: false,
                    modifiers: egui::Modifiers::NONE,
                }],
                ..Default::default()
            },
            |ui| eframe::App::ui(&mut window, ui, &mut frame),
        );
        assert!(
            !output
                .platform_output
                .commands
                .iter()
                .any(|command| matches!(command, egui::OutputCommand::CopyText(_))),
            "disabled Copy wrote the clipboard"
        );
        output.drop_without_applying_deltas();
        let shown = ui_pass(&mut window, &ctx, area, egui::RawInput::default());
        assert!(
            the_text_queued_up(&shown, "Paste"),
            "disabled Copy closed the menu"
        );
    }

    fn choose_menu_item(
        window: &mut Window,
        ctx: &egui::Context,
        area: egui::Rect,
        shapes: &[egui::epaint::ClippedShape],
        label: &str,
    ) {
        let at = text_center(shapes, label);
        one_frame_in(window, ctx, area, vec![egui::Event::PointerMoved(at)]);
        for pressed in [true, false] {
            one_frame_in(
                window,
                ctx,
                area,
                vec![egui::Event::PointerButton {
                    pos: at,
                    button: egui::PointerButton::Primary,
                    pressed,
                    modifiers: egui::Modifiers::NONE,
                }],
            );
        }
    }

    crate::windows_unix_omission!(
        menu_paste_reply_targets_original_console_after_focus_changes,
        "unix-only: requires POSIX raw-mode child byte capture to verify clipboard and keyboard delivery"
    );

    #[cfg(unix)]
    #[test]
    fn menu_paste_reply_targets_original_console_after_focus_changes() {
        let first = term_core::received::Received::new("menu-paste-first");
        let second = term_core::received::Received::new("menu-paste-second");
        let mut window = window_with(
            &first.script("stty raw -echo"),
            &second.script("stty raw -echo"),
        );
        window.chrome.enable();
        window.headers = window.headers.integrated(true);
        let (service, harness) = crate::clipboard::Service::testing();
        window.clipboard = service;
        first.wait_until_ready(Duration::from_secs(5));
        second.wait_until_ready(Duration::from_secs(5));
        let ctx = egui::Context::default();
        let area = area_of(1280.0);
        for _ in 0..2 {
            one_frame_in(&mut window, &ctx, area, vec![]);
        }
        let target = window.active().panes()[1].name;
        let shapes = menu_at_console(&mut window, &ctx, area, 1);
        choose_menu_item(&mut window, &ctx, area, &shapes, "Paste");
        assert_eq!(
            harness.requested(),
            Some((target, crate::clipboard::Source::Clipboard))
        );
        one_frame_in(&mut window, &ctx, area, vec![]);
        let first_point = console_point(&window, &ctx, area, 0);
        one_frame_in(
            &mut window,
            &ctx,
            area,
            mouse(
                first_point,
                egui::PointerButton::Primary,
                egui::Modifiers::NONE,
            ),
        );
        assert_ne!(window.active().focus, target);
        harness.reply(target, Ok("original".into()));
        one_frame_in(&mut window, &ctx, area, vec![]);
        one_frame_in(
            &mut window,
            &ctx,
            area,
            vec![egui::Event::Text("sentinel".into())],
        );
        assert_eq!(second.wait_for(8, Duration::from_secs(5)), b"original");
        assert_eq!(first.wait_for(8, Duration::from_secs(5)), b"sentinel");
    }

    crate::windows_unix_omission!(
        dragging_console_selection_publishes_primary,
        "unix-only: publishes through the Linux primary-selection clipboard"
    );

    #[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
    #[test]
    fn dragging_console_selection_publishes_primary() {
        let (mut window, ctx) = warmed();
        let (service, harness) = crate::clipboard::Service::testing();
        window.clipboard = service;
        let area = area_of(1280.0);
        for _ in 0..2 {
            one_frame_in(&mut window, &ctx, area, vec![]);
        }
        window.active_mut().panes_mut()[0]
            .screen
            .advance(b"selected text");
        let metrics = CellMetrics::measure(&ctx, egui::FontId::monospace(DEFAULT_FONT_SIZE));
        let work = tab_bar::geometry(&window, area, &metrics).consoles;
        let rect = pane_view::view_tiling(&window, work, &metrics).parts[0];
        let console = window.active().panes()[0]
            .chrome_within(rect, &metrics, SystemTime::now(), window.headers)
            .console();
        let start = console.left_top() + egui::vec2(metrics.width() * 0.2, metrics.height() * 0.5);
        let end = start + egui::vec2(metrics.width() * 7.0, 0.0);
        one_frame_in(
            &mut window,
            &ctx,
            area,
            vec![
                egui::Event::PointerMoved(start),
                egui::Event::PointerButton {
                    pos: start,
                    button: egui::PointerButton::Primary,
                    pressed: true,
                    modifiers: egui::Modifiers::NONE,
                },
            ],
        );
        one_frame_in(
            &mut window,
            &ctx,
            area,
            vec![egui::Event::PointerMoved(end)],
        );
        one_frame_in(
            &mut window,
            &ctx,
            area,
            vec![egui::Event::PointerButton {
                pos: end,
                button: egui::PointerButton::Primary,
                pressed: false,
                modifiers: egui::Modifiers::NONE,
            }],
        );
        let selected = window.active().panes()[0]
            .screen
            .selected_text()
            .expect("drag selected text");
        assert!(!selected.is_empty());
        assert_eq!(harness.primary().as_deref(), Some(selected.as_str()));
    }
```
