### crates/app/src/viewer/code_header/tests.rs

```
use super::super::{Kind, State, drawer, view};
use super::*;
use crate::opener::Call;
use std::time::{Duration, Instant};

#[test]
fn a_relative_path_is_counted_from_the_root_and_only_inside_it() {
    let root = Some(Path::new("/r"));
    assert_eq!(
        relative(Path::new("/r/src/a.ts"), root),
        Some(Path::new("src/a.ts"))
    );
    assert_eq!(relative(Path::new("/elsewhere/a.ts"), root), None);
    // A sibling whose name starts like the root is not inside it.
    assert_eq!(relative(Path::new("/r2/a.ts"), root), None);
    assert_eq!(relative(Path::new("/r"), root), None);
    assert_eq!(relative(Path::new("/r/src/a.ts"), None), None);
}

/// A tree of real files under the system's temporary folder, removed when dropped: what the
/// header shows is where links lead, so it needs files that exist.
struct Tree(PathBuf);

impl Tree {
    fn new(files: &[&str]) -> Self {
        let mut nonce = [0u8; 8];
        getrandom::fill(&mut nonce).unwrap();
        let base = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("header-{}", u64::from_ne_bytes(nonce)));
        for file in files {
            let path = base.join(file);
            std::fs::create_dir_all(path.parent().unwrap()).unwrap();
            std::fs::write(&path, "x".repeat(400)).unwrap();
        }
        std::fs::create_dir_all(&base).unwrap();
        Self(std::fs::canonicalize(base).unwrap())
    }

    fn at(&self, path: &str) -> PathBuf {
        self.0.join(path)
    }
}

impl Drop for Tree {
    fn drop(&mut self) {
        let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&self.0);
    }
}

/// The File tab showing `file` as code, with `root` as the Files folder it was opened from.
fn opened(file: &Path, root: Option<&Path>) -> State {
    let mut state = State::default();
    state.open(Kind::Code);
    state.path = file.to_string_lossy().into_owned();
    state.code.header.root = root.map(Path::to_owned);
    state
}

/// What one frame painted: each text with where it sits, and what it copied.
struct Painted {
    texts: Vec<(String, egui::Rect)>,
    copied: Vec<String>,
}

impl Painted {
    fn at(&self, text: &str) -> egui::Pos2 {
        self.texts
            .iter()
            .find(|(t, _)| t == text)
            .unwrap_or_else(|| panic!("{text:?} not painted: {:?}", self.names()))
            .1
            .center()
    }

    /// Where `text` is painted inside `area`, which tells the drawer's from Files' own.
    fn at_in(&self, text: &str, area: egui::Rect) -> egui::Pos2 {
        self.texts
            .iter()
            .map(|(t, rect)| (t, rect.center()))
            .find(|(t, at)| *t == text && area.contains(*at))
            .unwrap_or_else(|| panic!("{text:?} not painted in {area:?}: {:?}", self.names()))
            .1
    }

    fn has(&self, text: &str) -> bool {
        self.texts.iter().any(|(t, _)| t == text)
    }

    fn names(&self) -> Vec<&str> {
        self.texts.iter().map(|(t, _)| t.as_str()).collect()
    }
}

struct Drawer {
    ctx: egui::Context,
    time: f64,
}

impl Drawer {
    fn new() -> Self {
        let ctx = crate::ui::icons::installed_for_tests(1.0);
        ctx.global_style_mut(|style| {
            style.animation_time = 0.0;
            style.interaction.tooltip_delay = 0.0;
            style.interaction.show_tooltips_only_when_still = false;
        });
        Self { ctx, time: 0.0 }
    }

    fn frame(&mut self, state: &mut State, events: Vec<egui::Event>) -> Painted {
        self.time += 0.05;
        let rect = egui::Rect::from_min_size(egui::Pos2::ZERO, egui::vec2(900.0, 600.0));
        let output = self.ctx.run_ui(
            egui::RawInput {
                time: Some(self.time),
                screen_rect: Some(rect),
                events,
                ..Default::default()
            },
            |ui| {
                view::show(
                    state,
                    ui,
                    rect,
                    crate::theme::Theme::default(),
                    false,
                    false,
                    (
                        &drawer::Tabs {
                            changes: None,
                            document: Some("file".into()),
                            markdown: true,
                            side_by_side: false,
                        },
                        &drawer::Title {
                            project: None,
                            folder: "the source console".into(),
                            unavailable: false,
                        },
                    ),
                );
            },
        );
        let mut texts = Vec::new();
        for clipped in &output.shapes {
            if let egui::Shape::Text(text) = &clipped.shape {
                texts.push((
                    text.galley.text().to_owned(),
                    text.galley.rect.translate(text.pos.to_vec2()),
                ));
            }
            if let Some((glyph, _, rect)) = crate::ui::icons::painted_icon(&clipped.shape) {
                texts.push((glyph, rect));
            }
        }
        let copied = output
            .platform_output
            .commands
            .iter()
            .filter_map(|command| match command {
                egui::OutputCommand::CopyText(text) => Some(text.clone()),
                _ => None,
            })
            .collect();
        output.drop_without_applying_deltas();
        Painted { texts, copied }
    }

    /// Frames until the header knows where the file lives.
    fn settled(&mut self, state: &mut State) -> Painted {
        let deadline = Instant::now() + Duration::from_secs(5);
        loop {
            let painted = self.frame(state, vec![]);
            if state.code.header.resolved.is_some() {
                return self.frame(state, vec![]);
            }
            assert!(Instant::now() < deadline, "the header never resolved");
            std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(5));
            drop(painted);
        }
    }

    fn click(&mut self, state: &mut State, at: egui::Pos2) -> Painted {
        let button = |pressed| egui::Event::PointerButton {
            pos: at,
            button: egui::PointerButton::Primary,
            pressed,
            modifiers: egui::Modifiers::NONE,
        };
        let mut copied = Vec::new();
        for events in [
            vec![egui::Event::PointerMoved(at)],
            vec![button(true)],
            vec![button(false)],
        ] {
            copied.extend(self.frame(state, events).copied);
        }
        let mut painted = self.frame(state, vec![]);
        painted.copied.splice(0..0, copied);
        painted
    }

    fn hover(&mut self, state: &mut State, at: egui::Pos2) -> Painted {
        self.frame(state, vec![egui::Event::PointerMoved(at)]);
        self.frame(state, vec![egui::Event::PointerMoved(at)])
    }

    /// Open the menu and click `item` in it; what that frame and the next ones painted.
    fn menu_item(&mut self, state: &mut State, item: &str) -> Painted {
        // A fresh context paints nothing on its first pass.
        self.frame(state, vec![]);
        let painted = self.frame(state, vec![]);
        let more = painted.at(egui_phosphor::regular::CARET_DOWN);
        let menu = self.click(state, more);
        self.click(state, menu.at(item))
    }

    /// Open the menu and rest the pointer on `item`; what that frame painted, its tooltip included.
    fn menu_hover(&mut self, state: &mut State, item: &str) -> Painted {
        // A fresh context paints nothing on its first pass.
        self.frame(state, vec![]);
        let painted = self.frame(state, vec![]);
        let more = painted.at(egui_phosphor::regular::CARET_DOWN);
        let menu = self.click(state, more);
        self.hover(state, menu.at(item))
    }
}

/// Whether a painted text starts with `start`.
fn says(painted: &Painted, start: &str) -> bool {
    painted.texts.iter().any(|(t, _)| t.starts_with(start))
}

fn recorded(state: &State) -> &[Call] {
    match &state.code.header.desktop {
        opener::Desktop::Recorded(calls) => calls,
        opener::Desktop::System => unreachable!("the tests' desktop records"),
    }
}

/// The header reads from the Files folder down to the file, the file with its type's icon.
#[test]
fn the_path_runs_from_the_files_folder_to_the_file() {
    let tree = Tree::new(&["r/src/a.ts"]);
    let mut state = opened(&tree.at("r/src/a.ts"), Some(&tree.at("r")));
    let painted = Drawer::new().settled(&mut state);
    let order: Vec<f32> = ["r", "src", egui_phosphor::regular::FILE_TS, "a.ts"]
        .iter()
        .map(|text| painted.at(text).x)
        .collect();
    assert!(order.windows(2).all(|w| w[0] < w[1]), "{order:?}");
    assert_eq!(painted.texts.iter().filter(|(t, _)| t == "›").count(), 2);
}

/// Copy absolute path and Copy relative path put the literal paths on the clipboard.
#[test]
fn the_copy_items_copy_the_literal_paths() {
    let tree = Tree::new(&["r/src/a.ts"]);
    let file = tree.at("r/src/a.ts");
    let mut state = opened(&file, Some(&tree.at("r")));
    let mut drawer = Drawer::new();
    drawer.settled(&mut state);
    let absolute = drawer.menu_item(&mut state, "Copy absolute path");
    assert_eq!(absolute.copied, [file.to_str().unwrap()]);
    let relative = drawer.menu_item(&mut state, "Copy relative path");
    assert_eq!(relative.copied, ["src/a.ts"]);
}

/// Outside the folder it was opened from, Copy relative path is off and says why; Copy absolute
/// path still copies.
#[test]
fn outside_the_files_folder_there_is_no_relative_path_and_it_says_why() {
    let tree = Tree::new(&["r/src/a.ts", "other/b.ts"]);
    let mut state = opened(&tree.at("r/src/a.ts"), Some(&tree.at("other")));
    let mut drawer = Drawer::new();
    let painted = drawer.settled(&mut state);
    // Outside the root the whole path stands for itself, with no folder to click.
    assert!(
        !painted.has("src") && !painted.has("›"),
        "{:?}",
        painted.names()
    );
    let more = painted.at(egui_phosphor::regular::CARET_DOWN);
    let menu = drawer.click(&mut state, more);
    let item = menu.at("Copy relative path");
    let hovered = drawer.hover(&mut state, item);
    assert!(
        hovered
            .texts
            .iter()
            .any(|(t, _)| t.starts_with("Outside the Files folder it was opened from")),
        "{:?}",
        hovered.names()
    );
    let clicked = drawer.click(&mut state, item);
    assert!(clicked.copied.is_empty(), "{:?}", clicked.copied);
    let absolute = drawer.menu_item(&mut state, "Copy absolute path");
    assert_eq!(absolute.copied, [tree.at("r/src/a.ts").to_str().unwrap()]);
}

crate::windows_unix_omission!(

/// A name with a line break or a direction override is never copied, and Open shows it in the
/// file manager instead of launching it, though its last extension is a document's.
#[cfg(unix)]
#[test]
fn a_name_that_does_not_show_as_itself_is_never_copied_or_launched() {
    for name in ["r/two\nlines.txt", "r/x\u{202e}gpj.txt"] {
        let tree = Tree::new(&[name]);
        let file = tree.at(name);
        let mut state = opened(&file, Some(&tree.at("r")));
        let mut drawer = Drawer::new();
        let painted = drawer.settled(&mut state);
        for item in ["Copy absolute path", "Copy relative path"] {
            let clicked = drawer.menu_item(&mut state, item);
            assert!(
                clicked.copied.is_empty(),
                "{name:?} {item}: {:?}",
                clicked.copied
            );
        }
        let painted = drawer.click(&mut state, painted.at("Open"));
        assert_eq!(recorded(&state), [Call::Reveal(file)], "{name:?}");
        drop(painted);
        // And each refused item says why.
        for item in ["Copy absolute path", "Copy relative path"] {
            let hovered = Drawer::new().menu_hover(&mut state, item);
            assert!(
                hovered.has(UNSAFE),
                "{name:?} {item}: {:?}",
                hovered.names()
            );
        }
    }
}

crate::windows_unix_omission!(
    a_folder_that_does_not_show_as_itself_is_never_copied,
    "unix-only: constructs a non-UTF-8 path with Unix OsStr encoding"
);

/// The path is judged whole: a line break in a folder's name refuses both Copy items, and a
/// plain path beside it copies.
#[cfg(unix)]
#[test]
fn a_folder_that_does_not_show_as_itself_is_never_copied() {
    let tree = Tree::new(&["r/two\nlines/a.txt", "r/plain/a.txt"]);
    for (name, copies) in [("r/two\nlines/a.txt", false), ("r/plain/a.txt", true)] {
        let file = tree.at(name);
        let mut state = opened(&file, Some(&tree.at("r")));
        let mut drawer = Drawer::new();
        drawer.settled(&mut state);
        for item in ["Copy absolute path", "Copy relative path"] {
            let clicked = Drawer::new().menu_item(&mut state, item);
            assert_eq!(clicked.copied.is_empty(), !copies, "{name:?} {item}");
            if !copies {
                let hovered = Drawer::new().menu_hover(&mut state, item);
                assert!(
                    hovered.has(UNSAFE),
                    "{name:?} {item}: {:?}",
                    hovered.names()
                );
            }
        }
    }
}

/// Copy relative path says why it is off: while the file is being found, when it was not opened
/// from a Files folder, and when the file or that folder could not be found.
#[test]
fn copy_relative_path_says_why_it_is_off() {
    let tree = Tree::new(&["r/src/a.ts"]);
    let file = tree.at("r/src/a.ts");
    let reason = |state: &mut State| Drawer::new().menu_hover(state, "Copy relative path");

    // Still being found: the answer is held back, then its sender goes away.
    let mut state = opened(&file, Some(&tree.at("r")));
    Drawer::new().settled(&mut state);
    let (sender, answer) = mpsc::channel();
    state.code.header.resolved = None;
    state.code.header.answer = Some(answer);
    assert!(reason(&mut state).has(FINDING));
    drop(sender);
    let stopped = reason(&mut state);
    assert!(
        says(&stopped, "Could not find this file: the search stopped"),
        "{:?}",
        stopped.names()
    );

    let mut state = opened(&file, None);
    Drawer::new().settled(&mut state);
    assert!(reason(&mut state).has(NO_FOLDER));

    let mut state = opened(&tree.at("r/src/gone.ts"), Some(&tree.at("r")));
    Drawer::new().settled(&mut state);
    let gone = reason(&mut state);
    assert!(
        says(&gone, "Could not find this file: "),
        "{:?}",
        gone.names()
    );
    assert!(!says(&gone, "Outside the Files folder"));

    let mut state = opened(&file, Some(&tree.at("gone")));
    Drawer::new().settled(&mut state);
    let lost = reason(&mut state);
    assert!(
        says(
            &lost,
            "Could not find the Files folder it was opened from: "
        ),
        "{:?}",
        lost.names()
    );

    // And with both found it copies, so the reasons above are not what any state says.
    let mut state = opened(&file, Some(&tree.at("r")));
    Drawer::new().settled(&mut state);
    let copied = Drawer::new().menu_item(&mut state, "Copy relative path");
    assert_eq!(copied.copied, ["src/a.ts"]);
}

/// Open hands a document to its application and shows anything else in the file manager, and its
/// tooltip says which before the click; Open in File Manager always shows.
#[test]
fn open_launches_a_document_reveals_a_script_and_says_which() {
    let tree = Tree::new(&["r/notes.txt", "r/run.sh"]);
    for (name, says, call) in [
        ("r/notes.txt", OPENS, Call::Open as fn(PathBuf) -> Call),
        ("r/run.sh", REVEALS, Call::Reveal),
    ] {
        let file = tree.at(name);
        let mut state = opened(&file, Some(&tree.at("r")));
        let mut drawer = Drawer::new();
        let painted = drawer.settled(&mut state);
        let open = painted.at("Open");
        assert!(drawer.hover(&mut state, open).has(says), "{name}");
        drawer.click(&mut state, open);
        drawer.menu_item(&mut state, "Open in File Manager");
        assert_eq!(
            recorded(&state),
            [call(file.clone()), Call::Reveal(file)],
            "{name}"
        );
    }
    // A document's name on a link to a script: the tooltip and the click follow the script.
    #[cfg(unix)]
    {
        let disguised = tree.at("r/notes-link.txt");
        std::os::unix::fs::symlink(tree.at("r/run.sh"), &disguised).unwrap();
        let mut state = opened(&disguised, Some(&tree.at("r")));
        let mut drawer = Drawer::new();
        let open = drawer.settled(&mut state).at("Open");
        assert!(drawer.hover(&mut state, open).has(REVEALS));
        drawer.click(&mut state, open);
        assert_eq!(recorded(&state), [Call::Reveal(tree.at("r/run.sh"))]);
    }
}

/// Wrap lines in the menu is off until ticked, and ticked it folds the file's long line.
#[test]
fn wrap_lines_in_the_menu_folds_the_long_lines() {
    let tree = Tree::new(&["r/long.txt"]);
    let file = tree.at("r/long.txt");
    let text = format!("{}\n", "word ".repeat(80));
    let mut state = opened(&file, Some(&tree.at("r")));
    state.loaded = Some(super::super::Loaded {
        code: Some(super::super::code::CodeDocument::parse(&file, &text)),
        text,
        markdown: None,
        diff: None,
    });
    let mut drawer = Drawer::new();
    let line = |painted: &Painted| {
        painted
            .texts
            .iter()
            .find(|(t, _)| t.starts_with("word word"))
            .expect("the long line is painted")
            .1
    };
    let before = line(&drawer.settled(&mut state));
    drawer.menu_item(&mut state, "Wrap lines");
    let after = line(&drawer.frame(&mut state, vec![]));
    assert!(before.right() > 900.0, "{before:?}");
    assert!(after.right() <= 900.0 && after.height() > 2.0 * before.height());
}

/// A file that cannot be shown as text still has its header: it can be opened and copied.
#[test]
fn a_binary_file_keeps_its_header() {
    let tree = Tree::new(&["r/photo.png"]);
    let file = tree.at("r/photo.png");
    let mut state = opened(&file, Some(&tree.at("r")));
    state.message = "Binary file".into();
    let mut drawer = Drawer::new();
    let painted = drawer.settled(&mut state);
    assert!(painted.has("Binary file") && painted.has("photo.png"));
    drawer.click(&mut state, painted.at("Open"));
    assert_eq!(recorded(&state), [Call::Open(file)]);
}

mod window;

### crates/app/src/viewer/code_header/tests/window.rs

```
//! The header inside the whole window: what its clicks do to Files and to the focus.
use super::*;

/// A link removed when dropped.
struct Unlink(PathBuf);

impl Drop for Unlink {
    fn drop(&mut self) {
        let _ = std::fs::remove_file(&self.0);
    }
}

/// One pass of the whole window: the texts and icons it painted, and what it copied.
fn window_pass(
    window: &mut crate::Window,
    ctx: &egui::Context,
    time: &mut f64,
    events: Vec<egui::Event>,
) -> Painted {
    *time += 0.05;
    let mut frame = eframe::Frame::_new_kittest();
    let input = egui::RawInput {
        time: Some(*time),
        screen_rect: Some(egui::Rect::from_min_size(
            egui::Pos2::ZERO,
            egui::vec2(1200.0, 700.0),
        )),
        events,
        ..Default::default()
    };
    eframe::App::logic(window, ctx, &mut frame);
    let output = ctx.run_ui(input, |ui| eframe::App::ui(window, ui, &mut frame));
    let mut texts = Vec::new();
    for clipped in &output.shapes {
        if let egui::Shape::Text(text) = &clipped.shape {
            texts.push((
                text.galley.text().to_owned(),
                text.galley.rect.translate(text.pos.to_vec2()),
            ));
        }
        if let Some((glyph, _, rect)) = crate::ui::icons::painted_icon(&clipped.shape) {
            texts.push((glyph, rect));
        }
    }
    let copied = output
        .platform_output
        .commands
        .iter()
        .filter_map(|command| match command {
            egui::OutputCommand::CopyText(text) => Some(text.clone()),
            _ => None,
        })
        .collect();
    output.drop_without_applying_deltas();
    Painted { texts, copied }
}

/// Run window passes until `done` holds, or fail saying `what` never happened.
fn until(
    window: &mut crate::Window,
    ctx: &egui::Context,
    time: &mut f64,
    what: &str,
    mut done: impl FnMut(&crate::Window, &Painted) -> bool,
) -> Painted {
    let deadline = Instant::now() + Duration::from_secs(5);
    loop {
        let painted = window_pass(window, ctx, time, vec![]);
        if done(window, &painted) {
            return painted;
        }
        assert!(Instant::now() < deadline, "{what} never happened");
        std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(10));
    }
}

fn window_click(
    window: &mut crate::Window,
    ctx: &egui::Context,
    time: &mut f64,
    at: egui::Pos2,
) -> Vec<String> {
    let button = |pressed| egui::Event::PointerButton {
        pos: at,
        button: egui::PointerButton::Primary,
        pressed,
        modifiers: egui::Modifiers::NONE,
    };
    let mut copied = Vec::new();
    for events in [
        vec![egui::Event::PointerMoved(at)],
        vec![button(true)],
        vec![button(false)],
    ] {
        copied.extend(window_pass(window, ctx, time, events).copied);
    }
    copied
}
crate::windows_unix_omission!(
    a_folder_of_the_path_shows_in_files_and_the_menu_keeps_its_clicks,
    "unix-only: tests filesystem symlink path resolution in the Linux file tree"
);

/// In the whole window: a folder of the path shows that folder in Files, and the menu, which
/// floats over the drawer, keeps its clicks — the item acts and no console is focused by it.
#[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
#[test]
fn a_folder_of_the_path_shows_in_files_and_the_menu_keeps_its_clicks() {
    let tree = Tree::new(&["r/src/a.ts"]);
    let root = tree.at("r");
    let script = format!("cd '{}' && exec sleep 30", root.display());
    let mut window = crate::tests::window_of_tabs([[script.as_str(), "sleep 30"]]);
    crate::project_view::configure(&mut window, crate::settings::Entry::Normal, None);
    let pane = window.active().panes()[0].name;
    let ctx = crate::ui::icons::installed_for_tests(1.0);
    ctx.global_style_mut(|style| style.animation_time = 0.0);
    let mut time = 0.0;
    crate::sidebar::select(&mut window, crate::sidebar::Tool::Files);
    until(
        &mut window,
        &ctx,
        &mut time,
        "Files reaching the console's folder",
        |w, _| crate::explorer::root_of(w, pane).as_ref() == Some(&root),
    );
    assert!(crate::viewer::open_document_target(
        &mut window,
        pane,
        tree.at("r/src/a.ts"),
        None
    ));
    let painted = until(&mut window, &ctx, &mut time, "the header", |w, p| {
        w.sidebar.last_dock.is_some_and(|dock| {
            p.texts
                .iter()
                .any(|(t, r)| t == "›" && dock.contains(r.center()))
        })
    });
    drop(painted);
    // The drawer at its shortest, where the menu has the least room under the header.
    window.sidebar.set_height(0.0);
    window_pass(&mut window, &ctx, &mut time, vec![]);
    let painted = window_pass(&mut window, &ctx, &mut time, vec![]);
    let dock = window.sidebar.last_dock.expect("the drawer is placed");
    assert!(dock.height() <= 170.0, "the drawer stayed tall: {dock:?}");

    // A menu item over the drawer: it copies, and the focus stays where it was.
    let focused = window.active().focus;
    let more = painted.at_in(egui_phosphor::regular::CARET_DOWN, dock);
    window_click(&mut window, &ctx, &mut time, more);
    let menu = window_pass(&mut window, &ctx, &mut time, vec![]);
    for item in [
        "Open in File Manager",
        "Copy absolute path",
        "Copy relative path",
        "Wrap lines",
    ] {
        let at = menu.at(item);
        assert!(
            dock.contains(at),
            "{item} at {at:?} is outside the drawer {dock:?}"
        );
    }
    let item = menu.at("Copy relative path");
    let copied = window_click(&mut window, &ctx, &mut time, item);
    assert_eq!(copied, ["src/a.ts"]);
    assert_eq!(window.active().focus, focused);

    // With Files closed, a folder of the path opens Files, shows the folder expanded and
    // selected inside the tree it already had, and the file stays in the drawer.
    crate::sidebar::close_files(&mut window);
    window_pass(&mut window, &ctx, &mut time, vec![]);
    let painted = window_pass(&mut window, &ctx, &mut time, vec![]);
    assert!(!crate::sidebar::files_visible(&window));
    let dock = window.sidebar.last_dock.expect("the drawer is placed");
    window_click(&mut window, &ctx, &mut time, painted.at_in("src", dock));
    let painted = until(
        &mut window,
        &ctx,
        &mut time,
        "Files showing the folder",
        |w, _| crate::explorer::selected_of(w, pane) == Some((root.join("src"), true)),
    );
    assert_eq!(
        crate::explorer::root_of(&window, pane),
        Some(root.clone()),
        "the folder's root moved"
    );
    assert!(crate::sidebar::files_visible(&window));
    let dock = window
        .sidebar
        .last_dock
        .expect("the drawer is still placed");
    painted.at_in("a.ts", dock);
}
crate::windows_unix_omission!(
    a_folder_of_the_path_under_a_linked_root_shows_inside_the_tree,
    "unix-only: tests filesystem symlink path resolution in the Linux file tree"
);

/// A Files root reached through a link: the relative path still counts from it, a folder of the
/// path shows inside the tree Files already has, keeping that root, and once Files has moved
/// elsewhere the same folder opens as a new root.
#[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
#[test]
fn a_folder_of_the_path_under_a_linked_root_shows_inside_the_tree() {
    let tree = Tree::new(&["a/src/x.ts", "b/src/x.ts", "a/other/y.ts", "b/other/y.ts"]);
    let link = tree.0.with_file_name(format!(
        "{}-link",
        tree.0.file_name().unwrap().to_string_lossy()
    ));
    std::os::unix::fs::symlink(&tree.0, &link).unwrap();
    let _unlink = Unlink(link.clone());
    let mut window = crate::tests::window_of_tabs([["sleep 30", "sleep 30"]]);
    // The probe hands each console a folder under `link` as given, links unresolved.
    crate::project_view::configure(
        &mut window,
        crate::settings::Entry::Normal,
        Some(link.clone()),
    );
    let pane = window.active().panes()[0].name;
    let root = link.join(if pane % 2 == 0 { "a" } else { "b" });
    let ctx = crate::ui::icons::installed_for_tests(1.0);
    ctx.global_style_mut(|style| style.animation_time = 0.0);
    let mut time = 0.0;
    crate::sidebar::select(&mut window, crate::sidebar::Tool::Files);
    until(
        &mut window,
        &ctx,
        &mut time,
        "Files at the linked root",
        |w, _| crate::explorer::root_of(w, pane).as_ref() == Some(&root),
    );
    assert!(crate::viewer::open_document_target(
        &mut window,
        pane,
        root.join("src/x.ts"),
        None
    ));
    let painted = until(&mut window, &ctx, &mut time, "the header", |w, p| {
        w.sidebar.last_dock.is_some_and(|dock| {
            p.texts
                .iter()
                .any(|(t, r)| t == "›" && dock.contains(r.center()))
        })
    });
    let dock = window.sidebar.last_dock.expect("the drawer is placed");
    assert!(crate::sidebar::files_visible(&window));

    // The relative path counts from the root, links followed on both sides.
    let more = painted.at_in(egui_phosphor::regular::CARET_DOWN, dock);
    window_click(&mut window, &ctx, &mut time, more);
    let menu = window_pass(&mut window, &ctx, &mut time, vec![]);
    let copied = window_click(&mut window, &ctx, &mut time, menu.at("Copy relative path"));
    assert_eq!(copied, ["src/x.ts"]);

    // Inside the root Files shows: revealed in its tree, the root kept as Files spelled it.
    let painted = window_pass(&mut window, &ctx, &mut time, vec![]);
    let src = painted.at_in("src", dock);
    window_click(&mut window, &ctx, &mut time, src);
    until(&mut window, &ctx, &mut time, "Files showing src", |w, _| {
        crate::explorer::selected_of(w, pane) == Some((root.join("src"), true))
    });
    assert_eq!(crate::explorer::root_of(&window, pane), Some(root.clone()));

    // Files moved to another folder: the same click opens src as Files' new root.
    crate::explorer::open_path(&mut window, pane, root.join("other"));
    until(
        &mut window,
        &ctx,
        &mut time,
        "Files at the other folder",
        |w, _| crate::explorer::root_of(w, pane) == Some(root.join("other")),
    );
    let painted = window_pass(&mut window, &ctx, &mut time, vec![]);
    let dock = window
        .sidebar
        .last_dock
        .expect("the drawer is still placed");
    window_click(&mut window, &ctx, &mut time, painted.at_in("src", dock));
    until(
        &mut window,
        &ctx,
        &mut time,
        "Files rooted at src",
        |w, _| crate::explorer::root_of(w, pane) == Some(root.join("src")),
    );
}

### crates/app/src/viewer/delivery.rs

```
//! Deliver a reviewed draft only to the console that originated the viewer.
use eframe::egui;

pub(super) fn deliver(window: &mut crate::Window, ctx: &egui::Context) {
    if window.viewer.pending_send.is_none() {
        return;
    }
    let started = std::time::Instant::now();
    deliver_inner(window, ctx);
    window.viewer.record(started.elapsed());
    window.timed_separately += window.viewer.take_cost();
}

fn deliver_inner(window: &mut crate::Window, ctx: &egui::Context) {
    let Some(text) = window.viewer.pending_send.take() else {
        return;
    };
    let Some(origin) = window.viewer.origin.as_ref() else {
        window.viewer.message =
            "The origin console is unavailable; comments were not inserted.".into();
        return;
    };
    let name = origin.pane;
    let Some(tab_index) = window
        .tabs
        .iter()
        .position(|tab| tab.panes().iter().any(|pane| pane.name == name))
    else {
        window.viewer.message = "The origin console was closed; comments were not inserted.".into();
        return;
    };
    let tab = &mut window.tabs[tab_index];
    let pane = tab.pane_called(name).expect("pane found in this tab");
    if pane.held.is_some() {
        window.viewer.message =
            "The origin console already has a pending paste; comments were not inserted.".into();
        return;
    }
    if pane.pty.backlog().is_some_and(|backlog| backlog.gave_up) {
        window.viewer.message =
            "The origin console cannot receive input; comments were not inserted.".into();
        return;
    }
    let text = text.trim_end_matches(['\r', '\n']);
    let bracketed = pane.screen.bracketed_paste();
    if !bracketed && text.contains(['\r', '\n']) {
        window.viewer.message =
            "The origin console does not support multiline paste; comments were not inserted."
                .into();
        return;
    }
    let mut bytes = Vec::new();
    crate::keyboard::paste_or_hold(text, bracketed, &mut bytes, &mut pane.held);
    if pane.held.is_some() {
        pane.held = None;
        window.viewer.message =
            "Comments exceed the safe paste limit; they were not inserted.".into();
        return;
    }
    if let Err(error) = pane.pty.write(&bytes) {
        window.viewer.message = format!("Comments were not inserted: {error}");
        return;
    }
    pane.screen.scroll_by(term_core::Scroll::ToBottom);
    pane.hand_it_back_to_the_person();
    tab.focus = name;
    window.active = tab_index;
    window.viewer.review.clear();
    window.viewer.message =
        "Comments inserted in the origin console; review and submit them there.".into();
    ctx.request_repaint();
}

#[cfg(all(test, unix))]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::viewer::Source;
    use std::time::Duration;
    use term_core::received::Received;

    #[test]
    fn delivers_once_to_pinned_pane_and_refuses_closed_or_unwrapped_multiline() {
        let first = Received::new("review-first");
        let second = Received::new("review-second");
        let first_script = first.script("stty raw -echo");
        let second_script = second.script("stty raw -echo");
        let mut window = crate::tests::window_of_tabs([
            [&first_script, "sleep 30"],
            [&second_script, "sleep 30"],
        ]);
        first.wait_until_ready(Duration::from_secs(5));
        second.wait_until_ready(Duration::from_secs(5));
        let name = window.tabs[0].panes()[0].name;
        window.tabs[0]
            .pane_called(name)
            .unwrap()
            .screen
            .advance(b"\x1b[?2004h");
        window.active = 1;
        window.viewer.origin = Some(Source {
            pane: name,
            label: "origin".into(),
            cwd: None,
        });
        window.viewer.pending_send = Some("one\ntwo\n".into());
        let ctx = egui::Context::default();
        deliver(&mut window, &ctx);
        assert_eq!(
            first.wait_for(19, Duration::from_secs(5)),
            b"\x1b[200~one\ntwo\x1b[201~"
        );
        assert!(second.so_far().is_empty());
        assert_eq!(window.active, 0);
        deliver(&mut window, &ctx);
        assert_eq!(first.so_far(), b"\x1b[200~one\ntwo\x1b[201~");
        window.tabs[0]
            .pane_called(name)
            .unwrap()
            .screen
            .advance(b"\x1b[?2004l");
        window.viewer.pending_send = Some("another\nline".into());
        deliver(&mut window, &ctx);
        assert!(window.viewer.message.contains("multiline"));
        assert_eq!(first.so_far(), b"\x1b[200~one\ntwo\x1b[201~");
        window.viewer.origin.as_mut().unwrap().pane = usize::MAX;
        window.viewer.pending_send = Some("never".into());
        deliver(&mut window, &ctx);
        assert!(window.viewer.message.contains("closed"));
        assert!(second.so_far().is_empty());
    }
}

#[cfg(all(test, windows))]
mod tests {
    crate::windows_unix_omission!(
        delivers_once_to_pinned_pane_and_refuses_closed_or_unwrapped_multiline,
        "unix-only: uses POSIX shell fixtures and Unix PTY byte-delivery assertions"
    );
}

### crates/panel-control/src/lib.rs

```
#![deny(rustdoc::broken_intra_doc_links)]
//! **A second transport for the panel document, and not a second contract.**
//!
//! Since August 2026 `panel.json` is *a* transport and not *the* file: the contract is the document
//! and the source of truth is the state the application holds per pane (**D17**). This crate is what
//! makes that sentence true instead of aspirational — a producer that would rather speak than write
//! connects to its pane's endpoint and publishes, and the document it sends goes through the same
//! parser, the same ceiling and the same tolerance as the one on disk.
//!
//! **The file stays the default, and nothing here changes that.** Its barrier to entry is zero: a
//! shell script with `printf` publishes a panel. What this adds is for the producer that already has
//! a process running inside the pane and would rather not own a file — an agent, mainly — and for
//! whom the file's one real weakness bites: **the file does not know who writes it**, so two
//! producers are settled by the operating system's scheduling. A connection does know, which is what
//! lets a source *hold* a pane and be the only writer while it does.
//!
//! # What it deliberately does not carry
//!
//! **No pane identifier, on any message.** The pane a call acts on is the endpoint the connection
//! opened. `PANEL_ID` is in every descendant's environment and any sibling of this user reads it out
//! of `/proc/<pid>/environ`, so it is a name and not a credential; a field carrying it would be a
//! field somebody starts trusting, and the check that validates it is what a later refactor drops.
//!
//! OpenCode may also send a strict `agent_signal` v1 snapshot on its pane endpoint.
//! This independent connection does not claim the panel and is not an MCP tool; its
//! connection identity and disconnect are delivered to the application for arbitration.

//! # What this build serves
//!
//! The six verbs of the frozen wire format: `panel_set`, `panel_get`, `panel_patch`, and the three
//! `split_*` that let a connection **open a console of its own, read it and close it**. The three
//! panel verbs need nobody else — their work stays with the pane — and the three split verbs travel
//! to the window, which is the only party with a tree: a console is named by an opaque
//! handle scoped to the connection that opened it, and the person's first byte into it revokes the
//! handle (**D44**). All six are advertised, because all six are answered.

#[cfg(test)]
#[macro_export]
macro_rules! windows_unix_omission {
    ($name:ident, $reason:literal) => {
        #[cfg(windows)]
        #[test]
        #[ignore = $reason]
        fn $name() {}
    };
}

pub mod client;
pub mod codex_relay;
pub mod codex_route;
pub mod diagnostic_peer;
pub mod endpoint;
pub mod listener;
pub mod session;
pub mod wire;

pub use client::{Answered, Producer};
pub use diagnostic_peer::DiagnosticPeer;
pub use listener::{
    Answer, Channel, Connection, Delivery, OverlayAuthority, PublicationInspector,
    PublicationOrigin, PublishHandler, ReadDocument, What,
};
pub use wire::{Activity, AgentSnapshot, MAX_REQUEST, Reply, Request, Side};

### crates/term-core/examples/termios_probe.rs

```
//! Can the terminal ask the kernel which mode the program inside is in?
//!
//! Spawns two children through `portable-pty` and reads the *slave's* line discipline off the
//! **master** file descriptor, which is the premise the diagnosis of #160 rests on. The first child
//! is left in the default line discipline; the second turns it off with `stty raw -echo`. If the
//! premise holds, the two runs print different flags.
//!
//! Run with:
//!   cargo run -p term-core --example termios_probe

// **Unix only, and the docblock above is why.** `MasterPty`'s termios accessors are all
// `#[cfg(unix)]` in `portable-pty`, so on Windows this file would not build — and F2.7 is Windows.
// An example that breaks the workspace build on the platform it explains is worse than no example.
#[cfg(unix)]
use std::io::Read;
#[cfg(unix)]
use std::time::{Duration, Instant};

#[cfg(unix)]
use portable_pty::{CommandBuilder, PtySize, native_pty_system};

#[cfg(unix)]
fn probe(label: &str, script: &str) {
    let pty = native_pty_system();
    let pair = pty
        .openpty(PtySize {
            rows: 24,
            cols: 80,
            pixel_width: 0,
            pixel_height: 0,
        })
        .expect("openpty");

    let mut cmd = CommandBuilder::new("sh");
    cmd.arg("-c");
    cmd.arg(script);
    let mut child = pair.slave.spawn_command(cmd).expect("spawn");
    drop(pair.slave);

    // Synchronise on the child's own output: once "ready" has come back, the script has run past
    // its `stty` and is sitting in `cat`'s read.
    let mut reader = pair.master.try_clone_reader().expect("reader");
    let mut seen = Vec::new();
    let deadline = Instant::now() + Duration::from_secs(5);
    // The word «failed» is the second way out: a script whose `stty` failed prints it instead of
    // `ready`, and without it this loop would sit in a read that `cat` never answers.
    while !seen.ends_with(b"ready") && !seen.ends_with(b"failed") && Instant::now() < deadline {
        let mut buf = [0u8; 64];
        match reader.read(&mut buf) {
            Ok(0) => break,
            Ok(n) => seen.extend_from_slice(&buf[..n]),
            Err(e) => {
                eprintln!("{label}: read error {e}");
                break;
            }
        }
    }

    match pair.master.get_termios() {
        Some(t) => {
            // `local_flags` is a bitflags set: its Debug spells the flags by name, so this probe
            // needs no dependency of its own to say whether ICANON is on.
            println!("{label}: fd={:?}", pair.master.as_raw_fd());
            println!("{label}: local_flags = {:?}", t.local_flags);

            let start = Instant::now();
            let rounds = 10_000;
            for _ in 0..rounds {
                std::hint::black_box(pair.master.get_termios());
            }
            let per = start.elapsed() / rounds;
            println!("{label}: get_termios costs {per:?} per call");
        }
        None => println!("{label}: get_termios() returned None"),
    }

    let _ = child.kill();
    let _ = child.wait();
}

#[cfg(unix)]
fn main() {
    probe("default   ", "printf ready; exec cat");
    probe(
        "stty raw  ",
        "stty raw -echo && printf ready || printf failed; exec cat",
    );
}

#[cfg(windows)]
fn main() {}
```

### packaging/windows/test-launch-contract.ps1

```
# These portable checks exercise the launch oracle without substituting for native execution.
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
$errors = $null
$ast = [System.Management.Automation.Language.Parser]::ParseFile((Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'test-launch.ps1'), [ref]$null, [ref]$errors)
if ($errors.Count) { $errors | Format-List; exit 1 }
$type = $ast.FindAll({ param($n) $n -is [System.Management.Automation.Language.StringConstantExpressionAst] -and $n.Value.StartsWith('using System;') }, $true)
Add-Type -TypeDefinition $type[0].Value
$function = $ast.FindAll({ param($n) $n -is [System.Management.Automation.Language.FunctionDefinitionAst] -and $n.Name -eq 'Saved-Leaves' }, $true)
Invoke-Expression $function[0].Extent.Text
$leaf = '{"leaf":{"panel_width":36,"folded":false}}' | ConvertFrom-Json
if ((Saved-Leaves $leaf) -ne 1) { throw 'valid leaf rejected' }
$branch = @{branch=@{children=@(@{node=$leaf}, @{node=$leaf})}}
if ((Saved-Leaves $branch) -ne 2) { throw 'valid branch rejected' }
foreach ($invalid in @(@{leaf=@{panel_width=0;folded=$false}}, @{leaf=@{panel_width=36}}, @{branch=@{children=@()}})) {
    $failed = $false
    try { Saved-Leaves $invalid } catch { $failed = $true }
    if (-not $failed) { throw 'invalid layout accepted' }
}
'PowerShell syntax, C# native interop compilation, saved leaf and invalid tree fixtures passed.'
```
